import React from 'react';
import { useEditor } from './hooks/useEditor';
import { useDropHandler } from './hooks/useDropHandler';
import { EditorStage } from './components/canvas/EditorStage';
import { LeftPanel } from './components/panels/LeftPanel';
import { RightPanel } from './components/panels/RightPanel';
import { SlidesBar } from './components/panels/SlidesBar';
import { InlineTextEditor } from './InlineTextEditor';
import { TextToolbar } from './TextToolbar';
import { ContextMenu } from './ContextMenu';
import type { ContextAction } from './ContextMenu';
import { FloatingProps } from './FloatingProps';
import './App.css';

export default function App() {
  const editor = useEditor();
  const drop = useDropHandler(editor);

  const contextMenuActions: ContextAction[] = React.useMemo(() => {
    const actions: ContextAction[] = [];

    if (editor.selected && editor.selected.type === 'line') {
      actions.push({
        label: 'вћ• Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РѕРїРѕСЂРЅСѓСЋ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ (РІ РєРѕРЅРµС†)',
        action: () => editor.appendAnchor(editor.selected as any),
      });
      const pts = (editor.selected as any).points || [];
      if (pts.length > 4) {
        actions.push({
          label: 'вћ– РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅСЋСЋ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ',
          action: () => editor.removeLastAnchor(editor.selected as any),
        });
      }
    }

    actions.push(
      { label: 'вњ‚ Р’С‹СЂРµР·Р°С‚СЊ', shortcut: 'Ctrl+X', action: () => editor.cut() },
      { label: 'рџ“‹ РљРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ', shortcut: 'Ctrl+C', action: () => editor.copy() },
      { label: 'рџ“Њ Р’СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ', shortcut: 'Ctrl+V', action: () => editor.paste() },
      { label: 'рџ“‘ Р”СѓР±Р»РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ', shortcut: 'Ctrl+D', action: () => editor.duplicate() },
      { label: 'рџЋЁ РљРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЃС‚РёР»СЊ', action: () => editor.copyStyle() },
      { label: 'рџ–Њ РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ СЃС‚РёР»СЊ', action: () => editor.applyStyle() },
      { label: 'в¬† Р’РїРµСЂС‘Рґ РЅР° СЃР»РѕР№', action: () => editor.moveLayer(1) },
      { label: 'в¬‡ РќР°Р·Р°Рґ РЅР° СЃР»РѕР№', action: () => editor.moveLayer(-1) },
      { label: 'рџ—‘ РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ', shortcut: 'Delete', action: () => editor.removeSelected(), danger: true },
    );

    return actions;
  }, [editor]);

  const editingKind = editor.editing?.kind;
  const isRichEditing = editingKind === 'text' || editingKind === 'footer';

  return (
    <div className="app">
      <LeftPanel editor={editor} />

      <main
        className={`canvas-wrap ${drop.dragOver ? 'drag-over' : ''} ${editor.fillMode ? 'fill-mode' : ''}`}
        ref={editor.wrapRef}
        onDragOver={drop.onDragOver}
        onDragLeave={drop.onDragLeave}
        onDrop={drop.onDrop}
      >
        <div className="zoom-bar">
          {/* вњ… РљРЅРѕРїРєРё Рё РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°СЋС‚ СЃ displayZoom (100% = 100% РІ UI) */}
          <button onClick={() => editor.setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.2, +(z - 0.1).toFixed(2)))}>в€’</button>
          <span>{Math.round(editor.displayZoom * 100)}%</span>
          <button onClick={() => editor.setZoom((z) => Math.min(2, +(z + 0.1).toFixed(2)))}>+</button>
          {editor.copiedStyle && <span className="style-badge">рџЋЁ РЎС‚РёР»СЊ СЃРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ</span>}
          {editor.selectedIds.length > 1 && <span className="style-badge">Р’С‹Р±СЂР°РЅРѕ: {editor.selectedIds.length}</span>}
          {editor.fillMode && (
            <span className="style-badge" style={{ background: editor.fillColor, color: '#000' }}>рџЄЈ Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</span>
          )}
          <span className="hint-drag">РџРµСЂРµС‚Р°С‰РёС‚Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ РёР· Р»РµРІРѕР№ РїР°РЅРµР»Рё РёР»Рё Р°С‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚ РёР· РїР°РЅРµР»Рё РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…</span>
        </div>

        <div className="stage-inner">
  <EditorStage editor={editor} wrapRef={editor.wrapRef as any} />
</div>

        {editor.editing && (
          <InlineTextEditor
  x={editor.editing.x}
  y={editor.editing.y}
  width={editor.editing.width}
  height={editor.editing.height}
  value={editor.editing.value}
  html={editor.editing.html}
  kind={editor.editing.kind}
  richText={editor.selected?.type === 'text' ? editor.selected.richText : undefined}
  fontSize={editor.editing.fontSize}
  fontFamily={editor.editing.fontFamily}
  fontStyle={editor.editing.fontStyle}
  textDecoration={editor.editing.textDecoration}
  fill={editor.editing.fill}
  align={editor.editing.align}
  verticalAlign={editor.editing.verticalAlign}
  padding={editor.editing.padding}
  lineHeight={editor.editing.lineHeight}
  clickX={editor.editing.clickX}
  clickY={editor.editing.clickY}
  onChange={(patch) =>
    editor.setEditing({
      ...editor.editing!,
      ...('value' in patch && patch.value !== undefined ? { value: patch.value } : {}),
      ...('text' in patch && patch.text !== undefined ? { value: patch.text } : {}),
      ...('html' in patch && patch.html !== undefined ? { html: patch.html } : {}),
    })
  }
  onCommit={() => editor.commitEdit()}
  onCancel={() => { editor.setEditing(null); editor.setShowTextToolbar(false); }}
/>
        )}

        {editor.editing && editor.showTextToolbar && !isRichEditing && (
          <TextToolbar
            x={editor.editing.x}
            y={Math.max(0, editor.editing.y - 50)}
            fontSize={editor.editing.fontSize / editor.zoom}
            fontFamily={editor.editing.fontFamily}
            fontStyle={editor.editing.fontStyle}
            textDecoration={editor.editing.textDecoration}
            fill={editor.editing.fill}
            align={editor.editing.align}
            onCommit={() => editor.commitEdit()}
            onChange={editor.onToolbarChange}
          />
        )}

        {editor.floatingPos && editor.selected && !editor.editing && (
          <FloatingProps
            x={editor.floatingPos.x} y={editor.floatingPos.y}
            element={editor.selected}
            onUpdate={(p) => editor.updateWithHistory(editor.selectedId!, p)}
            onClose={() => editor.setFloatingPos(null)}
            onBringForward={() => editor.moveLayer(1)}
            onSendBackward={() => editor.moveLayer(-1)}
            onDelete={editor.removeSelected}
          />
        )}
      </main>

      <RightPanel editor={editor} />

      <SlidesBar editor={editor} />

      {editor.contextMenu && (
        <ContextMenu
          x={editor.contextMenu.x} y={editor.contextMenu.y}
          actions={contextMenuActions}
          onClose={() => editor.setContextMenu(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Stage, Layer, Rect, Transformer, Image as KImage, Line as KLine, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import type { EditorApi } from '../../hooks/useEditor';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../types';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '../../types';
import {
  TextEl, ImageEl, ShapeEl, LineEl, TableEl, FooterEl, PlaceholderEl, FillEl,
} from '../elements';

import { Stamp } from '../../Stamp';

interface Props {
  editor: EditorApi;
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
}

export const EditorStage: React.FC<Props> = ({ editor, wrapRef }) => {
  const { activeSlide, zoom, selectedIds, editing } = editor;
  const fillsLayerRef = useRef<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = editor.trRef.current;
    const layer = editor.layerRef.current;
    if (!tr || !layer) return;

    if (!selectedIds.length) {
      tr.nodes([]);
      tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
      return;
    }

    const nodes = selectedIds
      .map((id) => layer.findOne('#' + id))
      .filter((n): n is any => !!n);

    tr.nodes(nodes);
    tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedIds, activeSlide, editor.trRef, editor.layerRef]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const layer = fillsLayerRef.current;
    if (!layer) return;
    const canvas = layer.getCanvas?.()?._canvas as HTMLCanvasElement | undefined;
    if (canvas) canvas.style.opacity = '0.6';
  }, [activeSlide.id, activeSlide.elements]);

  const handleStageMouseDown = (e: any) => {
    if (editor.fillMode) {
      editor.handleFillClick(() => { /* onEmpty РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё */ });
      return;
    }
    if (e.target !== e.target.getStage()) return;
    if (editing) { editor.commitEdit(); return; }

    editor.clearSelection();
    editor.setFloatingPos(null);
    editor.setShowTextToolbar(false);
    editor.rubberBandStart();
  };

  const handleContextMenu = (e: any, elementId: string | null) => {
    e.evt.preventDefault();
    if (elementId && !selectedIds.includes(elementId)) editor.setSelectedIds([elementId]);
    editor.setContextMenu({ x: e.evt.clientX, y: e.evt.clientY, elementId });
    editor.setFloatingPos(null);
  };

  const commonHandlers = (el: CanvasElement) => ({
    onSelect: (e?: any) => {
      if (editor.fillMode) {
        if (el.type === 'shape' || el.type === 'fill') {
          editor.updateWithHistory(el.id, { fill: editor.fillColor });
        }
        return;
      }
      if (editing && editing.id !== el.id) editor.commitEdit();

      const shift = !!(e?.evt?.shiftKey || e?.evt?.metaKey || e?.evt?.ctrlKey);
      if (shift) {
        const ids = selectedIds.includes(el.id)
          ? selectedIds.filter((id) => id !== el.id)
          : [...selectedIds, el.id];
        editor.setSelectedIds(ids);
      } else if (!selectedIds.includes(el.id)) {
        editor.setSelectedIds([el.id]);
      }

      if (!editing || editing.id !== el.id) editor.setShowTextToolbar(false);

      const skipFloating = el.type === 'text' || el.type === 'footer';
      if (e?.evt && e.evt.button === 0 && !skipFloating) {
        editor.setFloatingPos({ x: e.evt.clientX, y: e.evt.clientY });
      } else if (skipFloating) {
        editor.setFloatingPos(null);
      }
    },
    onUpdate: (p: any) => editor.updateWithHistory(el.id, p),
    onDragStart: (e: any) => editor.handleElDragStart(el, e),
    onDragMove: (e: any) => editor.handleElDragMove(el, e),
    onDragEnd: (e: any) => editor.handleElDragEnd(el, e),
    onContextMenu: (e: any) => handleContextMenu(e, el.id),
    isSelected: selectedIds.includes(el.id),
  });

  return (
    <Stage
      ref={editor.stageRef as any}
      width={activeSlide.width * zoom}
      height={activeSlide.height * zoom}
      scaleX={zoom}
      scaleY={zoom}
      onMouseDown={handleStageMouseDown}
      onMouseMove={editor.rubberBandMove}
      onMouseUp={editor.rubberBandEnd}
      onDblClick={() => {
        if (editor.fillMode && editor.fillPoints.length >= 6) editor.closePolygon();
      }}
      onDblTap={() => {
        if (editor.fillMode && editor.fillPoints.length >= 6) editor.closePolygon();
      }}
      onContextMenu={(e) => handleContextMenu(e, null)}
    >
      <Layer>
        <Rect
          x={0} y={0}
          width={activeSlide.width}
          height={activeSlide.height}
          fill={activeSlide.background}
          listening={false}
          shadowColor="#000" shadowBlur={10} shadowOpacity={0.18} shadowOffsetY={2}
        />
        {activeSlide.backgroundImage && (
          <BgImage src={activeSlide.backgroundImage} w={activeSlide.width} h={activeSlide.height} />
        )}
        {editor.showGrid && <GridLayer w={activeSlide.width} h={activeSlide.height} />}
        {editor.showMargins && (
          <Rect
            x={activeSlide.padding.left}
            y={activeSlide.padding.top}
            width={Math.max(0, activeSlide.width - activeSlide.padding.left - activeSlide.padding.right)}
            height={Math.max(0, activeSlide.height - activeSlide.padding.top - activeSlide.padding.bottom)}
            stroke="#3b82f6" strokeWidth={1} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false}
          />
        )}
        {editor.fillMode && editor.fillPoints.length >= 2 && (
          <KLine points={editor.fillPoints} stroke={editor.fillColor} strokeWidth={2} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />
        )}
        {editor.fillMode && editor.fillPoints.map((_, i) => {
          if (i % 2 !== 0) return null;
          return (
            <Rect key={i} x={editor.fillPoints[i] - 3} y={editor.fillPoints[i + 1] - 3}
              width={6} height={6} fill="#6366f1" cornerRadius={3} listening={false} />
          );
        })}
        {editor.rubberBand && (() => {
          const b = editor.rubberBand!;
          const x = Math.min(b.x1, b.x2);
          const y = Math.min(b.y1, b.y2);
          const w = Math.abs(b.x2 - b.x1);
          const h = Math.abs(b.y2 - b.y1);
          if (w < 2 && h < 2) return null;
          return (
            <Rect x={x} y={y} width={w} height={h}
              fill="rgba(99,102,241,0.12)" stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />
          );
        })()}
      </Layer>

      <Layer ref={fillsLayerRef}>
        {activeSlide.elements.filter((el) => el.type === 'fill').map((el) => (
          <FillEl key={el.id} el={el as any} {...commonHandlers(el)} />
        ))}
      </Layer>

      <Layer ref={editor.layerRef as any}>
        {activeSlide.elements.filter((el) => el.type !== 'fill').map((el) => {
          /* вњ… РЎРєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј РѕСЂРёРіРёРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚ Сѓ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р°, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ СЃРµР№С‡Р°СЃ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ */
          const isEditing = editing?.id === el.id;

          switch (el.type) {
            case 'text':
              return (
                <TextEl
                  key={el.id}
                  el={el}
                  {...commonHandlers(el)}
                  isEditing={isEditing}
                  onDblClick={(e?: any) =>
                    editor.openEditor(el, 'text', e?.evt ? { x: e.evt.clientX, y: e.evt.clientY } : undefined)
                  }
                />
              );
            case 'image':
              return <ImageEl key={el.id} el={el} {...commonHandlers(el)} />;
            case 'shape':
              return (
                <ShapeEl
                  key={el.id}
                  el={el}
                  {...commonHandlers(el)}
                  isEditing={isEditing}
                  onDblClick={(e?: any) =>
                    editor.openEditor(el, 'shapeText', e?.evt ? { x: e.evt.clientX, y: e.evt.clientY } : undefined)
                  }
                />
              );
            case 'line':
              return (
                <LineEl
                  key={el.id} el={el} {...commonHandlers(el)}
                  onDblClick={(sx, sy) => {
                    const localX = sx - el.x;
                    const localY = sy - el.y;
                    const pts = el.points && el.points.length >= 4 ? el.points : [0, el.height / 2, el.width, el.height / 2];
                    let bestIdx = 2;
                    let bestDist = Infinity;
                    for (let i = 0; i + 3 < pts.length; i += 2) {
                      const x1 = pts[i], y1 = pts[i + 1];
                      const x2 = pts[i + 2], y2 = pts[i + 3];
                      const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1;
                      const len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy || 1;
                      const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((localX - x1) * dx + (localY - y1) * dy) / len2));
                      const px = x1 + t * dx, py = y1 + t * dy;
                      const d = Math.hypot(localX - px, localY - py);
                      if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; bestIdx = i + 2; }
                    }
                    const newPts = [...pts.slice(0, bestIdx), localX, localY, ...pts.slice(bestIdx)];
                    editor.updateWithHistory(el.id, { points: newPts });
                  }}
                />
              );
            case 'table':
              return <TableEl key={el.id} el={el} {...commonHandlers(el)} onCellDblClick={(r, c) => editor.openCellEditor(el, r, c)} />;
            case 'footer':
              return (
                <FooterEl
                  key={el.id}
                  el={el}
                  {...commonHandlers(el)}
                  isEditing={isEditing}
                  onDblClick={() => editor.openEditor(el, 'footerText')}
                />
              );
            case 'placeholder':
              return <PlaceholderEl key={el.id} el={el} {...commonHandlers(el)} />;
            case 'stamp':
  return (
    <Stamp
      key={el.id}
      el={el}
      /* вњ… РЎРєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј С‚РµРєСЃС‚ С‚РѕР№ Р·РѕРЅС‹, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂР°СЏ СЃРµР№С‡Р°СЃ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ */
      isEditingZone={editing?.id === el.id ? editing.zone : null}
      onSelect={commonHandlers(el).onSelect}
      onUpdate={commonHandlers(el).onUpdate}
      onDragStart={commonHandlers(el).onDragStart}
      onDragMove={commonHandlers(el).onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={commonHandlers(el).onDragEnd}
      onContextMenu={commonHandlers(el).onContextMenu}
      onDblClick={(zone: 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom', e?: any) => {
        const field =
          zone === 'center' ? 'centerText'
          : zone === 'top' ? 'topText'
          : 'bottomText';
        editor.openEditor(
          el,
          field as any,
          e?.evt ? { x: e.evt.clientX, y: e.evt.clientY } : undefined,
        );
      }}
    />
  );
            default:
              return null;
          }
        })}

        {editor.guides.v.map((x, i) => (
          <Rect key={`gv-${i}`} x={x} y={0} width={1} height={activeSlide.height} fill="#ef4444" opacity={0.7} listening={false} />
        ))}
        {editor.guides.h.map((y, i) => (
          <Rect key={`gh-${i}`} x={0} y={y} width={activeSlide.width} height={1} fill="#ef4444" opacity={0.7} listening={false} />
        ))}

        <Transformer
          ref={editor.trRef as any}
          rotateEnabled
          keepRatio={false}
          borderStroke="#f59e0b"
          anchorStroke="#f59e0b"
          anchorFill="#fbbf24"
          anchorSize={10}
          anchorCornerRadius={2}
          boundBoxFunc={(o, n) => (n.width < 20 || n.height < 20 ? o : n)}
        />
      </Layer>
    </Stage>
  );
};

const BgImage: React.FC<{ src: string; w: number; h: number }> = ({ src, w, h }) => {
  const [img, setImg] = React.useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.src = src;
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
  }, [src]);
  if (!img) return null;
  return <KImage x={0} y={0} width={w} height={h} image={img} listening={false} />;
};

const GridLayer: React.FC<{ w: number; h: number }> = ({ w, h }) => (
  <Shape
    listening={false}
    sceneFunc={(ctx, shape) => {
      const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
      c.beginPath();
      for (let x = 0; x <= w; x += GRID_SIZE) {
        c.moveTo(x, 0);
        c.lineTo(x, h);
      }
      for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += GRID_SIZE) {
        c.moveTo(0, y);
        c.lineTo(w, y);
      }
      c.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7';
      c.lineWidth = 1;
      c.stroke();
      ctx.strokeShape(shape);
    }}
  />
);

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Circle, Line } from 'react-konva';
import type { FillElement } from '../../types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: FillElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<FillElement>) => void;
}

export const FillEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const points = el.points || [];
  if (points.length < 6) return null;

  const updatePoint = (idx: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    const np = [...points];
    np[idx * 2] = x;
    np[idx * 2 + 1] = y;
    onUpdate({ points: np });
  };

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Line
        points={points}
        closed
        fill={el.fill}
        opacity={1}
        stroke={el.stroke || undefined}
        strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth || 0}
        listening={true}
      />

      {isSelected && points.map((_, i) => {
        if (i % 2 !== 0) return null;
        return (
          <Circle
            key={i}
            x={points[i]} y={points[i + 1]}
            radius={5}
            fill="#fbbf24"
            stroke="#f59e0b"
            strokeWidth={1.5}
            draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragMove={(e) => {
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              updatePoint(i / 2, e.target.x(), e.target.y());
            }}
            onDragEnd={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text as KonvaText } from 'react-konva';
import type { FooterElement } from '../../types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: FooterElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<FooterElement>) => void;
  onDblClick: () => void;
  /** вњ… true в†’ СЃРєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј С‚РµРєСЃС‚, РїРѕРєР° РёРґС‘С‚ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ */
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

export const FooterEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
  isEditing,
}) => (
  <Group
    id={el.id}
    name="element"
    x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
    draggable
    onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
    onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
    onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }}
    onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }}
    onDragStart={onDragStart}
    onDragMove={onDragMove}
    onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}

    onTransformEnd={(e) => {
      const n = e.target;
      const sx = n.scaleX();
      n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
      onUpdate({
        x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
        width: Math.max(100, el.width * sx),
      });
    }}

    <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill={el.bgColor} />

        y={el.height / 2 - el.fontSize / 2}
        width={el.width}
        align="center"
        text={el.text}
        fontSize={el.fontSize}
        fill={el.fill}
      />
    )}
  </Group>
);

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { ImageElement } from '../../types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: ImageElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<ImageElement>) => void;
}

export const ImageEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.src = el.src;
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
  }, [el.src]);

  if (!img) return null;

  return (
    <KImage
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y}
      width={el.width} height={el.height}
      image={img}
      rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: el.width * sx, height: el.height * sy,
        });
      }}
    />
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Circle, Line } from 'react-konva';
import type { LineElement } from '../../types';
import { DASH_MAP } from './dashMap';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: LineElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<LineElement>) => void;
  onDblClick?: (stageX: number, stageY: number) => void;
}

export const LineEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const points = el.points && el.points.length >= 4
    ? el.points
    : [0, el.height / 2, el.width, el.height / 2];

  const updatePoint = (idx: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    const np = [...points];
    np[idx * 2] = x;
    np[idx * 2 + 1] = y;
    onUpdate({ points: np });
  };

  const handleDblClick = (e: any) => {
    e.cancelBubble = true;
    if (!onDblClick) return;
    const stage = e.target.getStage();
    const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
    if (!p || !stage) return;
    onDblClick(p.x / stage.scaleX(), p.y / stage.scaleY());
  };

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onDblClick={handleDblClick}
      onDblTap={handleDblClick}
    >
      {/* РќРµРІРёРґРёРјР°СЏ Р¶РёСЂРЅР°СЏ Р»РёРЅРёСЏ РґР»СЏ Р·Р°С…РІР°С‚Р° */}
      <Line points={points} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" strokeWidth={20} lineCap="round" listening={true} />

      {/* Р’РёРґРёРјР°СЏ Р»РёРЅРёСЏ */}
      <Line
        points={points}
        stroke={el.stroke}
        strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth}
        dash={DASH_MAP[el.dash]}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        listening={false}
      />

      {/* РЎС‚СЂРµР»РєР° */}
      {el.isArrow && points.length >= 4 && (() => {
        const n = points.length;
        const x1 = points[n - 4], y1 = points[n - 3];
        const x2 = points[n - 2], y2 = points[n - 1];
        const angle = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
        const len = 14;
        const a1 = angle + Math.PI - 0.35;
        const a2 = angle + Math.PI + 0.35;
        return (
          <Line
            points={[
              x2 + len * Math.cos(a1), y2 + len * Math.sin(a1),
              x2, y2,
              x2 + len * Math.cos(a2), y2 + len * Math.sin(a2),
            ]}
            stroke={el.stroke}
            strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth}
            lineCap="round"
            lineJoin="round"
            listening={false}
          />
        );
      })()}

      {/* РћРїРѕСЂРЅС‹Рµ С‚РѕС‡РєРё */}
      {isSelected && points.map((_, i) => {
        if (i % 2 !== 0) return null;
        const px = points[i], py = points[i + 1];
        return (
          <Circle
            key={i}
            x={px} y={py}
            radius={6}
            fill="#fbbf24"
            stroke="#f59e0b"
            strokeWidth={1.5}
            draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragMove={(e) => {
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              updatePoint(i / 2, e.target.x(), e.target.y());
            }}
            onDragEnd={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { PlaceholderElement } from '../../types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: PlaceholderElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<PlaceholderElement>) => void;
}

export const PlaceholderEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  const textRef = useRef<Konva.Text>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (el.fieldType !== 'image' || !el.sample) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.src = el.sample;
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
    i.onerror = () => setImg(null);
  }, [el.sample, el.fieldType]);

  const pad = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerW = Math.max(10, el.width - pad.left - pad.right);
  const pathLabel = `{{${el.fieldPath}}}`;
  const sampleText = el.sample || 'вЂ”';

  const konvaStyle = [
    (el.fontStyle || '').includes('bold') ? 'bold' : '',
    (el.fontStyle || '').includes('italic') ? 'italic' : '',
  ].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || 'normal';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (el.fieldType === 'image') return;
    if (el.inline) return;
    const node = textRef.current;
    if (!node) return;
    const measured = Math.ceil(node.height() + pad.top + pad.bottom + 24);
    if (Math.abs(measured - el.height) > 2) onUpdate({ height: measured });
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [el.sample, el.fontSize, el.width, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle, el.lineHeight, el.align, el.padding, el.fieldType, el.inline]);

  /* ================= INLINE MODE ================= */
  if (el.inline) {
    if (el.fieldType === 'image') {
      return (
        <Group
          id={el.id}
          name="element"
          x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
          draggable
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
          onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
          onDragStart={onDragStart}
          onDragMove={onDragMove}
          onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
          onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
          onTransformEnd={(e) => {
            const n = e.target;
            const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
            n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
            onUpdate({
              x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
              width: Math.max(20, el.width * sx),
              height: Math.max(20, el.height * sy),
            });
          }}
        >
          <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
          {img
            ? <KImage x={0} y={0} width={el.width} height={el.height} image={img} listening={false} />
            : <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="#f0f0f0" listening={false} />}
          {isSelected && (
            <Rect
              width={el.width} height={el.height}
              stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false}
            />
          )}
        </Group>
      );
    }

    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id}
        name="element"
        x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
        draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart}
        onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
        onTransformEnd={(e) => {
          const n = e.target;
          const sx = n.scaleX();
          n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
          onUpdate({
            x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
            width: Math.max(20, el.width * sx),
          });
        }}
      >
        {el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' && (
          <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill={el.background} />
        )}
        <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
        {isSelected && (
          <Rect
            width={el.width} height={el.height}
            stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false}
          />
        )}
        <Text
          ref={textRef}
          x={pad.left}
          y={pad.top}
          width={innerW}
          text={sampleText}
          fontSize={el.fontSize}
          fontFamily={el.fontFamily || 'Arial'}
          fontStyle={konvaStyle}
          textDecoration={el.textDecoration || ''}
          align={el.align}
          lineHeight={el.lineHeight || 1.2}
          fill={el.fill}
          wrap="word"
          listening={false}
        />
      </Group>
    );
  }

  /* ================= NOT INLINE вЂ” IMAGE ================= */
  if (el.fieldType === 'image') {
    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id}
        name="element"
        x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
        draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart}
        onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
        onTransformEnd={(e) => {
          const n = e.target;
          const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
          n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
          onUpdate({
            x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
            width: Math.max(40, el.width * sx),
            height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
          });
        }}
      >
        <Rect
          width={el.width} height={el.height}
          fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} cornerRadius={6}
        />
        <Rect width={el.width} height={22} fill="#6366f1" cornerRadius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />
        <Text
          x={8} y={6} width={el.width - 16}
          text={`рџ–ј ${pathLabel}`}
          fontSize={11} fontFamily="monospace" fill="#ffffff"
          listening={false}
        />
        {img
          ? (
            <KImage
              x={pad.left} y={22 + pad.top}
              width={innerW}
              height={Math.max(10, el.height - 22 - pad.top - pad.bottom)}
              image={img}
              listening={false}
            />
          )
          : (
            <Text
              x={pad.left} y={(el.height + 22) / 2} width={innerW}
              align="center"
              text="(РєР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєР° РЅРµ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶РµРЅР°)"
              fontSize={12} fill="#94a3b8"
              listening={false}
            />
          )}
      </Group>
    );
  }

  /* ================= NOT INLINE вЂ” TEXT ================= */
  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(60, el.width * sx),
        });
      }}
    >
      <Rect
        width={el.width} height={el.height}
        fill={el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' ? el.background : '#f8fafc'}
        stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} cornerRadius={6}
      />
      <Rect width={el.width} height={20} fill="#6366f1" cornerRadius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />
      <Text
        x={8} y={4} width={el.width - 16}
        text={pathLabel}
        fontSize={11} fontFamily="monospace" fill="#ffffff"
        listening={false}
      />
      <Text
        ref={textRef}
        x={pad.left}
        y={20 + pad.top}
        width={innerW}
        text={sampleText}
        fontSize={el.fontSize}
        fontFamily={el.fontFamily || 'Arial'}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle}
        textDecoration={el.textDecoration || ''}
        align={el.align}

        wrap="word"

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text as KonvaText } from 'react-konva';
import type {
  RichTableData,
  RichTableCell,
  PositionedRun,
} from '../../lib/richText/types';
import {
  computeTableLayout,
  cellPad,
  measureTableHeight,
  lineWidth,
  MAX_NEST_DEPTH,
  type CellBlockItem,
} from './tableLayout';

const CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN = 4;

export { measureTableHeight };

/* ============================================================
   РљРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚
   ============================================================ */

interface Props {
  data: RichTableData;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fallbackFontSize: number;
  fallbackFill: string;
  depth?: number;
}

export const RichTable: React.FC<Props> = ({
  data, x, y, width, fontFamily, fallbackFontSize, fallbackFill, depth = 0,
}) => {
  const layout = useMemo(
    () => computeTableLayout(data, width, fallbackFontSize, depth),
    [data, width, fallbackFontSize, depth],
  );

  if (!data.cells.length) return null;

  const { colOffsets, rowOffsets, cellItems, totalWidth, totalHeight } = layout;

  return (
    <Group x={x} y={y} listening={false}>

      {/* РЇС‡РµР№РєРё: С„РѕРЅ + РіСЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ */}
      {data.cells.map((cell, i) => (
        <CellBackground
          key={`bg-${i}`}
          cell={cell}
          colOffsets={colOffsets}
          rowOffsets={rowOffsets}
        />
      ))}

      {/* РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ СЏС‡РµРµРє */}
      {data.cells.map((cell, i) => (
        <CellContent
          key={`ct-${i}`}
          cell={cell}
          items={cellItems.get(cell)}
          colOffsets={colOffsets}
          rowOffsets={rowOffsets}
          fontFamily={fontFamily}
          fallbackFontSize={fallbackFontSize}
          fallbackFill={fallbackFill}
          depth={depth}
        />
      ))}

      {/* Р’РЅРµС€РЅРёР№ РєРѕРЅС‚СѓСЂ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹ */}
      <Rect
        x={0} y={0}
        width={totalWidth} height={totalHeight}
        stroke="#94a3b8" strokeWidth={1}
        listening={false}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   Р¤РѕРЅ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё
   ============================================================ */

const CellBackground: React.FC<{
  cell: RichTableCell;
  colOffsets: number[];
  rowOffsets: number[];
}> = ({ cell, colOffsets, rowOffsets }) => {
  const cx = colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
  const cy = rowOffsets[cell.rowIndex];
  const cw = colOffsets[Math.min(cell.colIndex + cell.colspan, colOffsets.length - 1)] - cx;
  const ch = rowOffsets[Math.min(cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan, rowOffsets.length - 1)] - cy;

  const fill = cell.bgColor || (cell.isHeader ? '#f1f5f9' : '#ffffff');
  const borderColor = cell.borderColor || '#cbd5e1';
  const borderWidth = cell.borderWidth ?? 1;
  const dash =
    cell.borderStyle === 'dashed' ? [4, 4]
    : cell.borderStyle === 'dotted' ? [2, 3]
    : undefined;

  return (
    <Rect
      x={cx} y={cy}
      width={cw} height={ch}
      fill={fill}
      stroke={borderWidth > 0 ? borderColor : undefined}
      strokeWidth={borderWidth}
      dash={dash}
      listening={false}
    />
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё вЂ” СѓС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ align Рё verticalAlign
   ============================================================ */

interface CellContentProps {
  cell: RichTableCell;
  items: CellBlockItem[] | undefined;
  colOffsets: number[];
  rowOffsets: number[];
  fontFamily: string;
  fallbackFontSize: number;
  fallbackFill: string;
  depth: number;
}

const CellContent: React.FC<CellContentProps> = ({
  cell, items, colOffsets, rowOffsets,
  fontFamily, fallbackFontSize, fallbackFill, depth,
}) => {
  if (!items || !items.length) return null;

  const p = cellPad(cell);
  const cellLeft = colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
  const cellTop = rowOffsets[cell.rowIndex];
  const cellRight = colOffsets[Math.min(cell.colIndex + cell.colspan, colOffsets.length - 1)];
  const cellBottom = rowOffsets[Math.min(cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan, rowOffsets.length - 1)];

  const innerLeft = cellLeft + p.left;
  const innerRight = cellRight - p.right;
  const innerTop = cellTop + p.top;
  const innerBottom = cellBottom - p.bottom;
  const innerWidth = Math.max(10, innerRight - innerLeft);
  const innerHeight = Math.max(10, innerBottom - innerTop);

  /* РЎСѓРјРјР°СЂРЅР°СЏ РІС‹СЃРѕС‚Р° РєРѕРЅС‚РµРЅС‚Р° РґР»СЏ vertical-align */
  const totalContentHeight =
    items.reduce((sum, it) => sum + it.height, 0) +
    Math.max(0, items.length - 1) * CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN;

  /* vertical-align: top / middle / bottom */
  const va = cell.verticalAlign ?? 'top';
  let topY: number;
  if (va === 'middle') {
    topY = innerTop + Math.max(0, (innerHeight - totalContentHeight) / 2);
  } else if (va === 'bottom') {
    topY = innerTop + Math.max(0, innerHeight - totalContentHeight);
  } else {
    topY = innerTop;
  }

  /* text-align: left / center / right / justify */
  const align = cell.align ?? (cell.isHeader ? 'center' : 'left');

  let cursorY = topY;

  return (
    <>
      {items.map((item, bi) => {
        const blockTop = cursorY;
        cursorY += item.height + CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN;

        if (item.kind === 'text') {
          return (
            <TextLayoutRuns
              key={`t-${bi}`}
              lines={item.layout.lines}
              lineHeight={item.layout.lineHeight}
              innerLeft={innerLeft}
              innerWidth={innerWidth}
              y={blockTop}
              align={align}
              bold={cell.isHeader}
              fontFamily={fontFamily}
              fallbackFontSize={fallbackFontSize}
              fallbackFill={fallbackFill}
            />
          );
        }

        if (depth + 1 > MAX_NEST_DEPTH) return null;

        return (
          <RichTable
            key={`nt-${bi}`}
            data={item.data}
            x={innerLeft}
            y={blockTop}
            width={innerWidth}
            fontFamily={fontFamily}
            fallbackFontSize={fallbackFontSize}
            fallbackFill={fallbackFill}
            depth={depth + 1}
          />
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   Р Р°РЅС‹ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РѕРј РІС‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёСЏ
   ============================================================ */

interface TextRunsProps {
  lines: PositionedRun[][];
  lineHeight: number;
  innerLeft: number;
  innerWidth: number;
  y: number;
  align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
  bold: boolean;
  fontFamily: string;
  fallbackFontSize: number;
  fallbackFill: string;
}

const TextLayoutRuns: React.FC<TextRunsProps> = ({
  lines, lineHeight, innerLeft, innerWidth, y, align,
  bold, fontFamily, fallbackFontSize, fallbackFill,
}) => (
  <>
    {lines.map((line, ri) => {
      /* РЎРґРІРёРі СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё РїРѕ РіРѕСЂРёР·РѕРЅС‚Р°Р»Рё вЂ” РІ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё РѕС‚ align. */
      const lw = lineWidth(line);
      let lineOffsetX = 0;
      if (align === 'center') {
        lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, (innerWidth - lw) / 2);
      } else if (align === 'right') {
        lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, innerWidth - lw);
      }
      // justify вЂ” СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂРёРј РєР°Рє left (РЅРµ СЂР°СЃС‚СЏРіРёРІР°РµРј РїСЂРѕР±РµР»С‹)

      return line.map((run, rj) => {
        const fs = run.actualFontSize || fallbackFontSize;
        const isBold = bold || run.bold;
        const fontStyle =
          isBold && run.italic ? 'italic bold'
          : isBold ? 'bold'
          : run.italic ? 'italic'
          : 'normal';
        const textDecoration =
          run.underline && run.strike ? 'underline line-through'
          : run.underline ? 'underline'
          : run.strike ? 'line-through'
          : '';

        /* Р’РµСЂС‚РёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃРґРІРёРі вЂ” С†РµРЅС‚СЂРёСЂСѓРµРј С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё. */
        const baselineShift = Math.max(0, (lineHeight - fs) / 2);

        return (
          <KonvaText
            key={`${ri}-${rj}`}
            x={innerLeft + lineOffsetX + run.x}
            y={y + ri * lineHeight + baselineShift}
            text={run.text}
            fontSize={fs}
            fontFamily={run.fontFamily || fontFamily}
            fontStyle={fontStyle}
            textDecoration={textDecoration}
            fill={run.color || fallbackFill}
            listening={false}
          />
        );
      });
    })}
  </>
);

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Circle, Line, Text, Star } from 'react-konva';
import type { ShapeElement } from '../../types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: ShapeElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<ShapeElement>) => void;
  onDblClick: (e?: any) => void;
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

export const ShapeEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
  isEditing,
}) => {
  const w = el.width, h = el.height;
  const common = {
    stroke: el.stroke,
    strokeWidth: el.strokeWidth,
    fill: el.fill === 'transparent' ? undefined : el.fill,
  };
  const hasText = !!el.text && !isEditing;
  const fontSize = el.fontSize || 16;

  const textPad = el.textPadding || { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 };
  const textAlign = el.textAlign || 'center';
  const textVerticalAlign = el.textVerticalAlign || 'middle';
  const textFontFamily = el.textFontFamily || 'Arial';
  const textLineHeight = el.textLineHeight || 1.3;
  const textFontStyle = el.textFontStyle || 'normal';
  const textDecoration = el.textDecoration || '';

  const innerW = Math.max(1, w - textPad.left - textPad.right);
  const innerH = Math.max(1, h - textPad.top - textPad.bottom);

  let tx = textPad.left, ty = textPad.top, tw = innerW, th = innerH;
  if (el.shape === 'circle') {
    const d = Math.min(w, h);
    tw = d * 0.7; th = d * 0.7;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = (h - th) / 2;
  } else if (el.shape === 'triangle') {
    tw = w * 0.55; th = h * 0.45;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = h * 0.5;
  } else if (el.shape === 'star') {
    tw = w * 0.5; th = h * 0.5;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = (h - th) / 2;
  }

  /* вњ… РљРѕРјРїРµРЅСЃР°С†РёСЏ leading вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ top-align, РєР°Рє Рё РІ TextEl */
  const lineBoxOffset = textVerticalAlign === 'top'
    ? ((textLineHeight - 1) * fontSize) / 2
    : 0;

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(10, el.width * sx),
          height: Math.max(10, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      {el.shape === 'rect' && <Rect width={w} height={h} cornerRadius={el.radius || 0} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'roundRect' && <Rect width={w} height={h} cornerRadius={el.radius ?? 16} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'circle' && <Circle x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radius={Math.min(w, h) / 2} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'triangle' && <Line points={[w / 2, 0, w, h, 0, h, w / 2, 0]} closed {...common} lineJoin="round" />}
      {el.shape === 'star' && (
        <Star x={w / 2} y={h / 2} numPoints={5}
          innerRadius={Math.min(w, h) / 4}
          outerRadius={Math.min(w, h) / 2}
          {...common} />
      )}
      {el.shape === 'arrow' && (
        <Line
          points={[0, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2 - 15, w, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2 + 15, w - 20, h / 2]}
          {...common}
          lineJoin="round"
        />
      )}
      {hasText && (
        <Text
          x={tx} y={ty + lineBoxOffset} width={tw} height={th}
          text={el.text}
          fontSize={fontSize}
          fontFamily={textFontFamily}
          fontStyle={textFontStyle}
          textDecoration={textDecoration}
          fill={el.textFill}
          align={textAlign}
          verticalAlign={textVerticalAlign}
          lineHeight={textLineHeight}
          wrap="word"
          listening={false}
        />
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text } from 'react-konva';
import type { TableElement } from '../../types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: TableElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<TableElement>) => void;
  onCellDblClick?: (r: number, c: number) => void;
}

export const TableEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onCellDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
}) => {
  const cellW = el.width / el.cols;
  const cellH = el.height / el.rows;
  const cells: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  const texts: React.ReactNode[] = [];

  for (let r = 0; r < el.rows; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < el.cols; c++) {
      const bg = r === 0 ? el.headerBg : '#fff';
      cells.push(
        <Rect
          key={`r-${r}-${c}`}
          x={c * cellW} y={r * cellH}
          width={cellW} height={cellH}
          fill={bg}
          stroke={el.borderColor}
          strokeWidth={1}
          onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onCellDblClick?.(r, c); }}
          onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onCellDblClick?.(r, c); }}
        />
      );
      const cellText = el.data[r]?.[c] ?? '';
      const isPh = /\{\{[^}]+\}\}/.test(cellText);
      texts.push(
        <Text
          key={`t-${r}-${c}`}
          x={c * cellW + 6} y={r * cellH + 6}
          width={cellW - 12}
          text={cellText}
          fontSize={el.fontSize}
          fill={isPh ? '#2563eb' : '#111'}
          fontStyle={isPh ? 'italic' : 'normal'}
          listening={false}
        />
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(100, el.width * sx),
          height: Math.max(60, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      {cells}
      {texts}
    </Group>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   Р Р°СЃС‡С‘С‚ РіРµРѕРјРµС‚СЂРёРё С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹. РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РјР°С‚РµРјР°С‚РёРєР° вЂ” РЅРёРєР°РєРѕРіРѕ React.
   ============================================================ */

import type {
  RichTableData,
  RichTableCell,
  RichTableCellPadding,
  RichBlock,
} from '../../lib/richText/types';
import { layoutRuns } from '../../lib/richText/layout';

export const CELL_PADDING_DEFAULT: RichTableCellPadding =
  { top: 6, right: 8, bottom: 6, left: 8 };

const MIN_ROW_HEIGHT = 12;
const MIN_CELL_TEXT_LINE = 16;
const CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN = 4;
export const MAX_NEST_DEPTH = 4;

export type CellBlockItem =
  | { kind: 'text'; layout: ReturnType<typeof layoutRuns>; height: number }
  | { kind: 'table'; data: RichTableData; height: number };

export interface TableLayout {
  colOffsets: number[];
  rowOffsets: number[];
  cellItems: Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>;
  totalHeight: number;
  totalWidth: number;
}

export function cellPad(cell: RichTableCell): RichTableCellPadding {
  return cell.padding ?? CELL_PADDING_DEFAULT;
}

/** РЎСѓРјРјР° С€РёСЂРёРЅ СЂР°РЅРѕРІ РІ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРµ вЂ” РЅСѓР¶РЅР° РґР»СЏ center/right РІС‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёСЏ. */
export function lineWidth(line: { width: number }[]): number {
  let w = 0;
  for (const r of line) w += r.width;
  return w;
}

export function computeTableLayout(
  data: RichTableData,
  width: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  depth: number,
): TableLayout {
  const { cells, rowCount, colCount } = data;

  const colPixelWidths = resolveColPixelWidths(data, width, colCount);
  const colOffsets = buildOffsets(colPixelWidths);

  const { cellItems, cellNaturalHeights } = measureCells(
    cells, colOffsets, colCount, fallbackFontSize,
  );

  const rowHeights = resolveRowHeights(cells, rowCount, cellNaturalHeights);
  const rowOffsets = buildOffsets(rowHeights);

  return {
    colOffsets,
    rowOffsets,
    cellItems,
    totalHeight: rowOffsets[rowOffsets.length - 1] ?? 0,
    totalWidth: colOffsets[colOffsets.length - 1] ?? 0,
  };
}

export function measureTableHeight(
  data: RichTableData,
  width: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  depth = 0,
): number {
  if (!data.cells.length) return 0;
  return computeTableLayout(data, width, fallbackFontSize, depth).totalHeight;
}

/* ============================================================
   Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРµРµ
   ============================================================ */

function resolveColPixelWidths(
  data: RichTableData,
  totalWidth: number,
  colCount: number,
): number[] {
  const { colWidths } = data;
  if (colWidths && colWidths.length === colCount) {
    const sum = colWidths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    if (sum > 0) return colWidths.map((w) => (w / sum) * totalWidth);
  }
  return Array(colCount).fill(totalWidth / colCount);
}

function buildOffsets(widths: number[]): number[] {
  const offsets = [0];
  for (let i = 0; i < widths.length; i++) {
    offsets.push(offsets[i] + widths[i]);
  }
  return offsets;
}

function measureCells(
  cells: RichTableCell[],
  colOffsets: number[],
  colCount: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
): {
  cellItems: Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>;
  cellNaturalHeights: Map<RichTableCell, number>;
} {
  const cellItems = new Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>();
  const cellNaturalHeights = new Map<RichTableCell, number>();

  for (const cell of cells) {
    const p = cellPad(cell);
    const cw = colOffsets[Math.min(cell.colIndex + cell.colspan, colCount)]
             - colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
    const innerW = Math.max(10, cw - p.left - p.right);

    const items = measureCellContent(cell, innerW, fallbackFontSize);
    const contentHeight = items.reduce((sum, it) => sum + it.height, 0)
      + Math.max(0, items.length - 1) * CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN;

    const minContentHeight = Math.min(fallbackFontSize, MIN_CELL_TEXT_LINE);
    const minCellHeight = minContentHeight * 1.3 + p.top + p.bottom;

    cellItems.set(cell, items);
    cellNaturalHeights.set(
      cell,
      Math.max(minCellHeight, contentHeight + p.top + p.bottom),
    );
  }

  return { cellItems, cellNaturalHeights };
}

function measureCellContent(
  cell: RichTableCell,
  innerWidth: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
): CellBlockItem[] {
  const items: CellBlockItem[] = [];
  for (const block of cell.blocks as RichBlock[]) {
    if (block.kind === 'text') {
      const layout = layoutRuns(block.runs, innerWidth, 1.3);
      items.push({ kind: 'text', layout, height: layout.totalHeight });
    } else {
      const h = measureTableHeight(block.data, innerWidth, fallbackFontSize, 1);
      items.push({ kind: 'table', data: block.data, height: h });
    }
  }
  return items;
}

function resolveRowHeights(
  cells: RichTableCell[],
  rowCount: number,
  cellNaturalHeights: Map<RichTableCell, number>,
): number[] {
  const rowHeights: number[] = new Array(rowCount).fill(MIN_ROW_HEIGHT);

  for (const cell of cells) {
    if (cell.rowspan !== 1) continue;
    const h = cellNaturalHeights.get(cell) ?? 0;
    if (h > rowHeights[cell.rowIndex]) rowHeights[cell.rowIndex] = h;
  }

  for (let iter = 0; iter < 10; iter++) {
    let changed = false;
    for (const cell of cells) {
      if (cell.rowspan <= 1) continue;
      const need = cellNaturalHeights.get(cell) ?? 0;
      let have = 0;
      for (let r = cell.rowIndex; r < cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan; r++) {
        have += rowHeights[r] ?? 0;
      }
      if (have + 0.5 < need) {
        const delta = (need - have) / cell.rowspan;
        for (let r = cell.rowIndex; r < cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan; r++) {
          rowHeights[r] = (rowHeights[r] ?? 0) + delta;
        }
        changed = true;
      }
    }
    if (!changed) break;
  }

  return rowHeights;
}

FILE: components\elements\TextEl.tsx
SIZE: 10569 bytes

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text as KonvaText } from 'react-konva';
import type { TextElement } from '../../types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import {
  parseHtmlToBlocks,
  layoutRuns,
  plainToHtml,
  type RichTableData,
  type PositionedRun,
} from '../../lib/richText';
import { RichTable, measureTableHeight } from './RichTable';
import { TEXT_PLACEHOLDER } from '../../lib/constants';

const BLOCK_MARGIN = 0;

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: TextElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<TextElement>) => void;
  onDblClick: (e?: any) => void;
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

type PreparedBlock =
  | { kind: 'text'; layout: ReturnType<typeof layoutRuns>; height: number }
  | { kind: 'table'; data: RichTableData; height: number };

export const TextEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
  isEditing,
}) => {
  const padding = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, el.width - padding.left - padding.right);
  const innerHeight = Math.max(1, el.height - padding.top - padding.bottom);

  const isPlaceholder = useMemo(
    () => !el.html && (el.text === TEXT_PLACEHOLDER || !el.text?.trim()),
    [el.html, el.text],
  );

  const blocks = useMemo(() => {
    const baseFontStyle = el.fontStyle || 'normal';
    const resolvedFontStyle = isPlaceholder ? `${baseFontStyle} italic` : baseFontStyle;
    const resolvedFill = isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : el.fill;

    const sourceHtml = el.html && el.html.trim() ? el.html : plainToHtml(el.text || '');
    return parseHtmlToBlocks(sourceHtml, {
      fontSize: el.fontSize,
      fontFamily: el.fontFamily,
      fontStyle: resolvedFontStyle,
      textDecoration: el.textDecoration || '',
      fill: resolvedFill,
    });
  }, [
    el.html, el.text, el.fontSize, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle,
    el.textDecoration, el.fill, isPlaceholder,
  ]);

  const prepared = useMemo(() => {
    const items: PreparedBlock[] = [];
    let totalHeight = 0;

    for (const block of blocks) {
      if (block.kind === 'text') {
        const layout = layoutRuns(block.runs, innerWidth, el.lineHeight || 1.5);
        if (layout.lines.length === 0) continue;
        items.push({ kind: 'text', layout, height: layout.totalHeight });
        totalHeight += layout.totalHeight;
      } else {
        const h = measureTableHeight(block.data, innerWidth, el.fontSize);
        items.push({ kind: 'table', data: block.data, height: h });
        totalHeight += h;
      }
    }

    if (items.length > 1 && BLOCK_MARGIN > 0) {
      totalHeight += (items.length - 1) * BLOCK_MARGIN;
    }

    return { items, totalHeight };
  }, [blocks, innerWidth, el.lineHeight, el.fontSize]);

  /* ============================================================
     РџСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚
     ============================================================ */
  if (el.richText === false) {
    const pad = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
    const innerW = Math.max(1, el.width - pad.left - pad.right);
    const innerH = Math.max(1, el.height - pad.top - pad.bottom);
    const lineH = el.lineHeight || 1.4;

    const bg2 = el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' ? el.background : 'transparent';
    const hitFill2 = bg2 === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.001)' : bg2;

    const hitH2 = Math.max(
      el.height,
      el.fontSize * lineH * ((el.text || '').split('\n').length || 1) + pad.top + pad.bottom,
    );

    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id}
        name="element"
        x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
        width={el.width} height={el.height}
        clientRectFunc={() => ({ x: 0, y: 0, width: el.width, height: el.height })}
        draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(e); }}
        onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart}
        onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
        onTransformEnd={(e) => {
          const n = e.target;
          const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
          n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
          onUpdate({
            x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
            width: Math.max(20, el.width * sx),
            height: Math.max(20, el.height * sy),
          });
        }}
      >
        <Rect width={el.width} height={hitH2} fill={hitFill2} listening={true} />
        {!isEditing && (
          <KonvaText
            x={pad.left}
            y={pad.top}
            width={innerW}
            height={innerH}
            text={el.text || ''}
            fontSize={el.fontSize}
            fontFamily={el.fontFamily}
            fontStyle={el.fontStyle}
            textDecoration={el.textDecoration}
            lineHeight={lineH}
            fill={isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : el.fill}
            align={el.align}
            verticalAlign={el.verticalAlign}
            listening={false}
          />
        )}
      </Group>
    );
  }

  /* ============================================================
     Rich-С‚РµРєСЃС‚
     ============================================================ */
  let offsetY = padding.top;
  if (el.verticalAlign === 'middle') {
    offsetY = padding.top + Math.max(0, (innerHeight - prepared.totalHeight) / 2);
  } else if (el.verticalAlign === 'bottom') {
    offsetY = padding.top + Math.max(0, innerHeight - prepared.totalHeight);
  }

  const effectiveFill = isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : el.fill;
  const bg = el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' ? el.background : 'transparent';
  const hitFill = bg === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.001)' : bg;

  const hitHeight = Math.max(
    el.height,
    padding.top + prepared.totalHeight + padding.bottom,
  );

  let cursorY = offsetY;

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x}
      y={el.y}
      rotation={el.rotation}
      width={el.width}
      height={el.height}
      clientRectFunc={() => ({ x: 0, y: 0, width: el.width, height: el.height })}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX();
        const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(20, el.width * sx),
          height: Math.max(20, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      <Rect width={el.width} height={hitHeight} fill={hitFill} listening={true} />

      {!isEditing && prepared.items.map((item, bi) => {
        const blockTop = cursorY;
        cursorY += item.height;
        if (bi < prepared.items.length - 1) cursorY += BLOCK_MARGIN;

        if (item.kind === 'text') {
          return (
            <React.Fragment key={`b-${bi}`}>
              {item.layout.lines.map((line: PositionedRun[], i: number) => {
                const lineWidth = line.reduce((acc: number, r: PositionedRun) => acc + r.width, 0);
                let lineOffsetX = 0;
                if (el.align === 'center') {
                  lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, (innerWidth - lineWidth) / 2);
                } else if (el.align === 'right') {
                  lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, innerWidth - lineWidth);
                }

                return line.map((run, j) => {
                  const x = padding.left + lineOffsetX + run.x;
                  const y = blockTop + i * item.layout.lineHeight;
                  const baselineOffset = (item.layout.lineHeight - run.actualFontSize) / 2;

                  const resolvedFontStyle =
                    run.bold && run.italic ? 'italic bold'
                    : run.bold ? 'bold'
                    : run.italic ? 'italic'
                    : isPlaceholder ? 'italic'
                    : 'normal';

                  return (
                    <React.Fragment key={`${i}-${j}`}>
                      {run.bgColor && (
                        <Rect
                          x={x}
                          y={y + baselineOffset * 0.5}
                          width={run.width}
                          height={run.actualFontSize * 1.2}
                          fill={run.bgColor}
                          listening={false}
                        />
                      )}
                      <KonvaText
                        x={x}
                        y={y}
                        text={run.text}
                        fontSize={run.actualFontSize}
                        fontFamily={run.fontFamily || el.fontFamily}
                        fontStyle={resolvedFontStyle}
                        textDecoration={
                          run.underline && run.strike ? 'underline line-through'
                            : run.underline ? 'underline'
                            : run.strike ? 'line-through'
                            : ''
                        }
                        fill={run.color || effectiveFill}
                        listening={false}
                      />
                    </React.Fragment>
                  );
                });
              })}
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        }

        return (
          <RichTable
            key={`table-${bi}`}
            data={item.data}
            x={padding.left}
            y={blockTop}
            width={innerWidth}
            fontFamily={el.fontFamily}
            fallbackFontSize={el.fontSize}
            fallbackFill={el.fill}
          />
        );
      })}
    </Group>
  );
};

  icon?: string;
  defaultOpen?: boolean;
  children: React.ReactNode;

    <div className={`tool-section ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}>

      </button>
      {open && <div className="section-body">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  );
};

            onClick={() => { const l = newLine(); l.isArrow = true; add(l); }}>
            <span className="tile-ico">вћ¤</span><span>РЎС‚СЂРµР»РєР°</span>

            <span className="tile-ico">в…</span><span>Р—РІРµР·РґР°</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </ToolSection>
      <ToolSection title="РЁС‚Р°РјРїС‹" icon="рџ”–">
  <StampLibraryPanel editor={editor} />

        <label className="check-row">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={editor.fillMode} onChange={(e) => editor.setFillMode(e.target.checked)} />
          <span>Р РµР¶РёРј Р·Р°Р»РёРІРєРё</span>
        </label>
        <label className="field-label">Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°Р»РёРІРєРё</label>
        <input type="color" className="field" value={editor.fillColor} onChange={(e) => editor.setFillColor(e.target.value)} />
        <p className="hint-sm">
          РљР»РёРє РїРѕ С„РёРіСѓСЂРµ Р·Р°Р»РёРІР°РµС‚ РµС‘.<br />
          РљР»РёРєРё РїРѕ РїСѓСЃС‚РѕРјСѓ РјРµСЃС‚Сѓ вЂ” СЂРёСЃСѓСЋС‚ РїРѕР»РёРіРѕРЅ.<br />
          Enter / РґРІРѕР№РЅРѕР№ РєР»РёРє вЂ” Р·Р°РјРєРЅСѓС‚СЊ.<br />
          Esc вЂ” РѕС‚РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ.
        </p>

        </label>

          <button className="tool-btn" onClick={editor.undo} disabled={!editor.canUndo}>в†¶ РћС‚РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
          <button className="tool-btn" onClick={editor.redo} disabled={!editor.canRedo}>в†· РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚СЊ</button>
        </div>
      </ToolSection>
    </aside>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { EditorApi } from '../../hooks/useEditor';
import type {
  DashType,
  TextAlign,
  TextVerticalAlign,
  DataFieldType,
  Padding,
} from '../../types';

interface Props {
  editor: EditorApi;
}

/* ---------- Р’СЃРїРѕРјРѕРіР°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂРѕР»С‹ ---------- */

const RotationControl: React.FC<{ value: number; onChange: (v: number) => void }> = ({ value, onChange }) => (
  <>
    <label>РџРѕРІРѕСЂРѕС‚
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <input type="range" min="-180" max="180" value={value}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(+e.target.value)} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 0 }} />
        <input type="number" min="-180" max="180" value={Math.round(value)}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(+e.target.value)} style={{ width: 64 }} />
        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#666' }}>В°</span>
      </div>
    </label>
    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr 1fr', gap: 4, marginTop: 4 }}>
      <button style={{ margin: 0 }} onClick={() => onChange(value - 90)}>в€’90В°</button>
      <button style={{ margin: 0 }} onClick={() => onChange(0)}>0В°</button>
      <button style={{ margin: 0 }} onClick={() => onChange(value + 90)}>+90В°</button>
    </div>
  </>
);

const PaddingEditor: React.FC<{ value: Padding; onChange: (p: Padding) => void }> = ({ value, onChange }) => (
  <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
    <label>Р’РµСЂС…<input type="number" min="0" value={value.top} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, top: +e.target.value })} /></label>
    <label>РќРёР·<input type="number" min="0" value={value.bottom} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, bottom: +e.target.value })} /></label>
    <label>РЎР»РµРІР°<input type="number" min="0" value={value.left} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, left: +e.target.value })} /></label>
    <label>РЎРїСЂР°РІР°<input type="number" min="0" value={value.right} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, right: +e.target.value })} /></label>
  </div>
);

/* ---------- Р‘Р»РѕРє РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё РѕРґРЅРѕР№ Р·РѕРЅС‹ С€С‚Р°РјРїР° ---------- */

const StampZoneEditor: React.FC<{
  label: string;
  prefix: 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom';
  text: string;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  color: string;
  fallbackColor: string;
  onChange: (patch: Record<string, any>) => void;
}> = ({
  label, prefix, text, fontSize, fontFamily, fontStyle, textDecoration, color, fallbackColor, onChange,
}) => {
  const hasBold = (fontStyle || '').includes('bold');
  const hasItalic = (fontStyle || '').includes('italic');
  const hasUnderline = (textDecoration || '').includes('underline');

  const toggleStyle = (flag: 'bold' | 'italic') => {
    const parts = (fontStyle || '').split(' ').filter(Boolean);
    const has = parts.includes(flag);
    const next = has ? parts.filter((p) => p !== flag) : [...parts, flag];
    onChange({ [`${prefix}FontStyle`]: next.join(' ') || 'normal' });
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ marginTop: 8, padding: 8, border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 6 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: '#374151', marginBottom: 6 }}>
        {label}
      </div>

      <label>РўРµРєСЃС‚
        <input
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ [`${prefix}Text`]: e.target.value })}
        />
      </label>

      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
        <label>Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ
          <input
            type="number" min="6" max="200"
            value={fontSize}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ [`${prefix}FontSize`]: +e.target.value })}
          />
        </label>
        <label>РЎРµРјРµР№СЃС‚РІРѕ
          <select
            value={fontFamily}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ [`${prefix}FontFamily`]: e.target.value })}
          >
            <option value="Arial">Arial</option>
            <option value="Times New Roman">Times New Roman</option>
            <option value="Courier New">Courier New</option>
            <option value="Georgia">Georgia</option>
            <option value="Verdana">Verdana</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, marginTop: 6 }}>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={hasBold ? 'active' : ''}
          style={{ flex: 1, fontWeight: 700 }}
          onClick={() => toggleStyle('bold')}
        >B</button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={hasItalic ? 'active' : ''}
          style={{ flex: 1, fontStyle: 'italic' }}
          onClick={() => toggleStyle('italic')}
        >I</button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={hasUnderline ? 'active' : ''}
          style={{ flex: 1, textDecoration: 'underline' }}
          onClick={() => onChange({
            [`${prefix}TextDecoration`]: hasUnderline ? '' : 'underline',
          })}
        >U</button>
      </div>

      <label>Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°
        <input
          type="color"
          value={color || fallbackColor}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ [`${prefix}Color`]: e.target.value })}
        />
      </label>
    </div>
  );
};

/* ---------- РћСЃРЅРѕРІРЅР°СЏ РїР°РЅРµР»СЊ ---------- */

export const PropertiesPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ editor }) => {
  const selected = editor.selected;
  const selectedId = editor.selectedId;

    if (selectedId) editor.updateWithHistory(selectedId, patch);
  };

  return (
    <aside className="props">
      <h3>вљ™пёЏ РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР°</h3>

      {editor.selectedIds.length > 1 && (
        <p className="empty">
          Р’С‹Р±СЂР°РЅРѕ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ: {editor.selectedIds.length}. РљР»РёРєРЅРёС‚Рµ РѕРґРёРЅ РґР»СЏ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё.
        </p>
      )}

      {selected && editor.selectedIds.length === 1 && (
        <>
          {/* РћР±С‰РёРµ РґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, marginBottom: 8 }}>
            <button style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => editor.moveLayer(1)}>в¬† Р’РїРµСЂС‘Рґ</button>
            <button style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => editor.moveLayer(-1)}>в¬‡ РќР°Р·Р°Рґ</button>
          </div>
          <button className="danger" onClick={editor.removeSelected}>рџ—‘ РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ</button>
          <button onClick={editor.copyStyle}>рџЋЁ РљРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ СЃС‚РёР»СЊ</button>

          {/* ---------- FILL ---------- */}
          {selected.type === 'fill' && (
            <>

              <label>Р¦РІРµС‚
                <input type="color" value={selected.fill}
                  onChange={(e) => update({ fill: e.target.value })} />
              </label>
              <p className="hint-sm">
                РўРѕС‡РєРё РїРѕР»РёРіРѕРЅР° С‚СЏРЅРёС‚Рµ Р·Р° Р¶С‘Р»С‚С‹Рµ РјР°СЂРєРµСЂС‹.<br />
                РџСЂРѕР·СЂР°С‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ СѓРїСЂР°РІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РѕР±С‰РµР№ РґР»СЏ РІСЃРµС… Р·Р°Р»РёРІРѕРє.

          {/* ---------- LINE ---------- */}
          {selected.type === 'line' && (

              <h4>Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</h4>
              <label>Р¦РІРµС‚
                <input type="color" value={selected.stroke}

                <input type="number" min="1" value={selected.strokeWidth}

              <label>РўРёРї

                  <option value="dotted">РўРѕС‡РєРё</option>

                Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№ РєРѕРЅС‚СѓСЂ
              </label>

              {/* ---------- РўСЂРё Р·РѕРЅС‹ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°: РІРµСЂС… / С†РµРЅС‚СЂ / РЅРёР· ---------- */}

              <StampZoneEditor
                label="Р’РµСЂС…РЅРёР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚"

                color={selected.topColor}
                fallbackColor={selected.stampColor}
                onChange={update}
              />

              <StampZoneEditor
                label="Р¦РµРЅС‚СЂР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚"
                prefix="center"
                text={selected.centerText}
                fontSize={selected.centerFontSize}
                fontFamily={selected.centerFontFamily || 'Arial'}

                color={selected.centerColor}
                fallbackColor={selected.stampColor}

              />

              <StampZoneEditor
                label="РќРёР¶РЅРёР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚"

                textDecoration={selected.bottomTextDecoration || ''}
                color={selected.bottomColor}
                fallbackColor={selected.stampColor}
                onChange={update}
              />

                      ? parts.filter((p) => p !== 'bold').join(' ') || 'normal'

                }}>+ РЎС‚СЂРѕРєР°</button>

                  if (selected.rows <= 1) return;

                <input type="number" value={selected.fontSize}

            </>

          {/* ---------- FOOTER ---------- */}
          {selected.type === 'footer' && (

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { EditorApi } from '../../hooks/useEditor';
import { DataPanel } from '../../DataPanel';
import { PropertiesPanel } from './PropertiesPanel';

type TabKey = 'data' | 'props';

interface Props {
  editor: EditorApi;
}

export const RightPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ editor }) => {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<TabKey>('data');

  return (
    <aside className="right-panel">
      <div className="rp-tabs">
        <button
          type="button"
          className={`rp-tab ${tab === 'data' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setTab('data')}
        >
          рџ“Љ Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={`rp-tab ${tab === 'props' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setTab('props')}
        >
          вљ™пёЏ РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР°
          {editor.selectedIds.length > 0 && (
            <span className="rp-tab-badge">{editor.selectedIds.length}</span>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="rp-body">
        {tab === 'data' && <DataPanel data={editor.data} setData={editor.setData} />}
        {tab === 'props' && <PropertiesPanel editor={editor} />}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { EditorApi } from '../../hooks/useEditor';
import { SlideThumb } from '../../SlideThumb';

interface Props {
  editor: EditorApi;
}

export const SlidesBar: React.FC<Props> = ({ editor }) => (
  <footer className="slides">
    {editor.slides.map((s, i) => (
      <SlideThumb
        key={s.id}
        slide={s}
        index={i}
        active={s.id === editor.activeId}
        onClick={() => {
          editor.setActiveId(s.id);
          editor.clearSelection();
          editor.setFloatingPos(null);
          editor.setGuides({ v: [], h: [] });
        }}
        onDelete={() => editor.removeSlide(s.id)}
      />
    ))}
    <button className="add-slide" onClick={editor.addSlide}>
      + РЎР»Р°Р№Рґ
    </button>
  </footer>
);
