import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  title: string;
  colors: string[];
  onPick: (color: string) => void;
  onReset?: () => void;
  resetLabel?: string;
}

export const ColorPickerPopover: React.FC<Props> = ({
  title, colors, onPick, onReset, resetLabel = 'РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ',
}) => (
  <div className="rt-popover">
    <div className="rt-popover-title">{title}</div>
    <div className="rt-color-grid">
      {colors.map((c) => (
        <button
          key={c}
          type="button"
          className="rt-color-cell"
          style={{ background: c }}
          title={c}
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onPick(c); }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
    {onReset && (
      <button
        type="button"
        className="rt-popover-action"
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onReset(); }}
      >
        {resetLabel}
      </button>
    )}
  </div>
);

/* ============================================================
   РљР°СЃС‚РѕРјРЅС‹Рµ TipTap-СЂР°СЃС€РёСЂРµРЅРёСЏ. РќРёРєР°РєРѕРіРѕ UI, С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіСѓСЂР°С†РёСЏ.
   ============================================================ */

import { Extension } from '@tiptap/core';
import TableCell from '@tiptap/extension-table-cell';
import TableHeader from '@tiptap/extension-table-header';

/* ---------- FontSize ---------- */
export const FontSize = Extension.create({
  name: 'fontSize',
  addOptions() {
    return { types: ['textStyle'] };
  },
  addGlobalAttributes() {
    return [{
      types: this.options.types,
      attributes: {
        fontSize: {
          default: null,
          parseHTML: (el) => el.style.fontSize?.replace(/['"]+/g, '') || null,
          renderHTML: (attrs) =>
            attrs.fontSize ? { style: `font-size: ${attrs.fontSize}` } : {},
        },
      },
    }];
  },
  addCommands() {
    return {
      setFontSize: (fontSize: string) => ({ chain }: any) =>
        chain().setMark('textStyle', { fontSize }).run(),
      unsetFontSize: () => ({ chain }: any) =>
        chain().setMark('textStyle', { fontSize: null }).removeEmptyTextStyle().run(),
    } as any;
  },
});

/* ---------- РђС‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚С‹ СЏС‡РµРµРє С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹ ---------- */
const CELL_ATTRIBUTES = {
  bgColor: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.backgroundColor || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.bgColor ? { style: `background-color: ${attrs.bgColor}` } : {},
  },
  borderColor: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderColor || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.borderColor ? { style: `border-color: ${attrs.borderColor}` } : {},
  },
  borderWidth: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderWidth || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.borderWidth ? { style: `border-width: ${attrs.borderWidth}` } : {},
  },
  borderStyle: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderStyle || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.borderStyle ? { style: `border-style: ${attrs.borderStyle}` } : {},
  },
  cellPadding: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.padding || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) =>
      attrs.cellPadding ? { style: `padding: ${attrs.cellPadding}` } : {},
  },
};

export const CustomTableCell = TableCell.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return { ...this.parent?.(), ...CELL_ATTRIBUTES };
  },
});

export const CustomTableHeader = TableHeader.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return { ...this.parent?.(), ...CELL_ATTRIBUTES };
  },
});

/* ---------- РќР°Р±РѕСЂ СЂР°СЃС€РёСЂРµРЅРёР№ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР° ---------- */
import StarterKit from '@tiptap/starter-kit';
import Underline from '@tiptap/extension-underline';
import { TextStyle } from '@tiptap/extension-text-style';
import { Color } from '@tiptap/extension-color';
import { FontFamily } from '@tiptap/extension-font-family';
import TextAlign from '@tiptap/extension-text-align';
import Link from '@tiptap/extension-link';
import Highlight from '@tiptap/extension-highlight';
import Table from '@tiptap/extension-table';
import TableRow from '@tiptap/extension-table-row';

export function buildEditorExtensions(align: string) {
  return [
    StarterKit.configure({ heading: { levels: [1, 2, 3] } }),
    Underline,
    TextStyle,
    Color,
    FontFamily,
    FontSize,
    Highlight.configure({ multicolor: true }),
    TextAlign.configure({
      types: ['heading', 'paragraph'],
      defaultAlignment: (align as any) || 'left',
    }),
    Link.configure({ openOnClick: false, autolink: true }),
    Table.configure({ resizable: true }),
    TableRow,
    CustomTableHeader,
    CustomTableCell,
  ];
}

  open, onToggle, title, trigger, children,

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { Editor } from '@tiptap/react';
import { ToolbarButton, ToolbarIcon } from './ToolbarButton';
import { PopoverWrap } from './PopoverWrap';
import { ColorPickerPopover } from './ColorPickerPopover';
import { TableOperationsToolbar } from './TableOperationsToolbar';
import {
  TEXT_COLORS, BG_COLORS, FONT_SIZES, FONT_FAMILIES,
} from './palettes';

interface Props {
  editor: Editor;
  fallbackFontFamily: string;
  fallbackFontSize: number;
  fallbackColor: string;
}

type PopKind = null | 'color' | 'bg';

export const RichTextToolbar: React.FC<Props> = ({
  editor, fallbackFontFamily, fallbackFontSize, fallbackColor,
}) => {
  const [pop, setPop] = useState<PopKind>(null);
  const toggle = (k: PopKind) => setPop((v) => (v === k ? null : k));

  const currentFontSize = (editor.getAttributes('textStyle').fontSize || `${fallbackFontSize}px`).replace('px', '');
  const currentFontFamily = editor.getAttributes('textStyle').fontFamily || fallbackFontFamily;
  const currentColor = editor.getAttributes('textStyle').color || fallbackColor;
  const currentHighlight = editor.getAttributes('highlight').color || null;

  return (
    <div className="rt-toolbar">

      {/* Undo / Redo */}
      <ToolbarButton title="РћС‚РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ (Ctrl+Z)" onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().undo().run()}>в†¶</ToolbarButton>
      <ToolbarButton title="РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚СЊ (Ctrl+Shift+Z)" onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().redo().run()}>в†·</ToolbarButton>

      <span className="rt-sep" />

      {/* Block */}
      <select
        className="rt-select rt-select-block"
        value={
          editor.isActive('heading', { level: 1 }) ? 'h1'
          : editor.isActive('heading', { level: 2 }) ? 'h2'
          : editor.isActive('heading', { level: 3 }) ? 'h3'
          : 'p'
        }
        onChange={(e) => {
          const v = e.target.value;
          if (v === 'p') editor.chain().focus().setParagraph().run();
          else editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: +v[1] as 1 | 2 | 3 }).run();
        }}
        title="РЎС‚РёР»СЊ Р±Р»РѕРєР°"
      >
        <option value="p">РћР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№</option>
        <option value="h1">H1</option>
        <option value="h2">H2</option>
        <option value="h3">H3</option>
      </select>

      <select
        className="rt-select rt-select-font"
        value={currentFontFamily}
        onChange={(e) => editor.chain().focus().setFontFamily(e.target.value).run()}
        title="РЁСЂРёС„С‚"
      >
        {FONT_FAMILIES.map((f) => (
          <option key={f.value} value={f.value}>{f.label}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <select
        className="rt-select rt-select-size"
        value={currentFontSize}
        onChange={(e) => (editor.chain().focus() as any).setFontSize(`${e.target.value}px`).run()}
        title="Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ"
      >
        {FONT_SIZES.map((s) => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
      </select>

      <span className="rt-sep" />

      {/* Bold / Italic / Underline / Strike */}
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive('bold')} title="Р–РёСЂРЅС‹Р№ (Ctrl+B)"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()}><b>B</b></ToolbarButton>
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive('italic')} title="РљСѓСЂСЃРёРІ (Ctrl+I)"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()}><i>I</i></ToolbarButton>
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive('underline')} title="РџРѕРґС‡С‘СЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№ (Ctrl+U)"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleUnderline().run()}><u>U</u></ToolbarButton>
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive('strike')} title="Р—Р°С‡С‘СЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()}><s>S</s></ToolbarButton>

      {/* Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° */}
      <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
        <button type="button" className="rt-btn rt-color-btn"
          title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°"
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); toggle('color'); }}>
          <span className="rt-letter" style={{ borderBottomColor: currentColor }}>A</span>
        </button>
        {pop === 'color' && (
          <ColorPickerPopover
            title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°"
            colors={TEXT_COLORS.flat()}
            onPick={(c) => { editor.chain().focus().setColor(c).run(); setPop(null); }}
            onReset={() => { editor.chain().focus().unsetColor().run(); setPop(null); }}
            resetLabel="РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ С†РІРµС‚"
          />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Р¤РѕРЅ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° */}
      <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
        <button type="button" className="rt-btn rt-color-btn"
          title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С„РѕРЅР° С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°"
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); toggle('bg'); }}>
          <span className="rt-letter rt-bg-letter"
            style={{
              background: currentHighlight || 'transparent',
              borderBottomColor: currentHighlight || '#9ca3af',
            }}>A</span>
        </button>
        {pop === 'bg' && (
          <ColorPickerPopover
            title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С„РѕРЅР° С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°"
            colors={BG_COLORS.flat()}
            onPick={(c) => { editor.chain().focus().setHighlight({ color: c }).run(); setPop(null); }}
            onReset={() => { editor.chain().focus().unsetHighlight().run(); setPop(null); }}
            resetLabel="РЈР±СЂР°С‚СЊ С„РѕРЅ"
          />
        )}
      </div>

      <span className="rt-sep" />

      {/* Р’С‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёРµ */}
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'left' })} title="РџРѕ Р»РµРІРѕРјСѓ РєСЂР°СЋ"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('left').run()}>
        <ToolbarIcon>вЇ‡</ToolbarIcon>
      </ToolbarButton>
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'center' })} title="РџРѕ С†РµРЅС‚СЂСѓ"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('center').run()}>
        <ToolbarIcon>в‰Ў</ToolbarIcon>
      </ToolbarButton>
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'right' })} title="РџРѕ РїСЂР°РІРѕРјСѓ РєСЂР°СЋ"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('right').run()}>
        <ToolbarIcon>вЇ€</ToolbarIcon>
      </ToolbarButton>
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'justify' })} title="РџРѕ С€РёСЂРёРЅРµ"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('justify').run()}>
        <ToolbarIcon>в°</ToolbarIcon>
      </ToolbarButton>

      <span className="rt-sep" />

      {/* РЎРїРёСЃРєРё */}
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive('bulletList')} title="РњР°СЂРєРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBulletList().run()}>
        <ToolbarIcon>вЂўв‰Ў</ToolbarIcon>
      </ToolbarButton>
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive('orderedList')} title="РќСѓРјРµСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє"
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleOrderedList().run()}>
        <ToolbarIcon>1в‰Ў</ToolbarIcon>
      </ToolbarButton>

      <span className="rt-sep" />

      {/* РЎСЃС‹Р»РєР° */}
      <ToolbarButton active={editor.isActive('link')} title="РЎСЃС‹Р»РєР° (Ctrl+K)"
        onClick={() => {
          const prev = editor.getAttributes('link').href;
          const url = window.prompt('URL:', prev || 'https://');
          if (url === null) return;
          if (url === '') editor.chain().focus().unsetLink().run();
          else editor.chain().focus().setLink({ href: url }).run();
        }}>
        <ToolbarIcon>рџ”—</ToolbarIcon>
      </ToolbarButton>

      {/* РўР°Р±Р»РёС†Р°: РІСЃС‚Р°РІРєР° + РѕРїРµСЂР°С†РёРё */}
      <TableOperationsToolbar editor={editor} />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { Editor } from '@tiptap/react';
import { ToolbarButton, ToolbarIcon } from './ToolbarButton';
import { PopoverWrap } from './PopoverWrap';
import { TableSizePicker } from './TableSizePicker';
import {
  CellFillPopover,
  CellBorderPopover,
  CellPadPopover,
} from './TableCellPopovers';

interface Props {
  editor: Editor;
}

type PopKind = null | 'size' | 'fill' | 'border' | 'pad';

export const TableOperationsToolbar: React.FC<Props> = ({ editor }) => {
  const [pop, setPop] = useState<PopKind>(null);

  const toggle = (kind: PopKind) => setPop((v) => (v === kind ? null : kind));

  const insertTable = (rows: number, cols: number) => {
    editor.chain().focus().insertTable({ rows, cols, withHeaderRow: true }).run();
    setPop(null);
  };

  const setCellAttrs = (attrs: Record<string, any>) => {
    editor.chain().focus().updateAttributes('tableCell', attrs).run();
    editor.chain().focus().updateAttributes('tableHeader', attrs).run();
  };

  const insideTable = editor.isActive('table');

  return (
    <>
      <span className="rt-sep" />

      {/* Р’СЃС‚Р°РІРєР° */}
      <PopoverWrap
        open={pop === 'size'}
        onToggle={() => toggle('size')}
        title="Р’СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Сѓ"
        trigger={<ToolbarIcon>в–¦</ToolbarIcon>}
      >
        <TableSizePicker onPick={insertTable} />
      </PopoverWrap>

      {/* РћРїРµСЂР°С†РёРё вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹ */}
      {insideTable && (
        <>
          <span className="rt-sep" />

          <ToolbarButton title="Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ РЅРёР¶Рµ"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().addRowAfter().run()}>
            <ToolbarIcon>+в†“</ToolbarIcon>
          </ToolbarButton>
          <ToolbarButton title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().deleteRow().run()}>
            <ToolbarIcon>в€’в†“</ToolbarIcon>
          </ToolbarButton>
          <ToolbarButton title="Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‚РѕР»Р±РµС† СЃРїСЂР°РІР°"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().addColumnAfter().run()}>
            <ToolbarIcon>+в†’</ToolbarIcon>
          </ToolbarButton>
          <ToolbarButton title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЃС‚РѕР»Р±РµС†"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().deleteColumn().run()}>
            <ToolbarIcon>в€’в†’</ToolbarIcon>
          </ToolbarButton>

          <span className="rt-sep" />

          <ToolbarButton title="РћР±СЉРµРґРёРЅРёС‚СЊ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().mergeCells().run()}>
            <ToolbarIcon>в‡”</ToolbarIcon>
          </ToolbarButton>
          <ToolbarButton title="Р Р°Р·РґРµР»РёС‚СЊ СЏС‡РµР№РєСѓ"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().splitCell().run()}>
            <ToolbarIcon>в‡•</ToolbarIcon>
          </ToolbarButton>
          <ToolbarButton title="РћР±СЉРµРґРёРЅРёС‚СЊ/СЂР°Р·РґРµР»РёС‚СЊ"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().mergeOrSplit().run()}>
            <ToolbarIcon>в‡№</ToolbarIcon>
          </ToolbarButton>

          <span className="rt-sep" />

          {/* Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° СЏС‡РµР№РєРё */}
          <PopoverWrap
            open={pop === 'fill'}
            onToggle={() => toggle('fill')}
            title="Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° СЏС‡РµР№РєРё"
            trigger={<ToolbarIcon>рџЋЁ</ToolbarIcon>}
          >
            <CellFillPopover
              onSet={(bgColor) => { setCellAttrs({ bgColor }); setPop(null); }}
            />
          </PopoverWrap>

          {/* Р“СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹ */}
          <PopoverWrap
            open={pop === 'border'}
            onToggle={() => toggle('border')}
            title="Р“СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹"
            trigger={<ToolbarIcon>рџ”І</ToolbarIcon>}
          >
            <CellBorderPopover
              onSet={(attrs) => setCellAttrs(attrs)}
            />
          </PopoverWrap>

          {/* Padding */}
          <PopoverWrap
            open={pop === 'pad'}
            onToggle={() => toggle('pad')}
            title="Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРїС‹"
            trigger={<ToolbarIcon>рџ“Џ</ToolbarIcon>}
          >
            <CellPadPopover
              onSet={(cellPadding) => { setCellAttrs({ cellPadding }); setPop(null); }}
            />
          </PopoverWrap>

          <span className="rt-sep" />

          <ToolbarButton title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Сѓ"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().deleteTable().run()}>
            <ToolbarIcon style={{ color: '#dc2626' }}>рџ—‘</ToolbarIcon>
          </ToolbarButton>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

interface Props {

const MAX = 6;

export const TableSizePicker: React.FC<Props> = ({ onPick }) => (

    <div className="rt-popover-title">Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹</div>
    <div className="rt-table-grid">

  </div>

import React from 'react';

export const ToolbarIcon: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => (
  <span className="rt-icon">{children}</span>
);

export interface ToolbarButtonProps {
  active?: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
  title: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const ToolbarButton: React.FC<ToolbarButtonProps> = ({
  active, onClick, title, children,
}) => (
  <button
    type="button"
    className={`rt-btn ${active ? 'active' : ''}`}
    title={title}
    onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onClick(); }}
  >
    {children}
  </button>
);

import React from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampShapeKind, DashType } from '../../types';

interface Props {
  shape: StampShapeItem;
  onChange: (p: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => void;
}

const KIND_LABEL: Record<StampShapeKind, string> = {
  ellipse: 'Р­Р»Р»РёРїСЃ',
  rect: 'РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє',
  triangle: 'РўСЂРµСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє',
  line: 'Р›РёРЅРёСЏ',
};

export const ShapePropsPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ shape, onChange }) => (
  <>
    <h4>РЎР»РѕР№</h4>
    <label>РРјСЏ
      <input value={shape.name || ''} placeholder={KIND_LABEL[shape.kind]}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ name: e.target.value })} />
    </label>
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 6 }}>
      <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <input type="checkbox" checked={!!shape.locked}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ locked: e.target.checked })} />
        Р—Р°РјРѕРє
      </label>
      <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <input type="checkbox" checked={!!shape.hidden}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ hidden: e.target.checked })} />
        РЎРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ
      </label>
    </div>

    <h4>РўРёРї С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹</h4>
    <label>Р¤РѕСЂРјР°
      <select value={shape.kind}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ kind: e.target.value as StampShapeKind })}>
        <option value="ellipse">Р­Р»Р»РёРїСЃ</option>
        <option value="rect">РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє</option>
        <option value="triangle">РўСЂРµСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє</option>
        <option value="line">Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</option>
      </select>
    </label>

    {shape.kind !== 'line' && (
      <>
        <h4>Р“РµРѕРјРµС‚СЂРёСЏ</h4>
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
          <label>X <input type="number" value={Math.round(shape.x)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ x: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label>Y <input type="number" value={Math.round(shape.y)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ y: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label>РЁРёСЂРёРЅР° <input type="number" min="1" value={Math.round(shape.width)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ width: Math.max(1, +e.target.value) })} /></label>
          <label>Р’С‹СЃРѕС‚Р° <input type="number" min="1" value={Math.round(shape.height)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ height: Math.max(1, +e.target.value) })} /></label>
        </div>
      </>
    )}

    {shape.kind === 'rect' && (
      <>
        <h4>РЎРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ</h4>
        <label>Р Р°РґРёСѓСЃ СѓРіР»РѕРІ
          <input type="number" min="0" value={shape.cornerRadius}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ cornerRadius: +e.target.value })} />
        </label>
      </>
    )}

    {shape.kind === 'line' && (
      <>
        <h4>Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</h4>
        <p className="hint-sm">РўРѕС‡РєРё [x1,y1, x2,y2, вЂ¦] С‡РµСЂРµР· Р·Р°РїСЏС‚СѓСЋ, РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё СЃР»РѕСЏ.</p>
        <textarea
          value={(shape.points || []).join(',')}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const arr = e.target.value.split(',').map((x) => +x.trim()).filter((n) => !Number.isNaN(n));
            onChange({ points: arr });
          }} />
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6, marginTop: 6 }}>
          <label>РЎРјРµС‰РµРЅРёРµ X <input type="number" value={Math.round(shape.x)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ x: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label>РЎРјРµС‰РµРЅРёРµ Y <input type="number" value={Math.round(shape.y)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ y: +e.target.value })} /></label>
        </div>
      </>
    )}

    <h4>РћР±РІРѕРґРєР°</h4>
    <label>Р¦РІРµС‚
      <input type="color" value={shape.stroke}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ stroke: e.target.value })} />
    </label>
    <label>РўРѕР»С‰РёРЅР°
      <input type="number" min="0" max="40" value={shape.strokeWidth}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} />
    </label>
    <label>РўРёРї Р»РёРЅРёРё
      <select value={shape.dash}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ dash: e.target.value as DashType })}>
        <option value="solid">РЎРїР»РѕС€РЅР°СЏ</option>
        <option value="dashed">РџСѓРЅРєС‚РёСЂ</option>
        <option value="dotted">РўРѕС‡РєРё</option>
        <option value="dashdot">РЁС‚СЂРёС…-РїСѓРЅРєС‚РёСЂ</option>
        <option value="longdash">Р”Р»РёРЅРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓРЅРєС‚РёСЂ</option>
      </select>
    </label>

    {shape.kind !== 'line' && (
      <>
        <h4>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</h4>
        <label>Р¦РІРµС‚
          <input type="color" value={shape.fill || '#ffffff'}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })} />
        </label>
        <button onClick={() => onChange({ fill: undefined })}>РџСЂРѕР·СЂР°С‡РЅР°СЏ</button>
      </>
    )}
  </>
);

import React, { useRef } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampTextItem, StampShapeItem } from '../../types';
import { canvasFont, drawArcText } from '../../lib/stamps/render';
import { arcTextBBox, findSnapTarget, computeArcParamsForAngle } from './stampSnap';

interface Props {
  text: StampTextItem;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  snapEnabled: boolean;
  draggable: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onDblClick: () => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
}

export const StampArcTextView: React.FC<Props> = ({
  text, shapes, snapEnabled, draggable,
  onSelect, onDblClick, onUpdate,
}) => {
  const groupRef = useRef<Konva.Group>(null);

  const bbox = arcTextBBox({
    text: text.text,
    arcCx: text.arcCx, arcCy: text.arcCy, arcRadius: text.arcRadius,
    arcStartDeg: text.arcStartDeg, arcEndDeg: text.arcEndDeg,
    fontSize: text.fontSize,
  });

  const handlers = {
    onClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
    onTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
    onDblClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); },
    onDblTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); },
  };

  const handleDragEnd = () => {
    const g = groupRef.current;
    if (!g) return;
    const dx = g.x();
    const dy = g.y();
    g.x(0);
    g.y(0);

    const nx = text.arcCx + dx;
    const ny = text.arcCy + dy;

    if (snapEnabled) {
      // РС‰РµРј Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€СѓСЋ С„РёРіСѓСЂСѓ + РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‚ РµС‘ С†РµРЅС‚СЂР° Рє С‚РѕС‡РєРµ
      const target = findSnapTarget(nx, ny, '', shapes, 200);
      if (target) {
        const params = computeArcParamsForAngle(
          target.host,
          target.angleRad,
          text.fontSize,
          text.text.length,
          target.isOutside,
        );
        onUpdate(params);
        return;
      }
    }

    onUpdate({ arcCx: Math.round(nx), arcCy: Math.round(ny) });
  };

  return (
    <Group
      ref={groupRef}
      id={text.id}
      name="text-arc"
      x={0}
      y={0}
      draggable={draggable}
      onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
      {...handlers}
    >
      <Shape
        listening={false}
        sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
          const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
          drawArcText(c, {
            text: text.text,
            cx: text.arcCx, cy: text.arcCy,
            radius: text.arcRadius,
            startDeg: text.arcStartDeg,
            endDeg: text.arcEndDeg,
            flip: text.arcFlip,
            font: canvasFont(text.fontFamily, text.fontStyle, text.fontSize),
            fill: text.fill,
          });
        }}
      />
      <Rect
        x={bbox.x}
        y={bbox.y}
        width={Math.max(20, bbox.width)}
        height={Math.max(20, bbox.height)}
        fill="rgba(99,102,241,0.001)"
        listening={true}
        cornerRadius={4}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import React, {
  useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState,
} from 'react';
import {
  Stage, Layer, Rect, Line, Shape, Transformer, Circle, Text as KonvaText, Group,
} from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type {
  StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampShapeKind,
} from '../../types';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '../../types';
import { uid } from '../../lib/uid';
import { useStampHistory } from './useStampHistory';
import { ShapePropsPanel } from './ShapePropsPanel';
import { TextPropsPanel } from './TextPropsPanel';
import { StampLayersPanel } from './StampLayersPanel';
import { StampShapeView } from './StampShapeView';
import { StampStraightTextView } from './StampStraightTextView';
import { StampArcTextView } from './StampArcTextView';
import {
  snapPosition, computeNewShapeGeometry, findHostShape, computeArcParams,
} from './stampSnap';

const MIN_ZOOM = 0.4;
const MAX_ZOOM = 6;

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | null;
type PlacementMode = 'inside' | 'outside' | 'next';

interface Props {
  initialWidth: number;
  initialHeight: number;
  initialShapes: StampShapeItem[];
  initialTexts: StampTextItem[];
  initialName?: string;
  onSave: (data: {
    name: string;
    width: number;
    height: number;
    shapes: StampShapeItem[];
    texts: StampTextItem[];
  }) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const StampEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({
  initialWidth, initialHeight, initialShapes, initialTexts, initialName,
  onSave, onCancel,
}) => {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initialName || 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С€С‚Р°РјРї');
  const [width, setWidth] = useState(initialWidth);
  const [height, setHeight] = useState(initialHeight);

  const history = useStampHistory({ shapes: initialShapes, texts: initialTexts });
  const { shapes, texts } = history.state;

  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<SelectionType>(null);
  const [placementMode, setPlacementMode] = useState<PlacementMode>('inside');

  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [showGrid, setShowGrid] = useState(true);
  const [snap, setSnap] = useState(true);

  /* РџСЂРµРІСЊСЋ С‚РѕР»С‰РёРЅС‹ РїСЂРё РґСЂР°РіРµ РїРѕР»Р·СѓРЅРєР° */
  const [strokePreview, setStrokePreview] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const stageWrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);
  const layerRef = useRef<Konva.Layer>(null);

  const [wrapSize, setWrapSize] = useState({ w: 800, h: 600 });
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = stageWrapRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const update = () => {
      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      setWrapSize({ w: rect.width, h: rect.height });
    };
    update();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(update);
    ro.observe(el);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fitX = (wrapSize.w - 60) / width;
    const fitY = (wrapSize.h - 60) / height;
    setZoom(Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(fitX, fitY))));
  }, [width, height, wrapSize.w, wrapSize.h]);

  /* Transformer вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ shape */
  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current;
    const layer = layerRef.current;
    if (!tr || !layer) return;
    if (!selectedId || selectedType !== 'shape') {
      tr.nodes([]);
      layer.batchDraw();
      return;
    }
    const node = layer.findOne('#' + selectedId);
    if (node) tr.nodes([node]); else tr.nodes([]);
    layer.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts]);

  /* ---------- History ---------- */
  const commit = useCallback((nextShapes: StampShapeItem[], nextTexts: StampTextItem[]) => {
    history.push({ shapes: nextShapes, texts: nextTexts });
  }, [history]);

  const updateShape = useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => {
    commit(shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...patch } : s)), texts);
  }, [shapes, texts, commit]);

  const updateText = useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => {
    commit(shapes, texts.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, ...patch } : t)));
  }, [shapes, texts, commit]);

  /* ---------- Add shape ---------- */
  const addShape = (kind: StampShapeKind) => {
    const id = uid();
    let baseW = 160, baseH = 160;
    if (kind === 'rect') { baseW = 160; baseH = 100; }
    if (kind === 'triangle') { baseW = 140; baseH = 120; }
    if (kind === 'line') { baseW = 160; baseH = 0; }
    if (kind === 'circle') { baseW = 140; baseH = 140; }

    const host = selectedType === 'shape'
      ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) || null
      : null;

    const geom = computeNewShapeGeometry(host, baseW, baseH, width, height, placementMode, 12);

    /* Р”Р»СЏ РєСЂСѓРіР° w=h РІСЃРµРіРґР° вЂ” Р±РµСЂС‘Рј РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј */
    const newW = kind === 'circle' ? Math.min(geom.width, geom.height) : geom.width;
    const newH = kind === 'circle' ? Math.min(geom.width, geom.height) : geom.height;

    const newShape: StampShapeItem = {
      id, kind,
      x: geom.x, y: geom.y,
      width: newW, height: newH,
      cornerRadius: kind === 'rect' ? 12 : 0,
      stroke: '#1a1a1a',
      strokeWidth: 2,
      dash: 'solid',
    };
    if (kind === 'line') newShape.points = [0, 0, baseW, 0];

    commit([...shapes, newShape], texts);
    setSelectedId(id); setSelectedType('shape');
  };

  /* ---------- Add text ---------- */
  const addText = (mode: 'straight' | 'arc') => {
    const id = uid();

    if (mode === 'straight') {
      const host = selectedType === 'shape'
        ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) || null
        : null;
      const w = 180, h = 24;
      const pos = host
        ? { x: host.x + (host.width - w) / 2, y: host.y + (host.height - h) / 2 }
        : { x: (width - w) / 2, y: (height - h) / 2 };

      const t: StampTextItem = {
        id, text: 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚', mode: 'straight',
        x: Math.round(pos.x), y: Math.round(pos.y), width: w, align: 'center',
        arcCx: width / 2, arcCy: height / 2, arcRadius: 60,
        arcStartDeg: -30, arcEndDeg: 30, arcFlip: false,
        fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
        textDecoration: '', fill: '#1a1a1a',
      };
      commit(shapes, [...texts, t]);
      setSelectedId(id); setSelectedType('text');
      return;
    }

    /* arc-С‚РµРєСЃС‚: РїСЂРёРІСЏР·С‹РІР°РµРј Рє host'Сѓ СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ */
    const host = selectedType === 'shape'
      ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) || null
      : findHostShape(width / 2, height / 2, '', shapes);

    const hostShape: StampShapeItem = host || {
      id: 'fake', kind: 'ellipse',
      x: width * 0.1, y: height * 0.1,
      width: width * 0.8, height: height * 0.8,
      cornerRadius: 0, stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
    };
    const params = computeArcParams(hostShape, 14, 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚'.length, true, 0);

    const t: StampTextItem = {
      id, text: 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚', mode: 'arc',
      x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, align: 'center',
      ...params,
      fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
      textDecoration: '', fill: '#1a1a1a',
    };
    commit(shapes, [...texts, t]);
    setSelectedId(id); setSelectedType('text');
  };

  /* ---------- Delete / duplicate ---------- */
  const deleteSelected = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selectedId || !selectedType) return;
    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
      commit(shapes.filter((s) => s.id !== selectedId), texts);
    } else {
      commit(shapes, texts.filter((t) => t.id !== selectedId));
    }
    setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null);
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts, commit]);

  const duplicateSelected = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selectedId || !selectedType) return;
    const offset = 12;
    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
      const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === selectedId);
      if (!s) return;
      const copy: StampShapeItem = {
        ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s)), id: uid(),
        x: s.x + offset, y: s.y + offset,
      };
      commit([...shapes, copy], texts);
      setSelectedId(copy.id);
    } else {
      const t = texts.find((x) => x.id === selectedId);
      if (!t) return;
      const copy: StampTextItem = {
        ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t)), id: uid(),
      };
      if (copy.mode === 'straight') { copy.x += offset; copy.y += offset; }
      else { copy.arcCx += offset; copy.arcCy += offset; }
      commit(shapes, [...texts, copy]);
      setSelectedId(copy.id);
    }
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts, commit]);

  /* ---------- Layer toggles ---------- */
  const toggleHidden = useCallback((id: string, type: 'shape' | 'text') => {
    if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, hidden: !s.hidden } : s)), texts);
    else commit(shapes, texts.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, hidden: !t.hidden } : t)));
  }, [shapes, texts, commit]);

  const toggleLocked = useCallback((id: string, type: 'shape' | 'text') => {
    if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, locked: !s.locked } : s)), texts);
    else commit(shapes, texts.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, locked: !t.locked } : t)));
  }, [shapes, texts, commit]);

  const reorder = useCallback((id: string, type: 'shape' | 'text', dir: 1 | -1) => {
    if (type === 'shape') {
      const arr = [...shapes];
      const i = arr.findIndex((x) => x.id === id);
      const j = i + dir;
      if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= arr.length) return;
      [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
      commit(arr, texts);
    } else {
      const arr = [...texts];
      const i = arr.findIndex((x) => x.id === id);
      const j = i + dir;
      if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= arr.length) return;
      [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
      commit(shapes, arr);
    }
  }, [shapes, texts, commit]);

  /* ---------- Keyboard ---------- */
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const t = e.target as HTMLElement;
      const isField =
        t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.tagName === 'SELECT' ||
        t.isContentEditable;
      const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;

      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (selectedId) { setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null); }
        else onCancel();
        return;
      }
      if (isField) return;
      if ((e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') && selectedId) {
        e.preventDefault(); deleteSelected();
      } else if (ctrl && e.code === 'KeyD' && selectedId) {
        e.preventDefault(); duplicateSelected();
      } else if (ctrl && e.code === 'KeyZ' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault(); history.undo();
      } else if (ctrl && (e.code === 'KeyY' || (e.code === 'KeyZ' && e.shiftKey))) {
        e.preventDefault(); history.redo();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [selectedId, onCancel, deleteSelected, duplicateSelected, history]);

  /* ---------- Wheel ---------- */
  const onWheel = useCallback((e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<WheelEvent>) => {
    e.evt.preventDefault();
    const dir = e.evt.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const oldZoom = zoom;
    const next = Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, +(oldZoom + dir * 0.08).toFixed(3)));
    if (next === oldZoom) return;
    const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
    if (!pointer) { setZoom(next); return; }
    const mx = (pointer.x - pan.x) / oldZoom;
    const my = (pointer.y - pan.y) / oldZoom;
    setPan({ x: pointer.x - mx * next, y: pointer.y - my * next });
    setZoom(next);
  }, [zoom, pan]);

  /* ---------- Drag handlers ---------- */
  const handleShapeDragEnd = useCallback((id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => {
    const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!s) return;
    const node = e.target;
    const snapped = snapPosition(node.x(), node.y(), s.width, s.height, id, shapes, width, height, snap);
    node.x(snapped.x);
    node.y(snapped.y);
    updateShape(id, { x: snapped.x, y: snapped.y });
  }, [shapes, snap, width, height, updateShape]);

  const handleTextDragEnd = useCallback((id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => {
    const t = texts.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!t) return;
    const node = e.target;
    const snapped = snapPosition(node.x(), node.y(), t.width, t.fontSize * 1.4, id, shapes, width, height, snap);
    node.x(snapped.x);
    node.y(snapped.y);
    updateText(id, { x: snapped.x, y: snapped.y });
  }, [texts, shapes, snap, width, height, updateText]);

  /* ---------- Zoom controls ---------- */
  const zoomIn = () => setZoom((z) => Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, +(z + 0.1).toFixed(2)));
  const zoomOut = () => setZoom((z) => Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, +(z - 0.1).toFixed(2)));
  const zoomFit = () => {
    const fitX = (wrapSize.w - 60) / width;
    const fitY = (wrapSize.h - 60) / height;
    setZoom(Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(fitX, fitY))));
    setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  };

  const handleSave = () => onSave({ name, width, height, shapes, texts });

  const selectedShape = selectedType === 'shape' ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) : null;
  const selectedText = selectedType === 'text' ? texts.find((t) => t.id === selectedId) : null;

  /* ---------- РўРѕР»С‰РёРЅР°-СЃР»Р°Р№РґРµСЂ: РіРµРѕРјРµС‚СЂРёСЏ ---------- */
  const sliderBaseX = selectedShape ? selectedShape.x + selectedShape.width + 30 : 0;
  const sliderBaseY = selectedShape ? selectedShape.y + selectedShape.height / 2 : 0;
  const sliderLen = 140;
  const currentStroke = strokePreview ?? (selectedShape?.strokeWidth ?? 2);
  const handleX = sliderBaseX + Math.min(sliderLen, currentStroke * 5);

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
      <div className="stamp-editor-modal" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>

        {/* РЁР°РїРєР° */}
        <div className="stamp-editor-head">
          <input className="stamp-editor-name" value={name}
            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ С€С‚Р°РјРїР°" />
          <div className="stamp-editor-head-right">
            <label>РЁ
              <input type="number" min="40" max="1200" value={width}
                onChange={(e) => setWidth(Math.max(40, +e.target.value))} />
            </label>
            <label>Р’
              <input type="number" min="40" max="1200" value={height}
                onChange={(e) => setHeight(Math.max(40, +e.target.value))} />
            </label>
            <button className="stamp-btn primary" onClick={handleSave}>рџ’ѕ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
            <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={onCancel}>РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* РўСѓР»Р±Р°СЂ */}
        <div className="stamp-toolbar">
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('circle')}>в¬¤ РљСЂСѓРі</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('ellipse')}>в¬® Р­Р»Р»РёРїСЃ</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('rect')}>в–­ РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('triangle')}>в–і РўСЂРµСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('line')}>вћ– Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</button>

          <span className="stamp-sep" />

          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addText('straight')}>T РџСЂСЏРјРѕР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addText('arc')}>вЊ’ РўРµРєСЃС‚ РїРѕ РґСѓРіРµ</button>

          <span className="stamp-sep" />

          <label className="stamp-check" title="РљСѓРґР° РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏС‚СЊ РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚С‹">
            Р Р°Р·РјРµСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ
            <select value={placementMode}
              onChange={(e) => setPlacementMode(e.target.value as PlacementMode)}
              style={{ width: 110, marginLeft: 4 }}>
              <option value="inside">Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРё</option>
              <option value="outside">РЎРЅР°СЂСѓР¶Рё</option>
              <option value="next">Р СЏРґРѕРј</option>
            </select>
          </label>

          <span className="stamp-sep" />

          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => history.undo()} disabled={!history.canUndo}>в†¶</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => history.redo()} disabled={!history.canRedo}>в†·</button>

          <span className="stamp-sep" />

          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={zoomOut} disabled={zoom <= MIN_ZOOM}>в€’</button>
          <span className="stamp-zoom-label">{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={zoomIn} disabled={zoom >= MAX_ZOOM}>+</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={zoomFit}>в›¶</button>

          <span className="stamp-sep" />

          <label className="stamp-check">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={showGrid} onChange={(e) => setShowGrid(e.target.checked)} />
            РЎРµС‚РєР°
          </label>
          <label className="stamp-check">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={snap} onChange={(e) => setSnap(e.target.checked)} />
            Snap
          </label>
        </div>

        <div className="stamp-editor-body">
          <div className="stamp-editor-left">
            <StampLayersPanel
              shapes={shapes}
              texts={texts}
              selectedId={selectedId}
              selectedType={selectedType}
              onSelect={(id, type) => { setSelectedId(id); setSelectedType(type); }}
              onToggleHidden={toggleHidden}
              onToggleLocked={toggleLocked}
              onDelete={(id, type) => {
                if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.filter((s) => s.id !== id), texts);
                else commit(shapes, texts.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
                if (selectedId === id) { setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null); }
              }}
              onReorder={reorder}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="stamp-editor-canvas" ref={stageWrapRef}>
            <Stage
              ref={stageRef as any}
              width={wrapSize.w}
              height={wrapSize.h}
              x={pan.x} y={pan.y}
              scaleX={zoom} scaleY={zoom}
              onWheel={onWheel}
              onMouseDown={(e) => {
                if (e.target === e.target.getStage()) {
                  setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null);
                }
              }}
            >
              <Layer>
                <Rect x={0} y={0} width={width} height={height}
                  fill="#ffffff"
                  shadowColor="#000" shadowBlur={14} shadowOpacity={0.12}
                  listening={false} />

                {showGrid && (
                  <Shape listening={false} sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
                    const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
                    c.beginPath();
                    for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += GRID_SIZE) { c.moveTo(x, 0); c.lineTo(x, height); }
                    for (let y = 0; y <= height; y += GRID_SIZE) { c.moveTo(0, y); c.lineTo(width, y); }
                    c.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7';
                    c.lineWidth = 1 / zoom;
                    c.stroke();
                  }} />
                )}

                <Line points={[width / 2, 0, width / 2, height]}
                  stroke="#dbeafe" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
                <Line points={[0, height / 2, width, height / 2]}
                  stroke="#dbeafe" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />

                <Rect x={0} y={0} width={width} height={height}
                  stroke="#cbd5e1" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} listening={false} />
              </Layer>

              <Layer ref={layerRef as any}>
                {shapes.map((s) => (
                  s.hidden ? null : (
                    <StampShapeView
                      key={s.id}
                      shape={strokePreview !== null && s.id === selectedShape?.id
                        ? { ...s, strokeWidth: strokePreview }
                        : s}
                      draggable={!s.locked}
                      onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape'); }}
                      onDragEnd={(e) => handleShapeDragEnd(s.id, e)}
                    />
                  )
                ))}

                {texts.map((t) => (
                  t.hidden ? null : t.mode === 'straight' ? (
                    <StampStraightTextView
                      key={t.id}
                      text={t}
                      draggable={!t.locked}
                      onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}
                      onDblClick={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}
                      onDragEnd={(e) => handleTextDragEnd(t.id, e)}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <StampArcTextView
                      key={t.id}
                      text={t}
                      shapes={shapes}
                      snapEnabled={snap}
                      draggable={!t.locked}
                      onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}
                      onDblClick={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}
                      onUpdate={(patch) => updateText(t.id, patch)}
                    />
                  )
                ))}

                {/* Transformer вЂ” СЃ keepRatio РґР»СЏ РєСЂСѓРіР° */}
                <Transformer
                  ref={trRef as any}
                  rotateEnabled={false}
                  keepRatio={selectedShape?.kind === 'circle'}
                  borderStroke="#6366f1"
                  borderDash={[4, 4]}
                  anchorStroke="#6366f1"
                  anchorFill="#eef2ff"
                  anchorSize={8}
                  anchorCornerRadius={2}
                  padding={4}
                  boundBoxFunc={(oldBox, newBox) => {
                    if (newBox.width < 8 || newBox.height < 8) return oldBox;
                    return newBox;
                  }}
                  onTransformEnd={(e) => {
                    const node = e.target;
                    const id = node.id();
                    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
                      const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === id);
                      if (!s) return;
                      const newW = Math.max(8, s.width * node.scaleX());
                      const newH = Math.max(8, s.height * node.scaleY());
                      node.scaleX(1); node.scaleY(1);

                      if (s.kind === 'circle') {
                        const side = Math.max(newW, newH);
                        updateShape(id, {
                          x: Math.round(node.x()),
                          y: Math.round(node.y()),
                          width: Math.round(side),
                          height: Math.round(side),
                        });
                      } else {
                        updateShape(id, {
                          x: Math.round(node.x()),
                          y: Math.round(node.y()),
                          width: Math.round(newW),
                          height: Math.round(newH),
                        });
                      }
                    }
                  }}
                />

                {/* вњ… РЎР»Р°Р№РґРµСЂ С‚РѕР»С‰РёРЅС‹ вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ РІС‹РґРµР»РµРЅРЅРѕР№ С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ */}
                {selectedShape && (
                  <Group>
                    {/* РќР°РїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰Р°СЏ Р»РёРЅРёСЏ */}
                    <Line
                      points={[sliderBaseX, sliderBaseY, sliderBaseX + sliderLen, sliderBaseY]}
                      stroke="#cbd5e1"
                      strokeWidth={3}
                      lineCap="round"
                      listening={false}
                    />
                    {/* РњРµС‚РєР° */}
                    <KonvaText
                      x={sliderBaseX}
                      y={sliderBaseY - 28}
                      text={`РўРѕР»С‰РёРЅР°: ${currentStroke}`}
                      fontSize={11}
                      fontFamily="Arial"
                      fill="#6366f1"
                      listening={false}
                    />
                    {/* Р СѓС‡РєР° вЂ” РїРµСЂРµС‚Р°СЃРєРёРІР°РµРјР°СЏ */}
                    <Circle
                      x={handleX}
                      y={sliderBaseY}
                      radius={9}
                      fill="#6366f1"
                      stroke="#ffffff"
                      strokeWidth={2}
                      draggable
                      onDragStart={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; setStrokePreview(selectedShape.strokeWidth); }}
                      onDragMove={(e) => {
                        e.cancelBubble = true;
                        const nx = e.target.x();
                        const v = Math.max(0, Math.round((nx - sliderBaseX) / 5));
                        setStrokePreview(Math.min(40, v));
                      }}
                      onDragEnd={(e) => {
                        e.cancelBubble = true;
                        const nx = e.target.x();
                        const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(40, Math.round((nx - sliderBaseX) / 5)));
                        /* Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј СЂСѓС‡РєСѓ Рє РїСЂРµРґСЃРєР°Р·СѓРµРјРѕР№ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё вЂ” СЂРµР°РєС‚ СЃР°Рј РїРµСЂРµСЂРёСЃСѓРµС‚ */
                        e.target.x(sliderBaseX + Math.min(sliderLen, v * 5));
                        e.target.y(sliderBaseY);
                        setStrokePreview(null);
                        updateShape(selectedShape.id, { strokeWidth: v });
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                        const c = e.target.getStage();
                        if (c) c.container().style.cursor = 'ew-resize';
                      }}
                      onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                        const c = e.target.getStage();
                        if (c) c.container().style.cursor = 'default';
                      }}
                    />
                  </Group>
                )}
              </Layer>
            </Stage>

            <div className="stamp-editor-info">
              {width}Г—{height} В· {Math.round(zoom * 100)}% В· Р­Р»РµРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ: {shapes.length + texts.length}
              {selectedShape && ` В· ${selectedShape.name || selectedShape.kind}`}
              {selectedText && ` В· ${selectedText.name || 'РўРµРєСЃС‚'}`}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="stamp-editor-right">
            {selectedShape && (
              <ShapePropsPanel shape={selectedShape} onChange={(p) => updateShape(selectedShape.id, p)} />
            )}
            {selectedText && (
              <TextPropsPanel text={selectedText} onChange={(p) => updateText(selectedText.id, p)} />
            )}
            {!selectedShape && !selectedText && (
              <p className="stamp-editor-empty">
                <b>РљР»РёРєРЅРёС‚Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚</b>, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ.<br /><br />
                <b>РўСЏРЅРёС‚Рµ</b> вЂ” РґРІРёРіР°РµС‚СЃСЏ.<br />
                <b>РЈРіРѕР»РѕРє СЂР°РјРєРё</b> вЂ” СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ.<br />
                <b>РЎР»Р°Р№РґРµСЂ СЃРїСЂР°РІР° РѕС‚ С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹</b> вЂ” С‚РѕР»С‰РёРЅР° Р»РёРЅРёРё.<br />
                <b>Delete</b> вЂ” СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ.<br />
                <b>Ctrl+D</b> вЂ” РґСѓР±Р»РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ.<br />
                <b>Ctrl+Z / Shift+Ctrl+Z</b> вЂ” undo / redo.<br />
                <b>РљРѕР»РµСЃРѕ РјС‹С€Рё</b> вЂ” Р·СѓРј.<br />
                <b>Esc</b> вЂ” СЃРЅСЏС‚СЊ РІС‹РґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem } from '../../types';

interface LayerItem {
  id: string;
  type: 'shape' | 'text';
  name: string;
  icon: string;
  hidden?: boolean;
  locked?: boolean;
}

interface Props {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedType: 'shape' | 'text' | null;
  onSelect: (id: string, type: 'shape' | 'text') => void;
  onToggleHidden: (id: string, type: 'shape' | 'text') => void;
  onToggleLocked: (id: string, type: 'shape' | 'text') => void;
  onDelete: (id: string, type: 'shape' | 'text') => void;
  onReorder: (id: string, type: 'shape' | 'text', dir: 1 | -1) => void;
}

export const StampLayersPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({
  shapes, texts, selectedId, selectedType,
  onSelect, onToggleHidden, onToggleLocked, onDelete, onReorder,
}) => {
  /* РЎРІРµСЂС…Сѓ вЂ” СЃР°РјС‹Рµ РІРµСЂС…РЅРёРµ СЃР»РѕРё. РџРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє: С‚РµРєСЃС‚С‹ + С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ РІ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕРј РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєРµ. */
  const items: LayerItem[] = [
    ...texts.map((t) => ({
      id: t.id, type: 'text' as const,
      name: t.name || (t.mode === 'arc' ? 'РўРµРєСЃС‚ (РґСѓРіР°)' : 'РўРµРєСЃС‚'),
      icon: t.mode === 'arc' ? 'вЊ’' : 'T',
      hidden: t.hidden, locked: t.locked,
    })),
    ...shapes.map((s) => ({
      id: s.id, type: 'shape' as const,
      name: s.name || ({ ellipse: 'Р­Р»Р»РёРїСЃ', rect: 'РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє', triangle: 'РўСЂРµСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє', line: 'Р›РёРЅРёСЏ' })[s.kind],
      icon: s.kind === 'ellipse' ? 'в¬¤' : s.kind === 'rect' ? 'в–­' : s.kind === 'triangle' ? 'в–і' : 'вћ–',
      hidden: s.hidden, locked: s.locked,
    })),
  ];

  return (
    <div className="stamp-layers">
      <div className="stamp-layers-head">РЎР»РѕРё ({items.length})</div>
      <div className="stamp-layers-list">
        {items.length === 0 && (
          <p className="stamp-editor-empty">РџСѓСЃС‚Рѕ. Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ С„РёРіСѓСЂСѓ РёР»Рё С‚РµРєСЃС‚.</p>
        )}
        {items.map((item) => {
          const active = item.id === selectedId && item.type === selectedType;
          return (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className={`stamp-layer ${active ? 'active' : ''} ${item.hidden ? 'hidden' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => onSelect(item.id, item.type)}
            >
              <span className="stamp-layer-icon">{item.icon}</span>
              <span className="stamp-layer-name">{item.name}</span>

              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title={item.hidden ? 'РџРѕРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ' : 'РЎРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ'}
                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleHidden(item.id, item.type); }}>
                {item.hidden ? 'рџљ«' : 'рџ‘Ѓ'}
              </button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title={item.locked ? 'Р Р°Р·Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ' : 'Р—Р°Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ'}
                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleLocked(item.id, item.type); }}>
                {item.locked ? 'рџ”’' : 'рџ”“'}
              </button>

              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title="Р’С‹С€Рµ"
                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onReorder(item.id, item.type, 1); }}>в–І</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title="РќРёР¶Рµ"
                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onReorder(item.id, item.type, -1); }}>в–ј</button>

              <button className="stamp-layer-btn danger" title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ"
                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onDelete(item.id, item.type); }}>Г—</button>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Circle, Ellipse, Rect, Line, Text, Shape, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampElement } from '../../types';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP, konvaStyle, canvasFont, drawArcText } from '../../lib/stamps/render';

type Zone = 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom';

interface Props {
  el: StampElement;
  onSelect: (e?: any) => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<StampElement>) => void;
  onDblClick?: (zone: Zone, e?: any) => void;
  onDragStart?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragMove?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragEnd?: (e: any) => void;
  onContextMenu?: (e: any) => void;
  isEditingZone?: Zone | null;
}

export const StampLegacy: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
  isEditingZone = null,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = React.useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!el.centerImage) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.src = el.centerImage;
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
  }, [el.centerImage]);

  const w = el.width, h = el.height, cx = w / 2, cy = h / 2;
  const color = el.stampColor || '#1a1a1a';
  const shape = el.shape || 'circle';
  const dashOuter = STAMP_DASH_MAP[el.strokeDash || 'solid'];
  const dashInner = STAMP_DASH_MAP[el.innerStrokeDash || 'solid'];

  const topSize = el.topFontSize || 14;
  const centerSize = el.centerFontSize || 20;
  const bottomSize = el.bottomFontSize || 12;

  const topColor = el.topColor || color;
  const botColor = el.bottomColor || color;

  const showTop = isEditingZone !== 'top';
  const showCenter = isEditingZone !== 'center';
  const showBottom = isEditingZone !== 'bottom';

  const minR = Math.min(w, h) / 2;

  const zoneHandler = (zone: Zone) => (e: any) => {
    e.cancelBubble = true;
    onDblClick?.(zone, e);
  };

  const renderArc = (
    text: string, radius: number,
    startDeg: number, endDeg: number, flip: boolean,
    fontFamily: string, fontStyle: string, size: number, fill: string,
    key: string,
  ) => (
    <Shape
      key={key}
      listening={false}
      sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
        const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        drawArcText(c, {
          text, cx, cy, radius, startDeg, endDeg, flip,
          font: canvasFont(fontFamily, fontStyle, size),
          fill,
        });
      }}
    />
  );

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(), sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(40, el.width * sx),
          height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      {shape === 'circle' && (<>
        <Circle x={cx} y={cy} radius={minR - 4}
          stroke={color} strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth || 3} dash={dashOuter} />
        {el.showInner && <Circle x={cx} y={cy} radius={minR - 20}
          stroke={color} strokeWidth={el.innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={dashInner} />}
      </>)}

      {shape === 'oval' && (<>
        <Ellipse x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={w / 2 - 4} radiusY={h / 2 - 4}
          stroke={color} strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth || 3} dash={dashOuter} />
        {el.showInner && <Ellipse x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={w / 2 - 20} radiusY={h / 2 - 20}
          stroke={color} strokeWidth={el.innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={dashInner} />}
      </>)}

      {shape === 'rect' && (<>
        <Rect x={4} y={4} width={w - 8} height={h - 8} cornerRadius={6}
          stroke={color} strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth || 3} dash={dashOuter} />
        {el.showInner && <Rect x={16} y={16} width={w - 32} height={h - 32} cornerRadius={4}
          stroke={color} strokeWidth={el.innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={dashInner} />}
      </>)}

      {shape === 'triangle' && (<>
        <Line points={[cx, 4, w - 4, h - 4, 4, h - 4, cx, 4]} closed
          stroke={color} strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth || 3} lineJoin="round" dash={dashOuter} />
        {el.showInner && <Line points={[cx, 20, w - 20, h - 20, 20, h - 20, cx, 20]} closed
          stroke={color} strokeWidth={el.innerStrokeWidth || 1} lineJoin="round" dash={dashInner} />}
      </>)}

      {(shape === 'circle' || shape === 'oval') && (<>
        {showTop && el.topText && renderArc(
          el.topText, minR - 32, -153, -27, false,
          el.topFontFamily || 'Arial', el.topFontStyle || '', topSize, topColor,
          'legacy-top',
        )}
        {showBottom && el.bottomText && renderArc(
          el.bottomText, minR - 32, 153, 27, true,
          el.bottomFontFamily || 'Arial', el.bottomFontStyle || '', bottomSize, botColor,
          'legacy-bot',
        )}
      </>)}

      {(shape === 'rect' || shape === 'triangle') && (<>
        {showTop && (
          <Text x={0} y={18} width={w} align="center" text={el.topText}
            fontSize={topSize} fontFamily={el.topFontFamily || 'Arial'}
            fontStyle={konvaStyle(el.topFontStyle || '')}
            textDecoration={el.topTextDecoration || ''}
            fill={topColor} listening={false} />
        )}
        {showBottom && (
          <Text x={0} y={h - 32} width={w} align="center" text={el.bottomText}
            fontSize={bottomSize} fontFamily={el.bottomFontFamily || 'Arial'}
            fontStyle={konvaStyle(el.bottomFontStyle || '')}
            textDecoration={el.bottomTextDecoration || ''}
            fill={botColor} listening={false} />
        )}
      </>)}

      {showCenter && (
        <Text x={0} y={cy - centerSize / 2 - 2} width={w} align="center"
          text={el.centerText} fontSize={centerSize}
          fontFamily={el.centerFontFamily || 'Arial'}
          fontStyle={konvaStyle(el.centerFontStyle || 'bold')}
          textDecoration={el.centerTextDecoration || ''}
          fill={el.centerColor || color} listening={false} />
      )}

      {img && <KImage x={cx - 35} y={cy - 35} width={70} height={70} image={img} listening={false} />}

      <Rect x={0} y={0} width={w} height={h * 0.33}
        fill="transparent"
        onDblClick={zoneHandler('top')} onDblTap={zoneHandler('top')} />
      <Rect x={0} y={h * 0.33} width={w} height={h * 0.34}
        fill="transparent"
        onDblClick={zoneHandler('center')} onDblTap={zoneHandler('center')} />
      <Rect x={0} y={h * 0.67} width={w} height={h * 0.33}
        fill="transparent"
        onDblClick={zoneHandler('bottom')} onDblTap={zoneHandler('bottom')} />
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { EditorApi } from '../../hooks/useEditor';
import type { StampShape } from '../../types';
import { getAllStamps, addCustomStamp, type StampTemplate } from '../../lib/stamps/library';
import { newStampFromTemplate, newLegacyStamp } from '../../lib/stamps/factories';
import { StampEditor } from './StampEditor';

interface Props {
  editor: EditorApi;
}

const LEGACY_QUICK: { shape: StampShape; label: string; icon: string }[] = [
  { shape: 'circle', label: 'РљСЂСѓРіР»С‹Р№', icon: 'в—Ї' },
  { shape: 'oval', label: 'РћРІР°Р»', icon: 'в¬­' },
  { shape: 'rect', label: 'РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅС‹Р№', icon: 'в–­' },
  { shape: 'triangle', label: 'РўСЂРµСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅС‹Р№', icon: 'в–і' },
];

export const StampLibraryPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ editor }) => {
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const [editorState, setEditorState] = useState<null | {
    width: number; height: number;
    shapes: any[]; texts: any[];
    name?: string;
  }>(null);

  const stamps = useMemo(() => getAllStamps(), [version]);

  const applyStamp = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    editor.addElementWithHistory(newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200));
  };

  const addLegacy = (shape: StampShape) => {
    editor.addElementWithHistory(newLegacyStamp(shape, 200, 200));
  };

  const openNew = () => {
    setEditorState({ width: 200, height: 200, shapes: [], texts: [], name: 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С€С‚Р°РјРї' });
  };

  const handleSave = (data: {
    name: string; width: number; height: number;
    shapes: any[]; texts: any[];
  }) => {
    const tpl: StampTemplate = {
      id: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
      name: data.name,
      icon: 'в­ђ',
      width: data.width,
      height: data.height,
      shapes: data.shapes,
      texts: data.texts,
      custom: true,
    };
    addCustomStamp(tpl);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
    setEditorState(null);
    editor.addElementWithHistory(newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200));
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* -------- Р‘С‹СЃС‚СЂС‹Рµ (СЃС‚Р°СЂР°СЏ РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ) -------- */}
      <label className="field-label">Р‘С‹СЃС‚СЂС‹Рµ</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">
        {LEGACY_QUICK.map((q) => (
          <button key={q.shape} className="tool-tile"
            onClick={() => addLegacy(q.shape)}>
            <span className="tile-ico">{q.icon}</span>
            <span>{q.label}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* -------- Р‘РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєР° -------- */}
      <label className="field-label" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>
        Р‘РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєР°
      </label>
      <div className="tool-grid">
        {stamps.map((s) => (
          <button key={s.id} className="tool-tile" title={s.description}
            onClick={() => applyStamp(s)}>
            <span className="tile-ico">{s.icon || 'в­ђ'}</span>
            <span>{s.name}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <button className="tool-btn" style={{ marginTop: 8 }} onClick={openNew}>
        вћ• РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ С€С‚Р°РјРївЂ¦
      </button>

      {editorState && (
        <StampEditor
          initialWidth={editorState.width}
          initialHeight={editorState.height}
          initialShapes={editorState.shapes}
          initialTexts={editorState.texts}
          initialName={editorState.name}
          onSave={handleSave}
          onCancel={() => setEditorState(null)}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Ellipse, Rect, Line, Text, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem } from '../../types';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP, konvaStyle, canvasFont, drawArcText } from '../../lib/stamps/render';

interface Props {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  onShapeClick?: (id: string) => void;
  onTextClick?: (id: string) => void;
  selectedShapeId?: string | null;
  selectedTextId?: string | null;
  isEditingZone?: 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom' | null;
  /** Р•СЃР»Рё false вЂ” РЅРµ РЅР°РІРµС€РёРІР°РµРј draggable/handlers (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂ). */
  interactive?: boolean;
}

export const StampRenderer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  width, height, shapes, texts,
  onShapeClick, onTextClick,
  selectedShapeId, selectedTextId,
  isEditingZone = null,
  interactive = false,
}) => {
  const dashFor = (d: StampShapeItem['dash']) => STAMP_DASH_MAP[d];

  const renderShape = (s: StampShapeItem) => {
    if (s.hidden) return null;
    const dash = dashFor(s.dash);
    const isSel = s.id === selectedShapeId;
    const click = interactive && onShapeClick
      ? {
          onClick: () => onShapeClick(s.id),
          onTap: () => onShapeClick(s.id),
        }
      : {};

    const common = {
      stroke: s.stroke,
      strokeWidth: s.strokeWidth,
      dash,
      fill: s.fill,
      listening: interactive && !!onShapeClick,
      ...click,
    };

    const selectionShadow = isSel ? {
      shadowColor: '#6366f1', shadowBlur: 10, shadowOpacity: 0.5,
    } : {};

    if (s.kind === 'ellipse') {
      return (
        <Ellipse key={s.id}
          x={s.x + s.width / 2}
          y={s.y + s.height / 2}
          radiusX={s.width / 2}
          radiusY={s.height / 2}
          {...common}
          {...selectionShadow} />
      );
    }
    if (s.kind === 'rect') {
      return (
        <Rect key={s.id}
          x={s.x} y={s.y}
          width={s.width} height={s.height}
          cornerRadius={s.cornerRadius}
          {...common}
          {...selectionShadow} />
      );
    }
    if (s.kind === 'triangle') {
      return (
        <Line key={s.id}
          points={[
            s.x + s.width / 2, s.y,
            s.x + s.width, s.y + s.height,
            s.x, s.y + s.height,
          ]}
          closed
          lineJoin="round"
          {...common}
          {...selectionShadow} />
      );
    }
    if (s.kind === 'line') {
      return (
        <Line key={s.id}
          x={s.x} y={s.y}
          points={s.points || [0, 0, s.width, 0]}
          lineJoin="round"
          {...common}
          {...selectionShadow} />
      );
    }
    return null;
  };

  const renderText = (t: StampTextItem) => {
    if (t.hidden) return null;
    const isSel = t.id === selectedTextId;
    const click = interactive && onTextClick
      ? {
          onClick: () => onTextClick(t.id),
          onTap: () => onTextClick(t.id),
        }
      : {};

    const selectionShadow = isSel ? {
      shadowColor: '#6366f1', shadowBlur: 10, shadowOpacity: 0.5,
    } : {};

    if (t.mode === 'arc') {
      return (
        <Shape key={t.id}
          listening={interactive && !!onTextClick}
          {...click}
          sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
            const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
            drawArcText(c, {
              text: t.text,
              cx: t.arcCx,
              cy: t.arcCy,
              radius: t.arcRadius,
              startDeg: t.arcStartDeg,
              endDeg: t.arcEndDeg,
              flip: t.arcFlip,
              font: canvasFont(t.fontFamily, t.fontStyle, t.fontSize),
              fill: t.fill,
            });
          }} />
      );
    }

    return (
      <Text key={t.id}
        x={t.x} y={t.y} width={t.width}
        text={t.text}
        fontSize={t.fontSize}
        fontFamily={t.fontFamily}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle(t.fontStyle)}
        textDecoration={t.textDecoration}
        fill={t.fill}
        align={t.align}
        listening={interactive && !!onTextClick}
        {...click}
        {...selectionShadow} />
    );
  };

  return (
    <Group width={width} height={height}>
      {shapes.map(renderShape)}
      {!isEditingZone && texts.map(renderText)}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Ellipse, Rect, Line } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../types';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP } from '../../lib/stamps/render';

interface Props {
  shape: StampShapeItem;
  draggable: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onDragEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
}

export const StampShapeView: React.FC<Props> = ({
  shape, draggable, onSelect, onDragEnd,
}) => {
  const dash = STAMP_DASH_MAP[shape.dash];

  const base = {
    stroke: shape.stroke,
    strokeWidth: shape.strokeWidth,
    dash,
    fill: shape.fill || 'transparent',
    strokeScaleEnabled: false,
    listening: true,
  };

  const handlers = {
    onClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
    onTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
  };

  const radius = shape.kind === 'circle'
    ? Math.min(shape.width, shape.height) / 2
    : null;

  return (
    <Group
      id={shape.id}
      name="shape"
      x={shape.x}
      y={shape.y}
      draggable={draggable}
      onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
      {...handlers}
    >
      {shape.kind === 'circle' && radius !== null && (
        <Ellipse
          x={shape.width / 2}
          y={shape.height / 2}
          radiusX={Math.max(1, radius)}
          radiusY={Math.max(1, radius)}
          {...base}
        />
      )}

      {shape.kind === 'ellipse' && (
        <Ellipse
          x={shape.width / 2}
          y={shape.height / 2}
          radiusX={Math.max(1, shape.width / 2)}
          radiusY={Math.max(1, shape.height / 2)}
          {...base}
        />
      )}

      {shape.kind === 'rect' && (
        <Rect
          x={0} y={0}
          width={Math.max(1, shape.width)}
          height={Math.max(1, shape.height)}
          cornerRadius={shape.cornerRadius}
          {...base}
        />
      )}

      {shape.kind === 'triangle' && (
        <Line
          points={[
            shape.width / 2, 0,
            shape.width, shape.height,
            0, shape.height,
            shape.width / 2, 0,
          ]}
          closed
          lineJoin="round"
          {...base}
        />
      )}

      {shape.kind === 'line' && (
        <Line
          x={0} y={0}
          points={shape.points || [0, 0, shape.width, 0]}
          lineJoin="round"
          stroke={shape.stroke}
          strokeWidth={shape.strokeWidth}
          dash={dash}
          strokeScaleEnabled={false}
          listening={true}
          {...handlers}
        />
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};
