import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../types';

export const SNAP_TO_GRID = 10;
export const SNAP_THRESHOLD = 6;

export function snapPosition(
  x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number,
  excludeId: string, shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  canvasW: number, canvasH: number, enableSnap: boolean,
): { x: number; y: number } {
  if (!enableSnap) return { x: Math.round(x), y: Math.round(y) };

  let fx = x, fy = y;

  const gridX = Math.round(x / SNAP_TO_GRID) * SNAP_TO_GRID;
  const gridY = Math.round(y / SNAP_TO_GRID) * SNAP_TO_GRID;
  if (Math.abs(gridX - x) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fx = gridX;
  if (Math.abs(gridY - y) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fy = gridY;

  const cx = canvasW / 2, cy = canvasH / 2;
  if (Math.abs((fx + w / 2) - cx) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fx = cx - w / 2;
  if (Math.abs((fy + h / 2) - cy) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fy = cy - h / 2;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.id === excludeId || s.hidden) continue;
    if (s.kind === 'line') continue;
    const pts = [
      { x: s.x, y: s.y },
      { x: s.x + s.width / 2, y: s.y + s.height / 2 },
      { x: s.x + s.width, y: s.y + s.height },
      { x: s.x + s.width, y: s.y },
      { x: s.x, y: s.y + s.height },
    ];
    for (const p of pts) {
      if (Math.abs(fx - p.x) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fx = p.x;
      if (Math.abs(fy - p.y) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fy = p.y;
      if (Math.abs((fx + w / 2) - p.x) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fx = p.x - w / 2;
      if (Math.abs((fy + h / 2) - p.y) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fy = p.y - h / 2;
    }
  }
  return { x: Math.round(fx), y: Math.round(fy) };
}

/** РќР°Р№С‚Рё С„РёРіСѓСЂСѓ-С…РѕСЃС‚ Рё РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·РєРё РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‚СЂР° Рє С‚РѕС‡РєРµ. */
export function findSnapTarget(
  px: number, py: number,
  excludeId: string,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  maxDistance?: number,
): { host: StampShapeItem; angleRad: number; isOutside: boolean } | null {
  let best: StampShapeItem | null = null;
  let bestDist = Infinity;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.id === excludeId || s.hidden) continue;
    if (s.kind === 'line') continue;
    if (s.width < 30 || s.height < 30) continue;

    const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
    const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
    const d = Math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy);
    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = s; }
  }

  if (!best) return null;

  const cx = best.x + best.width / 2;
  const cy = best.y + best.height / 2;
  const rHost = Math.min(best.width, best.height) / 2;

  // РћРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РµРЅРёРµ РЅР° Р±Р»РёР·РѕСЃС‚СЊ (РЅРµ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·С‹РІР°С‚СЊ, РµСЃР»Рё РґР°Р»РµРєРѕ)
  if (maxDistance !== undefined && bestDist > rHost + maxDistance) return null;

  return {
    host: best,
    angleRad: Math.atan2(py - cy, px - cx),
    isOutside: bestDist > rHost * 0.65,
  };
}

/** РЎРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРѕ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рј API. */
export function findHostShape(
  px: number, py: number,
  excludeId: string, shapes: StampShapeItem[],
): StampShapeItem | null {
  const t = findSnapTarget(px, py, excludeId, shapes);
  return t ? t.host : null;
}

/**
 * РџРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ Рё СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ РЅРѕРІРѕР№ С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ host.
 *  inside  в†’ 60% host, С†РµРЅС‚СЂРёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ
 *  outside в†’ host + 2*padding, РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєР°
 *  next    в†’ С‚РѕС‚ Р¶Рµ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ, СЃРїСЂР°РІР°
 */
export function computeNewShapeGeometry(
  host: StampShapeItem | null,
  baseW: number, baseH: number,
  canvasW: number, canvasH: number,
  mode: 'inside' | 'outside' | 'next',
  padding = 12,
): { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } {
  if (!host) {
    return {
      x: Math.round((canvasW - baseW) / 2),
      y: Math.round((canvasH - baseH) / 2),
      width: baseW, height: baseH,
    };
  }

  const hostCx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const hostCy = host.y + host.height / 2;

  if (mode === 'inside') {
    const scale = 0.6;
    const w = Math.round(host.width * scale);
    const h = Math.round(host.height * scale);
    return { x: Math.round(hostCx - w / 2), y: Math.round(hostCy - h / 2), width: w, height: h };
  }

  if (mode === 'outside') {
    const w = Math.round(host.width + padding * 2);
    const h = Math.round(host.height + padding * 2);
    return { x: Math.round(hostCx - w / 2), y: Math.round(hostCy - h / 2), width: w, height: h };
  }

  return {
    x: Math.round(host.x + host.width + padding),
    y: Math.round(host.y),
    width: baseW, height: baseH,
  };
}

/**
 * Arc-РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂС‹ РґР»СЏ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°, РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ Рє С„РёРіСѓСЂРµ РІ Р·Р°РґР°РЅРЅРѕРј РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРё.
 *
 *  angleRad вЂ” РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‚СЂР° С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ Рє Р¶РµР»Р°РµРјРѕРјСѓ РјРµСЃС‚Сѓ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°
 *  isOutside вЂ” СЃРЅР°СЂСѓР¶Рё РёР»Рё РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РєРѕРЅС‚СѓСЂР°
 *
 * РџРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє Р±СѓРєРІ:
 *   РІРµСЂС…  в†’ СѓРІРµР»РёС‡РёРІР°СЏ СѓРіРѕР» (СЃР»РµРІР°-РЅР°РїСЂР°РІРѕ, СЃРёРјРІРѕР»С‹ "РЅР°СЂСѓР¶Сѓ")
 *   РЅРёР·   в†’ СѓРјРµРЅСЊС€Р°СЏ СѓРіРѕР» (СЃР»РµРІР°-РЅР°РїСЂР°РІРѕ, СЃРёРјРІРѕР»С‹ РїРµСЂРµРІРѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ, РєР°Рє РІ РїРµС‡Р°С‚СЏС…)
 *   Р±РѕРєР°  в†’ СЃРѕРѕС‚РІРµС‚СЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РёР№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє
 */
export function computeArcParamsForAngle(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  angleRad: number,
  fontSize: number,
  textLength: number,
  isOutside: boolean,
): {
  arcCx: number;
  arcCy: number;
  arcRadius: number;
  arcStartDeg: number;
  arcEndDeg: number;
  arcFlip: boolean;
} {
  const cx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const cy = host.y + host.height / 2;

  const baseR = Math.min(host.width, host.height) / 2;
  const radius = isOutside ? baseR + 20 : Math.max(20, baseR - 30);

  const charWidth = fontSize * 0.6;
  const textWidth = Math.max(charWidth, textLength * charWidth);
  const spanRad = Math.min(Math.PI * 1.9, Math.max(0.2, textWidth / radius));
  const halfSpanDeg = (spanRad * 180) / Math.PI / 2;

  const centerDeg = (angleRad * 180) / Math.PI;
  const sinA = Math.sin(angleRad);

  // isTop: С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РІ РІРµСЂС…РЅРµР№ РїРѕР»РѕРІРёРЅРµ С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ (sin < 0)
  // isBottom: РІ РЅРёР¶РЅРµР№ (sin > 0)
  const isTop = sinA < 0;

  let startDeg: number;
  let endDeg: number;

  if (isTop) {
    // СЃР»РµРІР°-РЅР°РїСЂР°РІРѕ = СѓРІРµР»РёС‡РµРЅРёРµ СѓРіР»Р° (РїРѕ С‡Р°СЃРѕРІРѕР№)
    startDeg = centerDeg - halfSpanDeg;
    endDeg = centerDeg + halfSpanDeg;
  } else {
    // СЃР»РµРІР°-РЅР°РїСЂР°РІРѕ = СѓРјРµРЅСЊС€РµРЅРёРµ СѓРіР»Р° (РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІ С‡Р°СЃРѕРІРѕР№)
    startDeg = centerDeg + halfSpanDeg;
    endDeg = centerDeg - halfSpanDeg;
  }

  return {
    arcCx: cx,
    arcCy: cy,
    arcRadius: radius,
    arcStartDeg: startDeg,
    arcEndDeg: endDeg,
    arcFlip: !isTop,
  };
}

/** РЎРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРѕ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рј API. */
export function computeArcParams(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  fontSize: number,
  textLength: number,
  isTop: boolean,
  ringOffset = 0,
): {
  arcCx: number; arcCy: number; arcRadius: number;
  arcStartDeg: number; arcEndDeg: number; arcFlip: boolean;
} {
  const cx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const cy = host.y + host.height / 2;
  const baseR = Math.min(host.width, host.height) / 2;
  const radius = Math.max(10, baseR - 12 - ringOffset * 12);

  const charWidth = fontSize * 0.6;
  const textWidth = Math.max(charWidth, textLength * charWidth);
  const spanRad = Math.min(Math.PI * 1.9, Math.max(0.3, textWidth / radius));
  const halfSpanDeg = (spanRad * 180) / Math.PI / 2;

  if (isTop) {
    return {
      arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
      arcStartDeg: -90 - halfSpanDeg,
      arcEndDeg: -90 + halfSpanDeg,
      arcFlip: false,
    };
  }
  return {
    arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
    arcStartDeg: 90 + halfSpanDeg,
    arcEndDeg: 90 - halfSpanDeg,
    arcFlip: true,
  };
}

export function arcTextBBox(t: {
  text: string;
  arcCx: number; arcCy: number; arcRadius: number;
  arcStartDeg: number; arcEndDeg: number; fontSize: number;
}): { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } {
  const chars = t.text.split('');
  if (chars.length === 0) {
    return { x: t.arcCx - 20, y: t.arcCy - 20, width: 40, height: 40 };
  }
  const start = (t.arcStartDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const end = (t.arcEndDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const step = chars.length > 1 ? (end - start) / (chars.length - 1) : 0;

  let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  chars.forEach((_, i) => {
    const a = start + i * step;
    const x = t.arcCx + Math.cos(a) * t.arcRadius;
    const y = t.arcCy + Math.sin(a) * t.arcRadius;
    if (x < minX) minX = x;
    if (y < minY) minY = y;
    if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
    if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
  });
  const pad = t.fontSize;
  return {
    x: minX - pad, y: minY - pad,
    width: (maxX - minX) + pad * 2,
    height: (maxY - minY) + pad * 2,
  };
}

export const StampStraightTextView: React.FC<Props> = ({

    onClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
    onTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },

  };

    <Group
      id={text.id}
      name="text"

      y={text.y}
      draggable={draggable}

      {...handlers}
    >
      {/* Р¤РѕРЅ-РїР»Р°С€РєР° РґР»СЏ hit-РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё: С‚РѕРЅРєРёР№ РїРѕР»СѓРїСЂРѕР·СЂР°С‡РЅС‹Р№, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ РјРµС€Р°Р»,

      <Text
        x={0} y={0}
        width={text.width}
        height={text.fontSize * 1.4}
        text={text.text}
        fontSize={text.fontSize}
        fontFamily={text.fontFamily}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle(text.fontStyle)}
        textDecoration={text.textDecoration}
        fill={text.fill}
        align={text.align}
        listening={true}
        hitStrokeWidth={0}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};

FILE: components\stamps\useStampHistory.ts
SIZE: 1721 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem } from '../../types';

export interface StampSnapshot {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
}

const MAX_HISTORY = 60;

export function useStampHistory(initial: StampSnapshot) {
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState<StampSnapshot>(initial);
  const historyRef = useRef<StampSnapshot[]>([initial]);
  const indexRef = useRef(0);
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0);

  const push = useCallback((next: StampSnapshot) => {
    const trimmed = historyRef.current.slice(0, indexRef.current + 1);
    trimmed.push(next);
    while (trimmed.length > MAX_HISTORY) trimmed.shift();
    historyRef.current = trimmed;
    indexRef.current = trimmed.length - 1;
    setCurrent(next);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  }, []);

  /** РћР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ Р±РµР· Р·Р°РїРёСЃРё РІ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, drag). */
  const replace = useCallback((next: StampSnapshot) => {
    setCurrent(next);
  }, []);

  const undo = useCallback(() => {
    if (indexRef.current <= 0) return;
    indexRef.current -= 1;
    setCurrent(historyRef.current[indexRef.current]);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  }, []);

  const redo = useCallback(() => {
    if (indexRef.current >= historyRef.current.length - 1) return;
    indexRef.current += 1;
    setCurrent(historyRef.current[indexRef.current]);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  }, []);

  return {
    state: current,
    push,
    replace,
    undo,
    redo,
    canUndo: indexRef.current > 0,
    canRedo: indexRef.current < historyRef.current.length - 1,
    version,
  };
}

FILE: ContextMenu.tsx
SIZE: 1565 bytes

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export interface ContextAction {
  label: string;
  shortcut?: string;
  action: () => void;
  danger?: boolean;
}

interface Props {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  actions: ContextAction[];
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const ContextMenu: React.FC<Props> = ({ x, y, actions, onClose }) => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    const onEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose(); };
    setTimeout(() => {
      document.addEventListener('mousedown', onClick);
      document.addEventListener('keydown', onEsc);
    }, 0);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onClick);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onEsc);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  const menuW = 240;
  const menuH = actions.length * 32 + 8;
  const left = Math.min(x, window.innerWidth - menuW - 8);
  const top = Math.min(y, window.innerHeight - menuH - 8);

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="context-menu" style={{ left, top }}>
      {actions.map((a, i) => (
        <div
          key={i}
          className={`context-menu-item ${a.danger ? 'danger' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => { a.action(); onClose(); }}
        >
          <span>{a.label}</span>
          {a.shortcut && <span className="shortcut">{a.shortcut}</span>}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { DataRoot, DataField, DataFieldType } from './types';

const uid = () => Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);

const FIELD_TYPES: { value: DataFieldType; label: string; icon: string }[] = [
  { value: 'string',  label: 'РўРµРєСЃС‚',   icon: 'T' },
  { value: 'number',  label: 'Р§РёСЃР»Рѕ',   icon: '#' },
  { value: 'date',    label: 'Р”Р°С‚Р°',    icon: 'рџ“…' },
  { value: 'boolean', label: 'Р”Р°/РќРµС‚',  icon: 'вњ“' },

export const newField = (name = 'newField', type: DataFieldType = 'string'): DataField => ({
  id: uid(),
  name,
  type,
  sample: type === 'string' ? 'РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ'
    : type === 'number' ? '0'
    : type === 'date' ? '01.01.2026'
    : type === 'boolean' ? 'true'
    : type === 'image' ? 'https://picsum.photos/200'
    : '',
  children: (type === 'object' || type === 'array') ? [] : undefined,
  sampleCount: type === 'array' ? 3 : undefined,
});

export const EXAMPLE_DATA: DataRoot[] = [

interface Props {
  data: DataRoot[];
  setData: (d: DataRoot[]) => void;
}

export const DataPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ data, setData }) => {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const toggle = (id: string) => setExpanded((e) => ({ ...e, [id]: !e[id] }));

  const updateField = (fieldId: string, patch: Partial<DataField>) => {
    const walk = (fields: DataField[]): DataField[] =>
      fields.map((f) => f.id === fieldId
        ? { ...f, ...patch }
        : { ...f, children: f.children ? walk(f.children) : undefined });
    setData(data.map((r) => ({ ...r, fields: walk(r.fields) })));
  };

  const removeField = (fieldId: string) => {
    const walk = (fields: DataField[]): DataField[] =>
      fields.filter((f) => f.id !== fieldId)
        .map((f) => ({ ...f, children: f.children ? walk(f.children) : undefined }));
    setData(data.map((r) => ({ ...r, fields: walk(r.fields) })));
  };

  const addChild = (parentId: string) => {
    const walk = (fields: DataField[]): DataField[] =>
      fields.map((f) => {
        if (f.id === parentId) {
          const c = newField('field', 'string');
          return { ...f, children: [...(f.children || []), c] };
        }
        return { ...f, children: f.children ? walk(f.children) : undefined };
      });
    setData(data.map((r) => ({ ...r, fields: walk(r.fields) })));
  };

  const addRoot = () => {
    const n = data.length + 1;
    setData([...data, { id: uid(), name: 'object' + n, fields: [] }]);
  };
  const updateRootName = (rootId: string, name: string) =>
    setData(data.map((r) => r.id === rootId ? { ...r, name } : r));
  const removeRoot = (rootId: string) => setData(data.filter((r) => r.id !== rootId));
  const addRootField = (rootId: string) =>
    setData(data.map((r) => r.id === rootId
      ? { ...r, fields: [...r.fields, newField('field', 'string')] } : r));

  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, payload: any) => {
    e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', JSON.stringify(payload));
    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy';
  };

  const download = (content: string, name: string, mime: string) => {
    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mime });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url; a.download = name; a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const exportData = () => {
    download(JSON.stringify({ version: '1.0', data }, null, 2), 'data-schema.json', 'application/json');
  };

  const normalizeField = (f: any): DataField => {
    const type = (f.type || 'string') as DataFieldType;
    const norm: DataField = {
      id: f.id || uid(),
      name: f.name || 'field',
      type,
      sample: f.sample ?? (type === 'image' ? 'https://picsum.photos/200' : ''),
      sampleCount: type === 'array' ? (f.sampleCount ?? 3) : undefined,
    };
    if ((type === 'object' || type === 'array') && Array.isArray(f.children)) {
      norm.children = f.children.map(normalizeField);
    } else if (type === 'object' || type === 'array') {
      norm.children = [];
    }
    return norm;
  };

  const importData = () => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file'; input.accept = '.json,application/json';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0]; if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(reader.result as string);
          let imported: DataRoot[];
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) imported = parsed;
          else if (parsed && Array.isArray(parsed.data)) imported = parsed.data;
          else throw new Error('РћР¶РёРґР°РµС‚СЃСЏ { data: [...] } РёР»Рё РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ');
          imported = imported.map((r: any) => ({
            id: r.id || uid(),
            name: r.name || 'object',
            fields: (r.fields || []).map(normalizeField),
          }));
          setData(imported);
        } catch (e: any) { alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚Р°: ' + (e?.message || e)); }
      };
      reader.readAsText(file);
    };
    input.click();
  };

  const loadExample = () => {
    if (data.length && !confirm('Р—Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ С‚РµРєСѓС‰СѓСЋ СЃС…РµРјСѓ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РїСЂРёРјРµСЂРѕРј?')) return;
    setData(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(EXAMPLE_DATA)));
  };

  const renderField = (f: DataField, parentPath: string, depth: number): React.ReactNode => {
    const isArray = f.type === 'array';
    const isObject = f.type === 'object';
    const isContainer = isArray || isObject;
    const ownPath = parentPath ? `${parentPath}.${f.name}` : f.name;
    const isLeaf = !isContainer;
    const isExp = expanded[f.id] !== false;

    return (
      <div key={f.id} className="df-node" style={{ paddingLeft: depth * 10 }}>
        <div className="df-row">
          {isContainer ? (
            <button className="df-toggle" onClick={() => toggle(f.id)}>{isExp ? 'в–ѕ' : 'в–ё'}</button>
          ) : <span className="df-toggle" />}

          <input className="df-name" value={f.name}
            onChange={(e) => updateField(f.id, { name: e.target.value })} />
          <select className="df-type" value={f.type}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const t = e.target.value as DataFieldType;
              const patch: Partial<DataField> = { type: t };
              if ((t === 'object' || t === 'array') && !f.children) patch.children = [];
              if (t === 'array') patch.sampleCount = f.sampleCount ?? 3;
              if (t === 'image' && !f.sample) patch.sample = 'https://picsum.photos/200';
              updateField(f.id, patch);
            }}>
            {FIELD_TYPES.map((t) => <option key={t.value} value={t.value}>{t.label}</option>)}
          </select>
          <button className="df-remove" onClick={() => removeField(f.id)} title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ">Г—</button>
        </div>

        {!isContainer && (
          <div className="df-row df-row-2">
            <span className="df-path" title={ownPath}>{ownPath}</span>
            <input className="df-sample" value={f.sample}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(f.id, { sample: e.target.value })} placeholder="РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ" />
          </div>
        )}

        {isArray && (

          <div className="df-chip" draggable

              + {isArray ? 'Р°С‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІР°' : 'РїРѕР»Рµ'}

            <p>РќР°С‡РЅРёС‚Рµ СЃ РєРЅРѕРїРєРё <b>рџ“‹ РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ</b> вЂ” Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЃСЏ С‚РµР°С‚СЂР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ Р±РёР»РµС‚.</p>

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement, DashType } from './types';

interface Props {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  element: CanvasElement;
  onUpdate: (patch: any) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onBringForward: () => void;
  onSendBackward: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

const DASH_OPTIONS: { id: DashType; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'solid', label: 'вЂ”' },
  { id: 'dashed', label: '--' },
  { id: 'dotted', label: '..' },
  { id: 'dashdot', label: '-.-' },
  { id: 'longdash', label: 'вЂ”вЂ”' },
];

export const FloatingProps: React.FC<Props> = ({
  x, y, element, onUpdate, onClose,
  onBringForward, onSendBackward, onDelete,
}) => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const down = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener('mousedown', down), 0);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', down);
  }, [onClose]);

  const w = 400;
  const left = Math.min(Math.max(8, x - w / 2), window.innerWidth - w - 8);
  const top = Math.min(Math.max(8, y - 70), window.innerHeight - 80);

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="floating-props" style={{ left, top }}>
      <div className="fp-group">
        <button title="Р’РїРµСЂС‘Рґ РЅР° СЃР»РѕР№" onClick={onBringForward}>в¬†</button>
        <button title="РќР°Р·Р°Рґ РЅР° СЃР»РѕР№" onClick={onSendBackward}>в¬‡</button>
        <button title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ" onClick={onDelete}>рџ—‘</button>
      </div>

      {element.type === 'shape' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label className="fp-label">Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</label>
            <input type="color"
              value={element.fill === 'transparent' ? '#ffffff' : element.fill}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })} />
            <button type="button" title="РџСЂРѕР·СЂР°С‡РЅР°СЏ" style={{ padding: '2px 6px', fontSize: 12 }}
              onClick={() => onUpdate({ fill: 'transparent' })}>в€…</button>
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label className="fp-label">РћР±РІРѕРґРєР°</label>
            <input type="color" value={element.stroke}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ stroke: e.target.value })} />
            <input type="number" min="0" max="20" value={element.strokeWidth}
              style={{ width: 50 }}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} />
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {element.type === 'stamp' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label className="fp-label">Р¦РІРµС‚</label>
            <input type="color" value={element.stampColor}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ stampColor: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label className="fp-label">Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</label>
            <input type="number" min="0" max="20" value={element.strokeWidth}
              style={{ width: 50 }}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} />
            <select value={element.strokeDash}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ strokeDash: e.target.value as DashType })}>
              {DASH_OPTIONS.map((d) => <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.label}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {element.type === 'text' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label className="fp-label">Р¦РІРµС‚</label>
            <input type="color" value={element.fill}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label className="fp-label">Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ</label>
            <input type="number" min="8" max="120" value={element.fontSize}
              style={{ width: 55 }}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fontSize: +e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label className="fp-label">РЁСЂРёС„С‚</label>
            <select value={element.fontFamily}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fontFamily: e.target.value })}>
              <option value="Arial">Arial</option>
              <option value="Times New Roman">Times</option>
              <option value="Courier New">Courier</option>
              <option value="Georgia">Georgia</option>
              <option value="Verdana">Verdana</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {element.type === 'line' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label className="fp-label">Р¦РІРµС‚</label>

FILE: hooks\inlineEditor\position.ts

import type { CanvasElement, Padding, StampElement } from '../../types';
import type { OverlayGeometry, Zone } from './types';

export interface StageOffset {
  offsetX: number;
  offsetY: number;
}

/**
 * РђР±СЃРѕР»СЋС‚РЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚С‹ stage РІ viewport.
 * Overlay СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂРёС‚СЃСЏ СЃ position: fixed вЂ” Р·РЅР°С‡РёС‚ РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚С‹ РѕС‚ viewport.
 */
export function getStageOffset(
  wrap: HTMLDivElement,
  stage: { container: () => HTMLDivElement },
): StageOffset {
  const stageRect = stage.container().getBoundingClientRect();
  return {
    offsetX: stageRect.left,
    offsetY: stageRect.top,
  };
}

const EMPTY_PAD: Padding = { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };

export function calcElementGeometry(
  el: CanvasElement,
  offset: StageOffset,
  zoom: number,
  contentHeight: number,
  kind: 'text' | 'shape' | 'footer' | 'stamp',
  zone?: Zone,
): OverlayGeometry {
  /* Р¤РёРіСѓСЂР°: text-area РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РЅРµС‘ */
  if (kind === 'shape' && el.type === 'shape') {
    return calcShapeTextArea(el, offset, zoom);
  }

  /* Stamp: РѕРІРµСЂР»РµР№ РїРѕ Р·РѕРЅРµ (top/center/bottom) */
  if (kind === 'stamp' && el.type === 'stamp') {
    return calcStampTextArea(el, zone || 'center', offset, zoom);
  }

  /* Text / Footer: РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚С‹ text-area РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р° */
  const pad = ('padding' in el && el.padding) ? el.padding : EMPTY_PAD;
  const innerW = Math.max(1, el.width - pad.left - pad.right);
  const innerH = Math.max(1, contentHeight - pad.top - pad.bottom);

  return {
    x: offset.offsetX + (el.x + pad.left) * zoom,
    y: offset.offsetY + (el.y + pad.top) * zoom,
    width: innerW * zoom,
    height: innerH * zoom,
  };
}

/** Text-area РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ вЂ” СЃРѕРІРїР°РґР°РµС‚ СЃ ShapeEl.tsx. */
function calcShapeTextArea(
  el: Extract<CanvasElement, { type: 'shape' }>,
  offset: StageOffset,
  zoom: number,
): OverlayGeometry {
  const w = el.width;
  const h = el.height;
  const pad = el.textPadding || { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 };

  let tx = 0, ty = 0, tw = w, th = h;

  if (el.shape === 'circle') {
    const d = Math.min(w, h);
    tw = d * 0.7; th = d * 0.7;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = (h - th) / 2;
  } else if (el.shape === 'triangle') {
    tw = w * 0.55; th = h * 0.45;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = h * 0.5;
  } else if (el.shape === 'star') {
    tw = w * 0.5; th = h * 0.5;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = (h - th) / 2;
  }

  tx += pad.left;
  ty += pad.top;
  tw = Math.max(1, tw - pad.left - pad.right);
  th = Math.max(1, th - pad.top - pad.bottom);

  return {
    x: offset.offsetX + (el.x + tx) * zoom,
    y: offset.offsetY + (el.y + ty) * zoom,
    width: Math.max(1, tw * zoom),
    height: Math.max(1, th * zoom),
  };
}

/**
 * Text-area РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё stamp.
 *  вЂў top: РїРѕР»РѕСЃР° РІ РІРµСЂС…РЅРµР№ С‡Р°СЃС‚Рё (С‚Р°Рј, РіРґРµ РёРґС‘С‚ РІРµСЂС…РЅСЏСЏ РґСѓРіР°)
 *  вЂў center: С†РµРЅС‚СЂ stamp
 *  вЂў bottom: РїРѕР»РѕСЃР° РІ РЅРёР¶РЅРµР№ С‡Р°СЃС‚Рё
 * Р РµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїСЂРѕРїРѕСЂС†РёРё СЃРѕРІРїР°РґР°СЋС‚ СЃ РѕС‚СЂРёСЃРѕРІРєРѕР№ РІ Stamp.tsx.
 */
function calcStampTextArea(
  el: StampElement,
  zone: Zone,
  offset: StageOffset,
  zoom: number,
): OverlayGeometry {
  const w = el.width;
  const h = el.height;

  let tx = 0, ty = 0, tw = w, th = 40;

  if (el.shape === 'circle' || el.shape === 'oval') {
    /* Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅСЏСЏ Р·РѕРЅР°, РЅРµ РІС‹С…РѕРґСЏС‰Р°СЏ Р·Р° РєРѕРЅС‚СѓСЂ */
    const innerD = Math.min(w, h) * 0.65;

    if (zone === 'top') {
      tw = innerD; th = 32;
      tx = (w - tw) / 2;
      ty = (h - innerD) / 2 + 4;
    } else if (zone === 'bottom') {
      tw = innerD; th = 32;
      tx = (w - tw) / 2;
      ty = (h + innerD) / 2 - th - 4;
    } else {
      tw = innerD; th = 40;
      tx = (w - tw) / 2;
      ty = h / 2 - th / 2;
    }
  } else if (el.shape === 'rect') {
    if (zone === 'top') {
      tw = w - 24; th = 30;
      tx = 12; ty = 12;
    } else if (zone === 'bottom') {
      tw = w - 24; th = 30;
      tx = 12; ty = h - 42;
    } else {
      tw = w - 24; th = 40;
      tx = 12; ty = h / 2 - 20;
    }
  } else {
    /* triangle */
    const innerD = Math.min(w, h) * 0.5;

    if (zone === 'top') {
      tw = innerD; th = 26;
      tx = (w - tw) / 2;
      ty = h * 0.35;
    } else if (zone === 'bottom') {
      tw = innerD; th = 26;
      tx = (w - tw) / 2;
      ty = h * 0.72;
    } else {
      tw = innerD; th = 40;
      tx = (w - tw) / 2;
      ty = h / 2 - 20;
    }
  }

  return {
    x: offset.offsetX + (el.x + tx) * zoom,
    y: offset.offsetY + (el.y + ty) * zoom,
    width: Math.max(30, tw * zoom),
    height: Math.max(20, th * zoom),
  };
}

export function calcCellGeometry(
  el: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number; cols: number; rows: number },
  offset: StageOffset,
  zoom: number,
  cell: { rowIndex: number; colIndex: number },
): OverlayGeometry {
  const cw = el.width / el.cols;
  const ch = el.height / el.rows;
  return {
    x: offset.offsetX + (el.x + cell.colIndex * cw) * zoom,
    y: offset.offsetY + (el.y + cell.rowIndex * ch) * zoom,
    width: Math.max(1, cw * zoom),
    height: Math.max(1, ch * zoom),
  };
}

export type EditField =

FILE: hooks\inlineEditor\useClickOutside.ts

import { useEffect } from 'react';

interface Args {
  enabled: boolean;
  onOutside: () => void;
  ignoreSelectors: string[];
}

/**
 * Р—Р°РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ РїСЂРё РєР»РёРєРµ РІРЅРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅРЅС‹С… СЃРµР»РµРєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ.
 *
 * РћСЃРѕР±РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё:
 *   вЂў РЎР»СѓС€Р°РµРј 'mousedown' РЅР° С„Р°Р·Рµ capture вЂ” СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹С….
 *   вЂў РќР• РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµРј onOutside СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ, Р° С‡РµСЂРµР· setTimeout(0), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РґР°С‚СЊ
 *     TipTap-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂСѓ С€Р°РЅСЃ РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°С‚СЊ blur Рё Р·Р°С„РёРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ С€РёСЂРёРЅС‹
 *     РєРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє С‡РµСЂРµР· С‚СЂР°РЅР·Р°РєС†РёСЋ.
 */
export function useClickOutside({ enabled, onOutside, ignoreSelectors }: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as Node | null;
      if (!target) return;

      const inside = ignoreSelectors.some((sel) => {
        const el = document.querySelector(sel);
        return el?.contains(target);
      });
      if (inside) return;

      // РћС‚РєР»Р°РґС‹РІР°РµРј, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ TipTap.onBlur СѓСЃРїРµР» РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ columnResizing
      setTimeout(() => onOutside(), 0);
    };

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler, true);
    }, 0);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler, true);
    };
  }, [enabled, onOutside, ignoreSelectors]);
}

import { useEffect } from 'react';

interface Args {
  /** Ref РЅР° РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂ С…РѕР»СЃС‚Р° */
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  /** Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃР»Р°Р№РґР° */
  slideWidth: number;
  slideHeight: number;
  /** РўРµРєСѓС‰Р°СЏ С„СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєРё Р·СѓРјР° */
  setZoom: (z: number | ((prev: number) => number)) => void;
  /** РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРїС‹ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё wrap (СЃРѕРІРїР°РґР°СЋС‚ СЃ padding РІ CSS) */
  padding?: number;
  /** Р РµР·РµСЂРІ РїРѕРґ sticky zoom-bar */
  topReserve?: number;
  /** РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ Рё РјР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ Р·СѓРј */
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
  /** РЎСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ Р»Рё Р°РІС‚Рѕ-С„РёС‚ РїСЂРё resize РѕРєРЅР° */
  refitOnResize?: boolean;
}

/**
 * РџРѕРґРіРѕРЅСЏРµС‚ zoom С‚Р°Рє, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СЃР»Р°Р№Рґ С†РµР»РёРєРѕРј РІР»РµР·Р°Р» РІ РІРёРґРёРјСѓСЋ РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚СЊ С…РѕР»СЃС‚Р°.
 * Р Р°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚:
 *  - РЅР° РјР°СѓРЅС‚
 *  - РїСЂРё СЃРјРµРЅРµ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂР°/РѕСЂРёРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёРё СЃР»Р°Р№РґР°
 *  - РїСЂРё resize РѕРєРЅР° (РµСЃР»Рё refitOnResize = true)
 */
export function useAutoFitZoom({
  wrapRef,
  slideWidth,
  slideHeight,
  setZoom,
  padding = 40,
  topReserve = 60,
  minZoom = 0.2,
  maxZoom = 2,
  refitOnResize = true,
}: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    if (!wrap || !slideWidth || !slideHeight) return;

    const fit = () => {
      const rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
      const availableW = Math.max(100, rect.width - padding * 2);
      const availableH = Math.max(100, rect.height - padding * 2 - topReserve);

      const zoomX = availableW / slideWidth;
      const zoomY = availableH / slideHeight;
      const next = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, Math.min(zoomX, zoomY)));

      setZoom(+next.toFixed(3));
    };

    // РџРµСЂРІС‹Р№ СЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚ вЂ” СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ
    fit();

    if (!refitOnResize) return;

    // РќР° resize вЂ” СЃ РЅРµР±РѕР»СЊС€РѕР№ Р·Р°РґРµСЂР¶РєРѕР№, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ РґС‘СЂРіР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ
    let raf = 0;
    const onResize = () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(fit);
    };

    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
    };
  }, [wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom, padding, topReserve, minZoom, maxZoom, refitOnResize]);
}

FILE: hooks\useCanvasWheelZoom.ts
SIZE: 5300 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';

interface Args {
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  zoom: number;
  setZoom: (z: number | ((prev: number) => number)) => void;
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
}

const STEP = 0.05;

/**
 * Ctrl+Wheel РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё wrapRef в†’ Р·СѓРј С…РѕР»СЃС‚Р° СЃ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµРј С‚РѕС‡РєРё РїРѕРґ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂРѕРј.
 *
 * РћСЃРѕР±РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё:
 *  вЂў РЎР»СѓС€Р°РµРј РЅР° С„Р°Р·Рµ capture, passive:false вЂ” РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРµ СѓСЃР»РѕРІРёРµ
 *    РґР»СЏ preventDefault Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂРЅРѕРіРѕ Р·СѓРјР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†С‹.
 *  вЂў РЎРєСЂРѕР»Р» РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј РЎРРќРҐР РћРќРќРћ РІ useLayoutEffect вЂ” РґРѕ paint.
 *    РўРѕРіРґР° Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ РЅРµ СѓСЃРїРµРІР°РµС‚ СЃР°Рј В«РїРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚СЊВ» scrollTop РїРѕРґ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ.
 *  вЂў Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј С‡РµСЂРµР· rAF вЂ” РЅР° СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№, РµСЃР»Рё Konva РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚
 *    СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ canvas РЅРµ РІ С‚РѕРј Р¶Рµ commit-Рµ, С‡С‚Рѕ React state.
 *  вЂў РћРєСЂСѓРіР»СЏРµРј scrollLeft/scrollTop РґРѕ С†РµР»РѕРіРѕ вЂ” СѓСЃС‚СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ sub-pixel РґСЂРµР±РµР·Рі.
 *  вЂў overflow-anchor: none (РІ CSS) + scroll-behavior: auto.
 */
export function useCanvasWheelZoom({
  wrapRef,
  zoom,
  setZoom,
  minZoom = 0.2,
  maxZoom = 2,
}: Args) {
  const zoomRef = useRef(zoom);
  zoomRef.current = zoom;

  /** РљСѓРґР° РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СЃРєСЂРѕР»Р» РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РѕС‡РµСЂРµРґРЅРѕРіРѕ Р·СѓРјР°. */
  const pendingScrollRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  /** РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµС‚ pendingScrollRef Рє СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Сѓ. Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ true, РµСЃР»Рё С‡С‚Рѕ-С‚Рѕ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРёР»Рё. */
  const applyPendingScroll = useCallback(() => {
    const el = wrapRef.current;
    const pending = pendingScrollRef.current;
    if (!el || !pending) return false;

    const maxX = Math.max(0, el.scrollWidth - el.clientWidth);
    const maxY = Math.max(0, el.scrollHeight - el.clientHeight);
    const fx = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(pending.x, maxX)));
    const fy = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(pending.y, maxY)));

    /* РџРёРЅРіСѓРµРј scroll-behavior РЅР° РІСЂРµРјСЏ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ */
    const prev = el.style.scrollBehavior;
    el.style.scrollBehavior = 'auto';

    if (el.scrollLeft !== fx) el.scrollLeft = fx;
    if (el.scrollTop !== fy) el.scrollTop = fy;

    el.style.scrollBehavior = prev;
    return true;
  }, [wrapRef]);

  /* вњ… 1. РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј СЃРєСЂРѕР»Р» РЎР РђР—РЈ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РєРѕРјРјРёС‚Р° вЂ” РґРѕ paint.
        Р­С‚Рѕ РєР»СЋС‡РµРІРѕР№ С„РёРєСЃ РѕС‚ РїРѕРґС‘СЂРіРёРІР°РЅРёР№. */
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    applyPendingScroll();
  }, [zoom, applyPendingScroll]);

  /* вњ… 2. Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј РµС‰С‘ СЂР°Р· РІ СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРј РєР°РґСЂРµ вЂ” РЅР° СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№,
        РµСЃР»Рё Konva РїРµСЂРµСЂРёСЃСѓРµС‚ canvas СЃ РЅРѕРІС‹Рј СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂРѕРј РїРѕР·Р¶Рµ, С‡РµРј React
        РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёР» state. Рљ СЌС‚РѕРјСѓ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚Сѓ scrollHeight СѓР¶Рµ РѕРєРѕРЅС‡Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№. */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pendingScrollRef.current) return;
    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      applyPendingScroll();
      /* РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј pending С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ С‚РѕРіРѕ, РєР°Рє РІСЃС‘ СЃС‚Р°Р±РёР»РёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ */
      pendingScrollRef.current = null;
    });
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [zoom, applyPendingScroll]);

  /* ---------- Wheel handler ---------- */
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = wrapRef.current;
    if (!el) return;

    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      if (!e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();

      const oldZoom = zoomRef.current;
      const direction = e.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1;
      const next = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, +(oldZoom + direction * STEP).toFixed(3)));
      if (next === oldZoom) return;

      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const mx = e.clientX - rect.left;
      const my = e.clientY - rect.top;

      const sx = el.scrollLeft;
      const sy = el.scrollTop;

      /* РўРѕС‡РєР° РєРѕРЅС‚РµРЅС‚Р° РїРѕРґ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂРѕРј РІ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµР№ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРµ */
      const contentX = sx + mx;
      const contentY = sy + my;
      const ratio = next / oldZoom;

      /* вњ… РћРєСЂСѓРіР»СЏРµРј С†РµР»РµРІС‹Рµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ вЂ” СѓСЃС‚СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ sub-pixel РґСЂРѕР¶Р°РЅРёРµ */
      pendingScrollRef.current = {
        x: Math.round(contentX * ratio - mx),
        y: Math.round(contentY * ratio - my),
      };

      setZoom(next);
    };

    const onGesture = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
    };

    el.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false, capture: true });
    el.addEventListener('gesturestart', onGesture, { passive: false } as any);
    el.addEventListener('gesturechange', onGesture, { passive: false } as any);

    return () => {
      el.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { capture: true } as any);
      el.removeEventListener('gesturestart', onGesture as any);
      el.removeEventListener('gesturechange', onGesture as any);
    };
  }, [wrapRef, setZoom, minZoom, maxZoom]);
}

FILE: hooks\useDataModel.ts

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { DataRoot, Slide } from '../types';
import { resolveSample } from '../lib/resolveSample';

export function useDataModel(
  slides: Slide[],
  setSlides: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Slide[]>>,
) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<DataRoot[]>([]);
  const initialised = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!data.length) return;
    setSlides((prev) => {
      let anyChanged = false;
      const next = prev.map((s) => {
        let changed = false;
        const elements = s.elements.map((el) => {
          if (el.type === 'placeholder' && el.fieldPath) {
            const resolved = resolveSample(data, el.fieldPath);
            if (resolved !== null && resolved !== el.sample) {
              changed = true;
              return { ...el, sample: resolved };
            }
          }
          return el;
        });
        if (!changed) return s;
        anyChanged = true;
        return { ...s, elements };
      });
      return anyChanged ? next : prev;
    });
  }, [data, setSlides]);

  useEffect(() => { initialised.current = true; }, []);

  return { data, setData };
}

FILE: hooks\useDropHandler.ts
SIZE: 3886 bytes

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type { EditorApi } from './useEditor';
import type { CanvasElement, DataFieldType } from '../types';
import { findElementAt } from '../lib/geometry';
import {
  newText, newSimpleText, newFooter, newTable, newLine, newShape, newStamp,
  makePlaceholder, makeArrayTable,
} from '../lib/factories';

export function useDropHandler(editor: EditorApi) {
  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);

  const onDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
    setDragOver(true);
  }, []);

  const onDragLeave = useCallback(() => setDragOver(false), []);

  const onDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setDragOver(false);

    const raw = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/json');
    if (!raw) return;

    try {
      const payload = JSON.parse(raw);
      const stage = editor.stageRef.current;
      if (!stage) return;

      const rect = stage.container().getBoundingClientRect();
      const zoom = editor.zoom;
      const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) / zoom;
      const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) / zoom;

      /* ---------- РђС‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚ РёР· РїР°РЅРµР»Рё РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… ---------- */
      if (payload.kind === 'array') {
        editor.addElementWithHistory(makeArrayTable(x, y, payload));
        return;
      }

      if (payload.kind === 'field') {
        const target = findElementAt(editor.activeSlide, x, y);
        if (target && target.type === 'text') {
          const path = `{{${payload.path}}}`;
          const oldText = target.text || '';
          const sep = oldText.length && !/[\s\n]$/.test(oldText) ? ' ' : '';
          editor.updateWithHistory(target.id, { text: oldText + sep + path });
          editor.setSelectedIds([target.id]);
          return;
        }
        editor.addElementWithHistory(
          makePlaceholder(x, y, payload.path, payload.fieldType as DataFieldType, payload.sample),
        );
        return;
      }

      /* ---------- РќРѕРІС‹Р№ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ СЃ Р»РµРІРѕР№ РїР°РЅРµР»Рё ---------- */
      if (payload.kind === 'new-element') {
        const t = payload.elementType as string;
        const cx = Math.max(0, x - 100);
        const cy = Math.max(0, y - 60);
        let el: CanvasElement | null = null;

        if (t === 'text') el = newText(cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'simple-text') el = newSimpleText(cx, cy);

        else if (t === 'footer') {
          const slide = editor.activeSlide;
          const f = newFooter(slide);
          const minY = slide.padding.top;
          const maxY = slide.height - slide.padding.bottom - f.height;
          f.y = Math.min(maxY, Math.max(minY, y));
          el = f;
        }

        else if (t === 'table') el = newTable(cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'line') el = newLine(cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'line-arrow') { const l = newLine(cx, cy); l.isArrow = true; el = l; }
        else if (t === 'rect') el = newShape('rect', cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'roundRect') el = newShape('roundRect', cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'circle') el = newShape('circle', cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'triangle') el = newShape('triangle', cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'star') el = newShape('star', cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'stamp-circle') el = newStamp('circle', cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'stamp-oval') el = newStamp('oval', cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'stamp-rect') el = newStamp('rect', cx, cy);
        else if (t === 'stamp-triangle') el = newStamp('triangle', cx, cy);

        if (el) editor.addElementWithHistory(el);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Drop parse error', err);
    }
  }, [editor]);

  return { dragOver, onDragOver, onDragLeave, onDrop };
}

import { useState, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement, LineElement } from '../types';
import { useSlides } from './useSlides';
import { useSelection } from './useSelection';
import { useHistory } from './useHistory';
import { useDataModel } from './useDataModel';
import { useInlineEditor } from './useInlineEditor';
import { useKeyboardShortcuts } from './useKeyboardShortcuts';
import { useGroupDrag } from './useGroupDrag';
import { useRubberBand } from './useRubberBand';
import { useFillMode } from './useFillMode';
import { useFileIO } from './useFileIO';
import { useCanvasWheelZoom } from './useCanvasWheelZoom';
import { computeSnap } from '../services/snap';
import { uid } from '../lib/uid';
import Konva from 'konva';

/**
 * РљРѕСЌС„С„РёС†РёРµРЅС‚ В«РІРёР·СѓР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ СѓРІРµР»РёС‡РµРЅРёСЏВ» РґР»СЏ РєРЅРёР¶РЅРѕР№ РѕСЂРёРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёРё.
 * РџСЂРё displayZoom = 100% РІ РїРѕСЂС‚СЂРµС‚Рµ С…РѕР»СЃС‚ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂРёС‚СЃСЏ РІ 1.65Г— вЂ” РІРёР·СѓР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ
 * СЌС‚Рѕ СЃРѕРѕС‚РІРµС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ 165% Р·СѓРјСѓ, РЅРѕ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЋ РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ В«100%В».
 */
const PORTRAIT_VISUAL_FACTOR = 1.65;

export function useEditor() {
  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);
  const layerRef = useRef<Konva.Layer>(null);

  const slidesApi = useSlides();
  const selection = useSelection(slidesApi.activeSlide);
  const history = useHistory();
  const dataApi = useDataModel(slidesApi.slides, slidesApi.setSlides);

  /* вњ… displayZoom вЂ” С‚Рѕ, С‡С‚Рѕ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ РІРёРґРёС‚ РІ zoom-bar Рё РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚.
     zoom вЂ” СЌС„С„РµРєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№, РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РІРµР·РґРµ РґР»СЏ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂР°.
     Р Р°Р·РЅРёС†Р° = РєРѕСЌС„С„РёС†РёРµРЅС‚ РѕСЂРёРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёРё. */
  const [displayZoom, setDisplayZoom] = useState(1);

  const orientationFactor =
    slidesApi.activeSlide.orientation === 'portrait' ? PORTRAIT_VISUAL_FACTOR : 1;

  const zoom = displayZoom * orientationFactor;

  const [showGrid, setShowGrid] = useState(true);
  const [showMargins, setShowMargins] = useState(true);
  const [guides, setGuides] = useState<{ v: number[]; h: number[] }>({ v: [], h: [] });
  const [copiedStyle, setCopiedStyle] = useState<Partial<CanvasElement> | null>(null);
  const [clipboard, setClipboard] = useState<CanvasElement[]>([]);
  const [floatingPos, setFloatingPos] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const [contextMenu, setContextMenu] = useState<{ x: number; y: number; elementId: string | null } | null>(null);

  /* ---------- Inline editor ---------- */
  const inlineEditor = useInlineEditor({
    wrapRef: wrapRef as any,
    stageRef: stageRef as any,
    zoom,
    activeSlide: slidesApi.activeSlide,
    updateElement: slidesApi.updateElement,
    onBeforeOpen: () => setFloatingPos(null),
  });

  /* ---------- Ctrl + Wheel в†’ Р·СѓРј С…РѕР»СЃС‚Р° СЃ С„РѕРєСѓСЃРѕРј РЅР° РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂРµ ---------- */
useCanvasWheelZoom({
  wrapRef: wrapRef as any,
  zoom: displayZoom,          /* вњ… РїРµСЂРµРґР°С‘Рј С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ вЂ” С…СѓРє СЃР°Рј РµРіРѕ С…СЂР°РЅРёС‚ */
  setZoom: setDisplayZoom,
  minZoom: 0.2,
  maxZoom: 2,
});

  /* ---------- Snap ---------- */
  const handleSnap = useCallback((e: any, el: CanvasElement) => {
    const node = e.target;
    const evt = e?.evt as MouseEvent | undefined;
    const bypassSnap = !!(evt && (evt.ctrlKey || evt.metaKey));

    if (bypassSnap) {
      setGuides({ v: [], h: [] });
      return;
    }

    const snapped = computeSnap(el, node.x(), node.y(), slidesApi.activeSlide, showGrid);
    node.x(snapped.x);
    node.y(snapped.y);
    setGuides({ v: snapped.v, h: snapped.h });
  }, [slidesApi.activeSlide, showGrid]);

  const groupDrag = useGroupDrag({
    activeSlide: slidesApi.activeSlide,
    selectedIds: selection.selectedIds,
    setSelectedIds: selection.setSelectedIds,
    setSlides: slidesApi.setSlides,
    activeId: slidesApi.activeId,
    handleSnap,
    setFloatingPos,
  });

  const pointerPos = useCallback(() => {
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
    const p = stage.getPointerPosition();
    if (!p) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
    return { x: p.x / zoom, y: p.y / zoom };
  }, [zoom]);

  const rubberBand = useRubberBand({
    stageRef: stageRef as any,
    zoom,
    activeSlide: slidesApi.activeSlide,
    setSelectedIds: selection.setSelectedIds,
  });

  const fillModeApi = useFillMode({
    activeSlide: slidesApi.activeSlide,
    updateElement: slidesApi.updateElement,
    addElement: slidesApi.addElement,
    pointerPos,
    onElementSelected: (id) => selection.select(id),
  });

  /* ================= РћРїРµСЂР°С†РёРё ================= */

  const removeSelected = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selection.selectedIds.length) return;
    const newSlides = slidesApi.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slidesApi.activeId
        ? s
        : { ...s, elements: s.elements.filter((e) => !selection.selectedIds.includes(e.id)) },
    );
    slidesApi.setSlides(newSlides);
    history.push(newSlides, slidesApi.activeId);
    selection.clear();
    setFloatingPos(null);
  }, [selection, slidesApi, history]);

  const updateWithHistory = useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    const newSlides = slidesApi.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slidesApi.activeId
        ? s
        : { ...s, elements: s.elements.map((el) => (el.id === id ? ({ ...el, ...patch } as CanvasElement) : el)) },
    );
    slidesApi.setSlides(newSlides);
    history.push(newSlides, slidesApi.activeId);
  }, [slidesApi, history]);

  const addElementWithHistory = useCallback((el: CanvasElement) => {
    const newSlides = slidesApi.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slidesApi.activeId ? s : { ...s, elements: [...s.elements, el] },
    );
    slidesApi.setSlides(newSlides);
    selection.setSelectedIds([el.id]);
    history.push(newSlides, slidesApi.activeId);
  }, [slidesApi, selection, history]);

  const undo = useCallback(() => {
    const prev = history.undo();
    if (!prev) return;
    slidesApi.setSlides(prev.slides);
    slidesApi.setActiveId(prev.activeId);
    selection.clear();
    setGuides({ v: [], h: [] });
  }, [history, slidesApi, selection]);

  const redo = useCallback(() => {
    const next = history.redo();
    if (!next) return;
    slidesApi.setSlides(next.slides);
    slidesApi.setActiveId(next.activeId);
    selection.clear();
    setGuides({ v: [], h: [] });
  }, [history, slidesApi, selection]);

  /* ================= Clipboard ================= */

  const copy = useCallback(() => {
    const els = slidesApi.activeSlide.elements.filter((el) =>
      selection.selectedIds.includes(el.id),
    );
    if (els.length) setClipboard(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(els)));
  }, [slidesApi.activeSlide, selection.selectedIds]);

  const cut = useCallback(() => {
    const els = slidesApi.activeSlide.elements.filter((el) =>
      selection.selectedIds.includes(el.id),
    );
    if (els.length) {
      setClipboard(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(els)));
      removeSelected();
    }
  }, [slidesApi.activeSlide, selection.selectedIds, removeSelected]);

  const paste = useCallback(() => {
    if (!clipboard.length) return;
    const pasted = clipboard.map((el) => ({
      ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)),
      id: uid(),
      x: el.x + 20,
      y: el.y + 20,
    }));
    const newSlides = slidesApi.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slidesApi.activeId ? s : { ...s, elements: [...s.elements, ...pasted] },
    );
    slidesApi.setSlides(newSlides);
    selection.setSelectedIds(pasted.map((el) => el.id));
    history.push(newSlides, slidesApi.activeId);
  }, [clipboard, slidesApi, selection, history]);

  const duplicate = useCallback(() => {
    const els = slidesApi.activeSlide.elements.filter((el) =>
      selection.selectedIds.includes(el.id),
    );
    if (!els.length) return;
    const pasted = els.map((el) => ({
      ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)),
      id: uid(),
      x: el.x + 20,
      y: el.y + 20,
    }));
    const newSlides = slidesApi.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slidesApi.activeId ? s : { ...s, elements: [...s.elements, ...pasted] },
    );
    slidesApi.setSlides(newSlides);
    selection.setSelectedIds(pasted.map((el) => el.id));
    history.push(newSlides, slidesApi.activeId);
  }, [slidesApi, selection, history]);

  /* ================= Р“РѕСЂСЏС‡РёРµ РєР»Р°РІРёС€Рё ================= */

  useKeyboardShortcuts({
    onUndo: undo,
    onRedo: redo,
    onCopy: copy,
    onPaste: paste,
    onDuplicate: duplicate,
    onDelete: removeSelected,
    onEscape: () => {
      if (fillModeApi.points.length) fillModeApi.cancel();
    },
    onEnter: () => {
      if (fillModeApi.fillMode && fillModeApi.points.length >= 6) fillModeApi.closePolygon();
    },
    disabled: !!inlineEditor.editing,
  });

  /* ================= Line anchors ================= */

  const appendAnchor = useCallback((el: LineElement) => {
    const pts = el.points && el.points.length >= 4 ? el.points : [0, el.height / 2, el.width, el.height / 2];
    const lastX = pts[pts.length - 2];
    const lastY = pts[pts.length - 1];
    const prevX = pts[pts.length - 4];
    const prevY = pts[pts.length - 3];
    const dx = lastX - prevX;
    const dy = lastY - prevY;
    updateWithHistory(el.id, { points: [...pts, lastX + dx, lastY + dy] });
  }, [updateWithHistory]);

  const removeLastAnchor = useCallback((el: LineElement) => {
    const pts = el.points && el.points.length >= 4 ? el.points : [];
    if (pts.length <= 4) return;
    updateWithHistory(el.id, { points: pts.slice(0, pts.length - 2) });
  }, [updateWithHistory]);

  /* ================= РџСЂРѕС‡РµРµ ================= */

  const moveLayer = useCallback((dir: 1 | -1) => {
    if (!selection.selectedId) return;
    slidesApi.moveLayer(selection.selectedId, dir);
  }, [selection.selectedId, slidesApi]);

  const updateSelected = useCallback((patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    if (!selection.selectedId) return;
    updateWithHistory(selection.selectedId, patch);
  }, [selection.selectedId, updateWithHistory]);

  const copyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    const el = selection.selected;
    if (!el) return;
    const { id, x, y, rotation, type, ...style } = el as any;
    setCopiedStyle(style);
  }, [selection.selected]);

  const applyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selection.selectedId || !copiedStyle) return;
    updateWithHistory(selection.selectedId, copiedStyle);
    setCopiedStyle(null);
  }, [selection.selectedId, copiedStyle, updateWithHistory]);

  /* ================= File IO ================= */

  const fileIO = useFileIO({
    slides: slidesApi.slides,
    setSlides: slidesApi.setSlides,
    activeId: slidesApi.activeId,
    setActiveId: slidesApi.setActiveId,
    activeSlide: slidesApi.activeSlide,
    data: dataApi.data,
    setData: dataApi.setData,
    addElementWithHistory,
    onImported: () => {
      selection.clear();
      setGuides({ v: [], h: [] });
      setContextMenu(null);
      setFloatingPos(null);
      inlineEditor.setEditing(null);
      inlineEditor.setShowToolbar(false);
    },
  });

  return {
    wrapRef, stageRef, trRef, layerRef,

    slides: slidesApi.slides,
    activeSlide: slidesApi.activeSlide,
    activeId: slidesApi.activeId,
    setActiveId: slidesApi.setActiveId,
    setSlides: slidesApi.setSlides,
    customSize: slidesApi.customSize,
    changeCustomSize: slidesApi.changeCustomSize,
    changeFormat: slidesApi.changeFormat,
    changeOrientation: slidesApi.changeOrientation,
    updateSlide: slidesApi.updateSlide,
    addSlide: slidesApi.addSlide,
    removeSlide: slidesApi.removeSlide,

    selectedIds: selection.selectedIds,
    selectedId: selection.selectedId,
    selected: selection.selected,
    select: selection.select,
    clearSelection: selection.clear,
    selectMany: selection.selectMany,
    isSelected: selection.isSelected,
    setSelectedIds: selection.setSelectedIds,

    undo, redo,
    canUndo: history.canUndo,
    canRedo: history.canRedo,

    data: dataApi.data,
    setData: dataApi.setData,

    editing: inlineEditor.editing,
    setEditing: inlineEditor.setEditing,
    showTextToolbar: inlineEditor.showToolbar,
    setShowTextToolbar: inlineEditor.setShowToolbar,
    openEditor: inlineEditor.open,
    openCellEditor: inlineEditor.openCell,
    commitEdit: inlineEditor.commit,
    onToolbarChange: inlineEditor.onToolbarChange,

    contextMenu, setContextMenu,
    floatingPos, setFloatingPos,
    copiedStyle, setCopiedStyle,

    /* вњ… Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј РґРІР° Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ:
       вЂў zoom вЂ” СЌС„С„РµРєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ (РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚РѕРІ)
       вЂў displayZoom вЂ” С‚Рѕ, С‡С‚Рѕ РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј Рё РјРµРЅСЏРµРј РІ UI */
    zoom,
    displayZoom,
    setZoom: setDisplayZoom,
    showGrid, setShowGrid,
    showMargins, setShowMargins,
    guides, setGuides,

    updateWithHistory,
    addElementWithHistory,
    removeSelected,
    moveLayer,
    updateSelected,
    handleSnap,
    handleElDragStart: groupDrag.onDragStart,
    handleElDragMove: groupDrag.onDragMove,
    handleElDragEnd: (el: CanvasElement, e: any) => {
      groupDrag.onDragEnd(el, e);
      setGuides({ v: [], h: [] });
    },

    copy, cut, paste, duplicate,
    copyStyle, applyStyle,

    appendAnchor,
    removeLastAnchor,

    exportJson: fileIO.exportJson,
    importJson: fileIO.importJson,
    exportSvg: fileIO.exportSvg,
    addImage: fileIO.addImage,
    setBackgroundImage: fileIO.setBackgroundImage,
    removeBackgroundImage: fileIO.removeBackgroundImage,

    rubberBand: rubberBand.band,
    rubberBandStart: rubberBand.start,
    rubberBandMove: rubberBand.move,
    rubberBandEnd: rubberBand.end,

    fillMode: fillModeApi.fillMode,
    setFillMode: fillModeApi.setFillMode,
    fillColor: fillModeApi.fillColor,
    setFillColor: fillModeApi.setFillColor,
    fillPoints: fillModeApi.points,
    handleFillClick: fillModeApi.handleClick,
    closePolygon: fillModeApi.closePolygon,
  };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement, LineElement } from '../types';
import type { useHistory } from './useHistory';
import type { EditorViewApi } from './useEditorView';

interface SlidesApi {
  slides: any[];
  activeId: string;
  activeSlide: any;
  setSlides: (s: any[]) => void;
  moveLayer: (id: string, dir: 1 | -1) => void;
}

interface SelectionApi {
  selectedIds: string[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  setSelectedIds: (ids: string[]) => void;
  clear: () => void;
}

interface Args {
  slides: SlidesApi;
  selection: SelectionApi;
  history: ReturnType<typeof useHistory>;
  view: EditorViewApi;
}

/**
 * РћРїРµСЂР°С†РёРё СЃ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р°РјРё Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃР»Р°Р№РґР°:
 * update, add, remove, moveLayer, anchors.
 */
export function useEditorActions({ slides, selection, history, view }: Args) {
  const removeSelected = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selection.selectedIds.length) return;
    const newSlides = slides.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slides.activeId
        ? s
        : { ...s, elements: s.elements.filter((e: CanvasElement) => !selection.selectedIds.includes(e.id)) },
    );
    slides.setSlides(newSlides);
    history.push(newSlides, slides.activeId);
    selection.clear();
    view.setFloatingPos(null);
  }, [selection, slides, history, view]);

  const updateWithHistory = useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    const newSlides = slides.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slides.activeId
        ? s
        : {
            ...s,
            elements: s.elements.map((el: CanvasElement) =>
              el.id === id ? ({ ...el, ...patch } as CanvasElement) : el,
            ),
          },
    );
    slides.setSlides(newSlides);
    history.push(newSlides, slides.activeId);
  }, [slides, history]);

  const addElementWithHistory = useCallback((el: CanvasElement) => {
    const newSlides = slides.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slides.activeId ? s : { ...s, elements: [...s.elements, el] },
    );
    slides.setSlides(newSlides);
    selection.setSelectedIds([el.id]);
    history.push(newSlides, slides.activeId);
  }, [slides, selection, history]);

  const moveLayer = useCallback((dir: 1 | -1) => {
    if (!selection.selectedId) return;
    slides.moveLayer(selection.selectedId, dir);
  }, [selection.selectedId, slides]);

  const updateSelected = useCallback((patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    if (!selection.selectedId) return;
    updateWithHistory(selection.selectedId, patch);
  }, [selection.selectedId, updateWithHistory]);

  const appendAnchor = useCallback((el: LineElement) => {
    const pts = el.points && el.points.length >= 4
      ? el.points
      : [0, el.height / 2, el.width, el.height / 2];
    const lastX = pts[pts.length - 2];
    const lastY = pts[pts.length - 1];
    const prevX = pts[pts.length - 4];
    const prevY = pts[pts.length - 3];
    const dx = lastX - prevX;
    const dy = lastY - prevY;
    updateWithHistory(el.id, { points: [...pts, lastX + dx, lastY + dy] });
  }, [updateWithHistory]);

  const removeLastAnchor = useCallback((el: LineElement) => {
    const pts = el.points && el.points.length >= 4 ? el.points : [];
    if (pts.length <= 4) return;
    updateWithHistory(el.id, { points: pts.slice(0, pts.length - 2) });
  }, [updateWithHistory]);

  return {
    removeSelected,
    updateWithHistory,
    addElementWithHistory,
    moveLayer,
    updateSelected,
    appendAnchor,
    removeLastAnchor,
  };
}

export type EditorActionsApi = ReturnType<typeof useEditorActions>;

FILE: hooks\useEditorClipboard.ts

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../types';
import { uid } from '../lib/uid';
import type { useHistory } from './useHistory';
import type { EditorViewApi } from './useEditorView';
import type { EditorActionsApi } from './useEditorActions';

interface SlidesApi {
  slides: any[];
  activeId: string;
  activeSlide: any;
  setSlides: (s: any[]) => void;
}

interface SelectionApi {
  selectedIds: string[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  selected: CanvasElement | null;
  setSelectedIds: (ids: string[]) => void;
}

interface Args {
  slides: SlidesApi;
  selection: SelectionApi;
  history: ReturnType<typeof useHistory>;
  view: EditorViewApi;
  actions: EditorActionsApi;
}

const PASTE_OFFSET = 20;

/**
 * Р‘СѓС„РµСЂ РѕР±РјРµРЅР° Рё СЃС‚РёР»СЊ: copy / cut / paste / duplicate / copyStyle / applyStyle.
 */
export function useEditorClipboard({ slides, selection, history, view, actions }: Args) {
  const [clipboard, setClipboard] = useState<CanvasElement[]>([]);

  const copy = useCallback(() => {
    const els = slides.activeSlide.elements.filter((el: CanvasElement) =>
      selection.selectedIds.includes(el.id),
    );
    if (els.length) setClipboard(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(els)));
  }, [slides.activeSlide, selection.selectedIds]);

  const cut = useCallback(() => {
    const els = slides.activeSlide.elements.filter((el: CanvasElement) =>
      selection.selectedIds.includes(el.id),
    );
    if (els.length) {
      setClipboard(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(els)));
      actions.removeSelected();
    }
  }, [slides.activeSlide, selection.selectedIds, actions]);

  const paste = useCallback(() => {
    if (!clipboard.length) return;
    const pasted = clipboard.map((el) => ({
      ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)),
      id: uid(),
      x: el.x + PASTE_OFFSET,
      y: el.y + PASTE_OFFSET,
    }));
    const newSlides = slides.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slides.activeId ? s : { ...s, elements: [...s.elements, ...pasted] },
    );
    slides.setSlides(newSlides);
    selection.setSelectedIds(pasted.map((el) => el.id));
    history.push(newSlides, slides.activeId);
  }, [clipboard, slides, selection, history]);

  const duplicate = useCallback(() => {
    const els = slides.activeSlide.elements.filter((el: CanvasElement) =>
      selection.selectedIds.includes(el.id),
    );
    if (!els.length) return;
    const pasted = els.map((el: CanvasElement) => ({
      ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)),
      id: uid(),
      x: el.x + PASTE_OFFSET,
      y: el.y + PASTE_OFFSET,
    }));
    const newSlides = slides.slides.map((s) =>
      s.id !== slides.activeId ? s : { ...s, elements: [...s.elements, ...pasted] },
    );
    slides.setSlides(newSlides);
    selection.setSelectedIds(pasted.map((el) => el.id));
    history.push(newSlides, slides.activeId);
  }, [slides, selection, history]);

  const copyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    const el = selection.selected;
    if (!el) return;
    const { id, x, y, rotation, type, ...style } = el as any;
    view.setCopiedStyle(style);
  }, [selection.selected, view]);

  const applyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selection.selectedId || !view.copiedStyle) return;
    actions.updateWithHistory(selection.selectedId, view.copiedStyle);
    view.setCopiedStyle(null);
  }, [selection.selectedId, view, actions]);

  return { copy, cut, paste, duplicate, copyStyle, applyStyle };
}

export type EditorClipboardApi = ReturnType<typeof useEditorClipboard>;

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../types';
import type { useHistory } from './useHistory';
import type { EditorViewApi } from './useEditorView';

interface SlidesApi {
  slides: Slide[];
  activeId: string;
  setSlides: (s: Slide[]) => void;
  setActiveId: (id: string) => void;
}

interface SelectionApi {
  clear: () => void;
}

interface Args {
  slides: SlidesApi;
  selection: SelectionApi;
  history: ReturnType<typeof useHistory>;
  view: EditorViewApi;
}

/**
 * Undo / redo. РћС‚РґРµР»РµРЅРѕ, РїРѕС‚РѕРјСѓ С‡С‚Рѕ СЌС‚Рѕ РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅР°СЏ РѕРїРµСЂР°С†РёСЏ,
 * РєРѕС‚РѕСЂР°СЏ РѕС‡РёС‰Р°РµС‚ РІС‹РґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ Рё СЃР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°РµС‚ РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰РёРµ.
 */
export function useEditorHistory({ slides, selection, history, view }: Args) {
  const undo = useCallback(() => {
    const prev = history.undo();
    if (!prev) return;
    slides.setSlides(prev.slides);
    slides.setActiveId(prev.activeId);
    selection.clear();
    view.clearGuides();
  }, [history, slides, selection, view]);

  const redo = useCallback(() => {
    const next = history.redo();
    if (!next) return;
    slides.setSlides(next.slides);
    slides.setActiveId(next.activeId);
    selection.clear();
    view.clearGuides();
  }, [history, slides, selection, view]);

  return {
    undo,
    redo,
    canUndo: history.canUndo,
    canRedo: history.canRedo,
  };
}

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../types';

export interface FloatingPos {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

export interface ContextMenuState {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  elementId: string | null;
}

export interface Guides {
  v: number[];
  h: number[];
}

/**
 * Р’СЃС‘ UI-СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР°: zoom, СЃРµС‚РєР°, РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰РёРµ, РїР»Р°РІР°СЋС‰РёРµ РїР°РЅРµР»Рё.
 * РќРµ Р·РЅР°РµС‚ РЅРё РїСЂРѕ СЃР»Р°Р№РґС‹, РЅРё РїСЂРѕ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ.
 */
export function useEditorView() {
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(0.6);
  const [showGrid, setShowGrid] = useState(true);
  const [showMargins, setShowMargins] = useState(true);
  const [guides, setGuides] = useState<Guides>({ v: [], h: [] });
  const [floatingPos, setFloatingPos] = useState<FloatingPos | null>(null);
  const [contextMenu, setContextMenu] = useState<ContextMenuState | null>(null);
  const [copiedStyle, setCopiedStyle] = useState<Partial<CanvasElement> | null>(null);

  const clearGuides = useCallback(() => setGuides({ v: [], h: [] }), []);

  return {
    zoom, setZoom,
    showGrid, setShowGrid,
    showMargins, setShowMargins,
    guides, setGuides, clearGuides,
    floatingPos, setFloatingPos,
    contextMenu, setContextMenu,
    copiedStyle, setCopiedStyle,
  };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement, DataRoot } from '../types';
import { getFormatSize, A4 } from '../types';
import { DEFAULT_PADDING } from '../lib/constants';
import { buildSvg } from '../services/svgExport';
import { uid } from '../lib/uid';
import { download } from '../lib/download';

interface Args {
  slides: Slide[];
  setSlides: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Slide[]>>;
  activeId: string;
  setActiveId: (id: string) => void;
  activeSlide: Slide;
  data: DataRoot[];
  setData: (d: DataRoot[]) => void;
  addElementWithHistory: (el: CanvasElement) => void;
  onImported?: () => void;
}

export function useFileIO({
  slides, setSlides, activeId, setActiveId, activeSlide,
  data, setData, addElementWithHistory, onImported,
}: Args) {

  /* ---------- JSON ---------- */

  const exportJson = useCallback(() => {
    const payload = {
      version: '1.5',
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      data,
      slides,
    };
    download(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), 'template.json', 'application/json');
  }, [slides, data]);

  const importJson = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = '.json,application/json';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(reader.result as string);
          let importedSlides: Slide[];
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) importedSlides = parsed;
          else if (parsed && Array.isArray(parsed.slides)) importedSlides = parsed.slides;
          else throw new Error('РќРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ slides');
          if (!importedSlides.length) throw new Error('Р¤Р°Р№Р» РїСѓСЃС‚РѕР№');

          importedSlides = importedSlides.map((s: any) => ({
            id: s.id || uid(),
            name: s.name || 'РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°',
            format: s.format || 'a4',
            orientation: s.orientation || 'portrait',
            width: s.width || A4.width,
            height: s.height || A4.height,
            background: s.background || '#ffffff',
            backgroundImage: s.backgroundImage,
            padding: s.padding || { ...DEFAULT_PADDING },
            elements: Array.isArray(s.elements)
              ? s.elements.map((el: any) => {
                  if (el.type === 'stamp') {
                    return {
                      ...el,
                      topFontSize: el.topFontSize ?? 14,
                      centerFontSize: el.centerFontSize ?? 20,
                      bottomFontSize: el.bottomFontSize ?? 12,
                    };
                  }
                  if (el.type === 'text' || el.type === 'placeholder') {
                    return {
                      ...el,
                      verticalAlign: el.verticalAlign || 'top',
                      lineHeight: el.lineHeight || 1.2,
                      padding: el.padding || { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
                      background: el.background || 'transparent',
                    };
                  }
                  return el;
                })
              : [],
          }));

          setSlides(importedSlides);
          setActiveId(importedSlides[0].id);
          if (parsed.data && Array.isArray(parsed.data)) setData(parsed.data);
          onImported?.();
        } catch (e: any) {
          alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚Р°: ' + (e?.message || e));
        }
      };
      reader.readAsText(file);
    };
    input.click();
  }, [setSlides, setActiveId, setData, onImported]);

  /* ---------- SVG ---------- */

  const exportSvg = useCallback(() => {
    const svg = buildSvg(activeSlide);
    download(svg, `${activeSlide.name || 'page'}.svg`, 'image/svg+xml');
  }, [activeSlide]);

  /* ---------- РљР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєРё ---------- */

  const addImage = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => {
        addElementWithHistory({
          id: uid(),
          type: 'image',
          x: 100, y: 100, width: 200, height: 200, rotation: 0,
          src: reader.result as string,
        });
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  }, [addElementWithHistory]);

  /* ---------- Р¤РѕРЅ С…РѕР»СЃС‚Р° ---------- */

  const setBackgroundImage = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => {
        setSlides((prev) =>
          prev.map((s) =>
            s.id !== activeId ? s : { ...s, backgroundImage: reader.result as string },
          ),
        );
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  }, [activeId, setSlides]);

  const removeBackgroundImage = useCallback(() => {
    if (!activeSlide.backgroundImage) return;
    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) =>
        s.id !== activeId ? s : { ...s, backgroundImage: undefined },
      ),
    );
  }, [activeId, activeSlide.backgroundImage, setSlides]);

  return {
    exportJson,
    importJson,
    exportSvg,
    addImage,
    setBackgroundImage,
    removeBackgroundImage,
  };
}

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement, FillElement } from '../types';
import { hitShapeEl } from '../lib/geometry';
import { uid } from '../lib/uid';

interface Args {
  activeSlide: Slide;
  updateElement: (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  addElement: (el: CanvasElement) => void;
  pointerPos: () => { x: number; y: number };
  onElementSelected?: (id: string) => void;
}

export function useFillMode({ activeSlide, updateElement, addElement, pointerPos, onElementSelected }: Args) {
  const [fillMode, setFillMode] = useState(false);
  const [fillColor, setFillColor] = useState('#fbbf24');
  const [points, setPoints] = useState<number[]>([]);

  const findShapeAt = useCallback((x: number, y: number): CanvasElement | null => {
    for (let i = activeSlide.elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const el = activeSlide.elements[i];
      if (hitShapeEl(el, x, y)) return el;
    }
    return null;
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const handleClick = useCallback((onEmpty: () => void): boolean => {
    if (!fillMode) return false;
    const pos = pointerPos();
    const target = findShapeAt(pos.x, pos.y);
    if (target && (target.type === 'shape' || target.type === 'fill')) {
      updateElement(target.id, { fill: fillColor });
      onElementSelected?.(target.id);
      return true;
    }
    onEmpty();
    setPoints((prev) => [...prev, pos.x, pos.y]);
    return true;
  }, [fillMode, fillColor, findShapeAt, updateElement, pointerPos, onElementSelected]);

  const closePolygon = useCallback(() => {
    if (points.length < 6) { setPoints([]); return; }
    const xs: number[] = [];
    const ys: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i += 2) {
      xs.push(points[i]);
      ys.push(points[i + 1]);
    }
    const minX = Math.min(...xs);
    const minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
    const maxY = Math.max(...ys);
    const rel = points.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY));

    const el: FillElement = {
      id: uid(), type: 'fill',
      x: minX, y: minY,
      width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
      height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
      rotation: 0,
      points: rel,
      fill: fillColor,
      stroke: '',
      strokeWidth: 0,
      opacity: 1,
    };
    addElement(el);
    setPoints([]);
  }, [points, fillColor, addElement]);

  const cancel = useCallback(() => setPoints([]), []);

  return { fillMode, setFillMode, fillColor, setFillColor, points, handleClick, closePolygon, cancel };
}

FILE: hooks\useGroupDrag.ts

import { useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement, Slide } from '../types';

interface GroupDragState {
  id: string;
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
  orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>;
}

interface Args {
  activeSlide: Slide;
  selectedIds: string[];
  setSelectedIds: (ids: string[]) => void;
  setSlides: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Slide[]>>;
  activeId: string;
  handleSnap: (e: any, el: CanvasElement) => void;
  setFloatingPos: (p: { x: number; y: number } | null) => void;
}

export function useGroupDrag({
  activeSlide, selectedIds, setSelectedIds, setSlides, activeId, handleSnap, setFloatingPos,
}: Args) {
  const ref = useRef<GroupDragState | null>(null);

  const onDragStart = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, e: any) => {
    setFloatingPos(null);
    const ids = selectedIds.includes(el.id) ? selectedIds : [el.id];
    if (!selectedIds.includes(el.id)) setSelectedIds([el.id]);
    const orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
    activeSlide.elements.forEach((ee) => {
      if (ids.includes(ee.id)) orig[ee.id] = { x: ee.x, y: ee.y };
    });
    ref.current = {
      id: el.id,
      startX: e.target.x(),
      startY: e.target.y(),
      orig,
    };
  }, [activeSlide, selectedIds, setSelectedIds, setFloatingPos]);

  const onDragMove = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, e: any) => {
    const g = ref.current;
    if (!g) return;
    const dx = e.target.x() - g.startX;
    const dy = e.target.y() - g.startY;

    const newElements = activeSlide.elements.map((ee) => {
      if (!g.orig[ee.id]) return ee;
      if (ee.id === el.id) return ee;
      return { ...ee, x: g.orig[ee.id].x + dx, y: g.orig[ee.id].y + dy };
    });

    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id !== activeId ? s : { ...s, elements: newElements })),
    );

    if (el.id === g.id) handleSnap(e, el);
  }, [activeSlide, activeId, setSlides, handleSnap]);

  const onDragEnd = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, e: any) => {
    const g = ref.current;
    if (!g) return;
    const dx = e.target.x() - g.startX;
    const dy = e.target.y() - g.startY;

    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id !== activeId) return s;
        return {
          ...s,
          elements: s.elements.map((ee) => {
            if (!g.orig[ee.id]) return ee;
            if (ee.id === el.id) return { ...ee, x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() };
            return { ...ee, x: g.orig[ee.id].x + dx, y: g.orig[ee.id].y + dy };
          }),
        };
      }),
    );
    ref.current = null;
  }, [activeId, setSlides]);

  return { onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd };
}

FILE: hooks\useHistory.ts
