import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../types';

export interface HistoryEntry {
  slides: Slide[];
  activeId: string;
}

export function useHistory() {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<HistoryEntry[]>([]);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(-1);

  const push = useCallback((slides: Slide[], activeId: string) => {
    setEntries((prev) => {
      const next = prev.slice(0, index + 1);
      next.push({ slides: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(slides)), activeId });
      if (next.length > 50) next.shift();
      return next;
    });
    setIndex((prev) => Math.min(prev + 1, 49));
  }, [index]);

  const undo = useCallback((): HistoryEntry | null => {
    if (index <= 0) return null;
    const prev = entries[index - 1];
    setIndex(index - 1);
    return prev;
  }, [entries, index]);

  const redo = useCallback((): HistoryEntry | null => {
    if (index >= entries.length - 1) return null;
    const next = entries[index + 1];
    setIndex(index + 1);
    return next;
  }, [entries, index]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    setEntries([]);
    setIndex(-1);
  }, []);

  const canUndo = index > 0;
  const canRedo = index < entries.length - 1;

  return { push, undo, redo, reset, canUndo, canRedo };
}

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement, TableElement } from '../types';
import type { EditState, EditField, EditKind, Zone, InlineEditorArgs } from './inlineEditor/types';
import { getStageOffset, calcElementGeometry, calcCellGeometry } from './inlineEditor/position';
import { useClickOutside } from './inlineEditor/useClickOutside';
import { TEXT_PLACEHOLDER } from '../lib/constants';

export type { EditField, EditKind, EditState } from './inlineEditor/types';

const OVERLAY_SELECTORS = [
  '.inline-editor-wrap',
  '.inline-text-editor',
  '.plain-contenteditable',
  '.rt-editor',
  '.rt-toolbar',
  '.rt-popover',
  '.rt-popover-wrap',
  '.text-toolbar',
];

function isEmptyText(text: string | undefined, html: string | undefined): boolean {
  const t = (text ?? '').trim();
  if (!t || t === TEXT_PLACEHOLDER) return true;
  if (html !== undefined) {
    const h = html.trim();
    if (!h || h === '<p></p>' || h === '<p><br></p>') return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export function useInlineEditor({
  wrapRef, stageRef, zoom, activeSlide, updateElement, onBeforeOpen,
}: InlineEditorArgs) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<EditState | null>(null);
  const [showToolbar, setShowToolbar] = useState(false);

  const commit = useCallback((valueOverride?: string, htmlOverride?: string) => {
    if (!editing) return;

    const v = valueOverride !== undefined ? valueOverride : editing.value;
    const h = htmlOverride !== undefined ? htmlOverride : editing.html;

    if (editing.field === 'tableCell') {
      commitCell(editing, v, activeSlide, updateElement);
    } else if (editing.field === 'text') {
      const empty = !v.trim();
      updateElement(editing.id, {
        text: empty ? TEXT_PLACEHOLDER : v,
        html: empty ? undefined : (h ?? undefined),
      } as any);
    } else if (editing.field === 'footerText') {
      const patch: any = { text: v };
      if (h !== undefined) patch.html = h;
      updateElement(editing.id, patch);
    } else if (editing.field === 'shapeText') {
      updateElement(editing.id, { text: v });
    } else {
      updateElement(editing.id, { [editing.field]: v } as any);
    }

    setShowToolbar(false);
    setEditing(null);
  }, [editing, activeSlide, updateElement]);

  const open = useCallback((
    el: CanvasElement,
    field: EditField,
    clickPos?: { x: number; y: number },
  ) => {
    onBeforeOpen?.();
    if (editing) commit();

    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!wrap || !stage) return;

    const offset = getStageOffset(wrap, stage as any);
    const info = describeElement(el, field);
    if (!info) return;

    /* вњ… РџРµСЂРµРґР°С‘Рј zone вЂ” РІР°Р¶РЅРѕ РґР»СЏ stamp (top/center/bottom) */
    const geom = calcElementGeometry(
      el, offset, zoom, info.contentHeight, info.kind as any, info.zone,
    );

    setEditing({
      id: el.id,
      field,
      kind: info.kind,
      zone: info.zone,
      value: info.value,
      html: info.html,
      x: geom.x,
      y: geom.y,
      width: geom.width,
      height: geom.height,
      fontSize: info.fontSize * zoom,
      fontFamily: info.fontFamily,
      fontStyle: info.fontStyle,
      textDecoration: info.textDecoration,
      align: info.align,
      fill: info.fill,
      lineHeight: info.lineHeight,
      verticalAlign: info.verticalAlign,
      padding: info.padding,
      clickX: clickPos?.x,
      clickY: clickPos?.y,
    });

    /* Rich (text/footer) вЂ” СЃРІРѕР№ С‚СѓР»Р±Р°СЂ Сѓ TipTap.
       Shape / stamp / table вЂ” СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ inline, Р±РµР· РІРЅРµС€РЅРµРіРѕ С‚СѓР»Р±Р°СЂР°. */
    const isRich = info.kind === 'text' || info.kind === 'footer';
    setShowToolbar(!isRich && info.kind !== 'shape' && info.kind !== 'stamp');
  }, [editing, commit, wrapRef, stageRef, zoom, onBeforeOpen]);

  const openCell = useCallback((el: TableElement, rowIndex: number, colIndex: number) => {
    onBeforeOpen?.();
    if (editing) commit();

    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!wrap || !stage) return;

    const offset = getStageOffset(wrap, stage as any);
    const geom = calcCellGeometry(el, offset, zoom, { rowIndex, colIndex });

    setEditing({
      id: el.id, field: 'tableCell', kind: 'table',
      tableRow: rowIndex, tableCol: colIndex,
      value: el.data[rowIndex]?.[colIndex] ?? '',
      x: geom.x, y: geom.y, width: geom.width, height: geom.height,
      fontSize: el.fontSize * zoom,
      fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
      align: 'left', fill: '#111',
      verticalAlign: 'top',
      padding: { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
    });
    setShowToolbar(false);
  }, [editing, commit, wrapRef, stageRef, zoom, onBeforeOpen]);

  useClickOutside({
    enabled: !!editing,
    onOutside: () => commit(),
    ignoreSelectors: OVERLAY_SELECTORS,
  });

  const onToolbarChange = useCallback((patch: any) => {
    setEditing((cur) => {
      if (!cur) return null;
      const next = { ...cur };
      if (patch.fontSize !== undefined) next.fontSize = patch.fontSize * zoom;
      if (patch.fontFamily !== undefined) next.fontFamily = patch.fontFamily;
      if (patch.fontStyle !== undefined) next.fontStyle = patch.fontStyle;
      if (patch.textDecoration !== undefined) next.textDecoration = patch.textDecoration;
      if (patch.fill !== undefined) next.fill = patch.fill;
      if (patch.align !== undefined) next.align = patch.align;

      const realPatch = buildRealPatch(cur, patch);
      updateElement(cur.id, realPatch);
      return next;
    });
  }, [zoom, updateElement]);

  return {
    editing,
    setEditing,
    showToolbar,
    setShowToolbar,
    open,
    openCell,
    commit,
    onToolbarChange,
  };
}

/* ============================================================ */

interface ElementDescription {
  kind: EditKind;
  zone?: Zone;
  value: string;
  html?: string;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
  fill: string;
  contentHeight: number;
  lineHeight?: number;
  verticalAlign?: 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
  padding?: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number };
}

function describeElement(el: CanvasElement, field: EditField): ElementDescription | null {
  if (el.type === 'text') {
    const empty = isEmptyText(el.text, el.html);
    return {
      kind: 'text',
      value: empty ? '' : el.text,
      html: empty ? undefined : el.html,
      fontSize: el.fontSize,
      fontFamily: el.fontFamily,
      fontStyle: el.fontStyle,
      textDecoration: el.textDecoration,
      align: el.align,
      fill: el.fill,
      lineHeight: el.lineHeight,
      verticalAlign: el.verticalAlign,
      padding: el.padding,
      contentHeight: el.height,
    };
  }
  if (el.type === 'footer') {
    return {
      kind: 'footer',
      value: el.text,
      html: el.html,
      fontSize: el.fontSize,
      fontFamily: 'Arial',
      fontStyle: 'normal',
      textDecoration: '',
      align: 'center',
      fill: el.fill,
      contentHeight: el.height,
    };
  }
  if (el.type === 'shape') {
    return {
      kind: 'shape',
      value: el.text,
      fontSize: el.fontSize,
      fontFamily: el.textFontFamily || 'Arial',
      fontStyle: el.textFontStyle || 'normal',
      textDecoration: el.textDecoration || '',
      align: el.textAlign || 'center',
      fill: el.textFill,
      lineHeight: el.textLineHeight || 1.3,
      verticalAlign: el.textVerticalAlign || 'middle',
      padding: el.textPadding || { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 },
      contentHeight: el.height,
    };
  }
  if (el.type === 'stamp') {
    return describeStamp(el, field);
  }
  return null;
}

function describeStamp(
  el: Extract<CanvasElement, { type: 'stamp' }>,
  field: EditField,
): ElementDescription {
  const zone: Zone =
    field === 'topText' ? 'top'
    : field === 'bottomText' ? 'bottom'
    : 'center';

  if (zone === 'top') {
    return {
      kind: 'stamp', zone, value: el.topText,
      fontSize: el.topFontSize || 14,
      fontFamily: el.topFontFamily || 'Arial',
      fontStyle: el.topFontStyle || 'normal',
      textDecoration: el.topTextDecoration || '',
      align: 'center',
      fill: el.topColor || el.stampColor,
      contentHeight: 32,
    };
  }
  if (zone === 'bottom') {
    return {
      kind: 'stamp', zone, value: el.bottomText,
      fontSize: el.bottomFontSize || 12,
      fontFamily: el.bottomFontFamily || 'Arial',
      fontStyle: el.bottomFontStyle || 'normal',
      textDecoration: el.bottomTextDecoration || '',
      align: 'center',
      fill: el.bottomColor || el.stampColor,
      contentHeight: 32,
    };
  }
  return {
    kind: 'stamp', zone, value: el.centerText,
    fontSize: el.centerFontSize || 20,
    fontFamily: el.centerFontFamily || 'Arial',
    fontStyle: el.centerFontStyle || 'normal',
    textDecoration: el.centerTextDecoration || '',
    align: 'center',
    fill: el.centerColor || el.stampColor,
    contentHeight: 40,
  };
}

function buildRealPatch(cur: EditState, patch: any): Record<string, any> {
  const real: Record<string, any> = {};

  if (cur.kind === 'stamp' && cur.zone) {
    const prefix = cur.zone === 'top' ? 'top' : cur.zone === 'bottom' ? 'bottom' : 'center';
    if (patch.fontSize !== undefined) real[`${prefix}FontSize`] = patch.fontSize;
    if (patch.fontFamily !== undefined) real[`${prefix}FontFamily`] = patch.fontFamily;
    if (patch.fontStyle !== undefined) real[`${prefix}FontStyle`] = patch.fontStyle;
    if (patch.textDecoration !== undefined) real[`${prefix}TextDecoration`] = patch.textDecoration;
    if (patch.fill !== undefined) real[`${prefix}Color`] = patch.fill;
    return real;
  }

  if (cur.kind === 'text') {
    if (patch.fontSize !== undefined) real.fontSize = patch.fontSize;
    if (patch.fontFamily !== undefined) real.fontFamily = patch.fontFamily;
    if (patch.fontStyle !== undefined) real.fontStyle = patch.fontStyle;
    if (patch.textDecoration !== undefined) real.textDecoration = patch.textDecoration;
    if (patch.fill !== undefined) real.fill = patch.fill;
    if (patch.align !== undefined) real.align = patch.align;
    return real;
  }

  if (patch.fontSize !== undefined) real.fontSize = patch.fontSize;
  if (patch.fill !== undefined) {
    if (cur.kind === 'shape') real.textFill = patch.fill;
    else real.fill = patch.fill;
  }
  return real;
}

function commitCell(
  editing: EditState,
  value: string,
  activeSlide: { elements: CanvasElement[] },
  updateElement: (id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void,
): void {
  const el = activeSlide.elements.find((e) => e.id === editing.id) as TableElement | undefined;
  if (!el || el.type !== 'table') return;
  const dataArr = el.data.map((row) => [...row]);
  const r = editing.tableRow!;
  const c = editing.tableCol!;
  if (dataArr[r] && c < dataArr[r].length) {
    dataArr[r][c] = value;
    updateElement(editing.id, { data: dataArr });
  }
}

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export interface ShortcutHandlers {
  onUndo: () => void;
  onRedo: () => void;
  onCopy: () => void;
  onPaste: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onEscape?: () => void;
  onEnter?: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Р“Р»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РіРѕСЂСЏС‡РёРµ РєР»Р°РІРёС€Рё СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР°.
 *
 * РџСЂР°РІРёР»Р°:
 *  вЂў РЎР»СѓС€Р°С‚РµР»СЊ РІРµС€Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° window РІ capture-С„Р°Р·Рµ вЂ” РЅРµ РґР°С‘Рј РЅРёРєРѕРјСѓ В«СЃСЉРµСЃС‚СЊВ» СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ.
 *  вЂў Ctrl+A РќР• РїРµСЂРµС…РІР°С‚С‹РІР°РµРј РЅРёРєРѕРіРґР° вЂ” РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂСѓ / ProseMirror.
 *  вЂў РљРѕРіРґР° РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚ inline-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ вЂ” СЃР»СѓС€Р°С‚РµР»СЊ РІРѕРѕР±С‰Рµ РЅРµ РЅР°РІРµС€РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ
 *    (СЃРј. `disabled`), РїРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ Ctrl+A СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РІ contentEditable TipTap.
 *  вЂў Р’ input/textarea/select/ contentEditable РЅРµ РјРµС€Р°РµРј РЅР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕРјСѓ РІРІРѕРґСѓ.
 */
export function useKeyboardShortcuts(h: ShortcutHandlers) {
  const hRef = useRef(h);
  hRef.current = h;

  useEffect(() => {
    /* вњ… РџСЂРё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РѕРј TipTap-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРµ РІРѕРѕР±С‰Рµ РЅРµ РІРјРµС€РёРІР°РµРјСЃСЏ */
    if (hRef.current.disabled) return;

    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const handlers = hRef.current;
      if (handlers.disabled) return;

      const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      const tag = t?.tagName;
      const isField =
        tag === 'INPUT' ||
        tag === 'TEXTAREA' ||
        tag === 'SELECT' ||
        (t != null && (t as HTMLElement).isContentEditable === true);

      const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
      const code = e.code;

      /* вњ… Ctrl+A вЂ” РќР• РїРµСЂРµС…РІР°С‚С‹РІР°РµРј, РѕС‚РґР°С‘Рј Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂСѓ */
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyA') return;

      /* Undo / Redo */
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyZ' && !e.shiftKey) {
        if (isField) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        handlers.onUndo();
        return;
      }
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyZ' && e.shiftKey) {
        if (isField) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        handlers.onRedo();
        return;
      }
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyY') {
        if (isField) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        handlers.onRedo();
        return;
      }

      /* РћСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІРЅРµ РїРѕР»РµР№ РІРІРѕРґР° */
      if (isField) return;

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyC') { e.preventDefault(); handlers.onCopy(); return; }
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyV') { e.preventDefault(); handlers.onPaste(); return; }
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyD') { e.preventDefault(); handlers.onDuplicate(); return; }

      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handlers.onDelete();
        return;
      }

      if (e.key === 'Escape') { handlers.onEscape?.(); return; }
      if (e.key === 'Enter') { handlers.onEnter?.(); }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
  }, [h.disabled]); // в†ђ РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРіРґР° РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ disabled
}

import { useState, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../types';
import { getBBox } from '../lib/geometry';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import { RefObject } from 'react';

export interface RubberBand {
  x1: number;
  y1: number;
  x2: number;
  y2: number;
}

interface Args {
  stageRef: RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
  zoom: number;
  activeSlide: Slide;
  setSelectedIds: (ids: string[]) => void;
}

export function useRubberBand({ stageRef, zoom, activeSlide, setSelectedIds }: Args) {
  const [band, setBand] = useState<RubberBand | null>(null);
  const startRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  const pointerPos = () => {
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
    const p = stage.getPointerPosition();
    if (!p) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
    return { x: p.x / zoom, y: p.y / zoom };
  };

  const start = useCallback(() => {
    const pos = pointerPos();
    startRef.current = pos;
    setBand({ x1: pos.x, y1: pos.y, x2: pos.x, y2: pos.y });
  }, [zoom, stageRef]);

  const move = useCallback(() => {
    if (!startRef.current) return;
    const pos = pointerPos();
    const s = startRef.current;
    setBand({ x1: s.x, y1: s.y, x2: pos.x, y2: pos.y });
  }, [zoom, stageRef]);

  const end = useCallback(() => {
    if (!band || !startRef.current) {
      startRef.current = null;
      setBand(null);
      return;
    }
    const x1 = Math.min(band.x1, band.x2);
    const x2 = Math.max(band.x1, band.x2);
    const y1 = Math.min(band.y1, band.y2);
    const y2 = Math.max(band.y1, band.y2);

    if (Math.abs(x2 - x1) < 4 && Math.abs(y2 - y1) < 4) {
      startRef.current = null;
      setBand(null);
      return;
    }
    const inside = activeSlide.elements.filter((el) => {
      const bb = getBBox(el);
      return bb.x >= x1 && bb.y >= y1 && bb.x + bb.w <= x2 && bb.y + bb.h <= y2;
    });
    setSelectedIds(inside.map((e) => e.id));
    startRef.current = null;
    setBand(null);
  }, [band, activeSlide, setSelectedIds]);

  return { band, start, move, end };
}

import { useState, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement, Slide } from '../types';

export function useSelection(activeSlide: Slide) {
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const selectedId = selectedIds[0] ?? null;
  const selected = useMemo(
    () => activeSlide.elements.find((e) => e.id === selectedId) ?? null,
    [activeSlide, selectedId],
  );

  const select = useCallback((id: string, multi = false) => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) => {
      if (!multi) return [id];
      return prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id];
    });
  }, []);

  const clear = useCallback(() => setSelectedIds([]), []);
  const selectMany = useCallback((ids: string[]) => setSelectedIds(ids), []);
  const isSelected = useCallback(
    (el: CanvasElement) => selectedIds.includes(el.id),
    [selectedIds],
  );

  return { selectedIds, selectedId, selected, select, clear, selectMany, isSelected, setSelectedIds };
}

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement, DocFormat, Orientation } from '../types';
import { getFormatSize } from '../types';
import { newSlide } from '../lib/factories';

/* ---------- РҐРµР»РїРµСЂ: РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡С‘С‚ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ РїСЂРё СЃРјРµРЅРµ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂРѕРІ СЃР»Р°Р№РґР° ---------- */
function reflowElements(
  elements: CanvasElement[],
  slide: Slide,
  newWidth: number,
  newHeight: number,
): CanvasElement[] {
  const printableWidth = Math.max(
    0,
    newWidth - slide.padding.left - slide.padding.right,
  );
  const printableHeight = Math.max(
    0,
    newHeight - slide.padding.top - slide.padding.bottom,
  );

  return elements.map((el) => {
    if (el.type === 'footer') {
      return {
        ...el,
        x: slide.padding.left,
        y: newHeight - slide.padding.bottom - el.height,
        width: printableWidth,
      };
    }

    if (el.type === 'placeholder') {
      return {
        ...el,
        x: slide.padding.left,
        y: slide.padding.top,
        width: printableWidth,
        height: printableHeight,
      };
    }

    // РћСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚С‹ вЂ” РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ РѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РёРІР°РµРј, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ РІС‹Р»РµР·Р°Р»Рё Р·Р° Р»РёСЃС‚
    const maxX = Math.max(0, newWidth - el.width);
    const maxY = Math.max(0, newHeight - el.height);

    return {
      ...el,
      x: Math.min(el.x, maxX),
      y: Math.min(el.y, maxY),
      width: Math.min(el.width, printableWidth),
    };
  });
}

export function useSlides() {
  const [slides, setSlides] = useState<Slide[]>(() => [newSlide(1)]);
  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState(() => slides[0].id);
  const [customSize, setCustomSize] = useState({ w: 794, h: 1123 });

  const activeSlide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId)!;

  const updateElement = useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) =>
        s.id !== activeId
          ? s
          : {
              ...s,
              elements: s.elements.map((el) =>
                el.id === id ? ({ ...el, ...patch } as CanvasElement) : el,
              ),
            },
      ),
    );
  }, [activeId]);

  const updateSlide = useCallback((patch: Partial<Slide>, _history = false) => {
    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id === activeId ? { ...s, ...patch } : s)),
    );
  }, [activeId]);

  const addElement = useCallback((el: CanvasElement) => {
    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) =>
        s.id !== activeId ? s : { ...s, elements: [...s.elements, el] },
      ),
    );
  }, [activeId]);

  const removeElements = useCallback((ids: string[]) => {
    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) =>
        s.id !== activeId
          ? s
          : { ...s, elements: s.elements.filter((e) => !ids.includes(e.id)) },
      ),
    );
  }, [activeId]);

  const moveLayer = useCallback((id: string, dir: 1 | -1) => {
    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id !== activeId) return s;
        const idx = s.elements.findIndex((e) => e.id === id);
        if (idx < 0) return s;
        const target = idx + dir;
        if (target < 0 || target >= s.elements.length) return s;
        const arr = [...s.elements];
        [arr[idx], arr[target]] = [arr[target], arr[idx]];
        return { ...s, elements: arr };
      }),
    );
  }, [activeId]);

  const addSlide = useCallback(() => {
    setSlides((prev) => {
      const s = newSlide(prev.length + 1, activeSlide.format, activeSlide.orientation);
      s.padding = { ...activeSlide.padding };
      setActiveId(s.id);
      return [...prev, s];
    });
  }, [activeSlide]);

  const removeSlide = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setSlides((prev) => {
      if (prev.length === 1) return prev;
      const next = prev.filter((s) => s.id !== id);
      if (activeId === id) setActiveId(next[0].id);
      return next;
    });
  }, [activeId]);

  /* ---------------- Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚ ---------------- */

  const changeFormat = useCallback((format: DocFormat) => {
    const { w, h } = getFormatSize(
      format,
      activeSlide.orientation,
      format === 'custom' ? customSize : undefined,
    );

    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id !== activeId) return s;
        const elements = reflowElements(s.elements, s, w, h);
        return { ...s, format, width: w, height: h, elements };
      }),
    );
  }, [activeId, activeSlide.orientation, customSize]);

  /* ---------------- РћСЂРёРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёСЏ ---------------- */

  const changeOrientation = useCallback((orientation: Orientation) => {
    const { w, h } = getFormatSize(
      activeSlide.format,
      orientation,
      activeSlide.format === 'custom' ? customSize : undefined,
    );

    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id !== activeId) return s;
        const elements = reflowElements(s.elements, s, w, h);
        return { ...s, orientation, width: w, height: h, elements };
      }),
    );
  }, [activeId, activeSlide.format, customSize]);

  /* ---------------- РљР°СЃС‚РѕРјРЅС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ ---------------- */

  const changeCustomSize = useCallback((w: number, h: number) => {
    setCustomSize({ w, h });
    if (activeSlide.format !== 'custom') return;

    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id !== activeId) return s;

        const newWidth = s.orientation === 'portrait' ? w : h;
        const newHeight = s.orientation === 'portrait' ? h : w;
        const elements = reflowElements(s.elements, s, newWidth, newHeight);

        return { ...s, width: newWidth, height: newHeight, elements };
      }),
    );
  }, [activeId, activeSlide.format]);

  return {
    slides, setSlides,
    activeId, setActiveId,
    activeSlide,
    customSize, changeCustomSize,
    updateElement, updateSlide,
    addElement, removeElements, moveLayer,
    addSlide, removeSlide,
    changeFormat, changeOrientation,
  };
}

FILE: InlineEditor.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface Props {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  value: string;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  align: string;
  fill: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const InlineTextEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({
  x, y, width, height, value, fontSize, fontFamily,
  fontStyle, textDecoration, align, fill,
  onChange, onCommit, onCancel,
}) => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    ref.current?.focus();
    // РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅР°РІР»РёРІР°РµРј РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂ РІ РєРѕРЅРµС†
    const len = value.length;
    ref.current?.setSelectionRange(len, len);
  }, []);

  return (
    <textarea
      ref={ref}
      className="inline-text-editor"
      style={{
        left: x,
        top: y,
        width,
        minHeight: height,
        fontSize,
        fontFamily,
        fontWeight: fontStyle.includes('bold') ? 'bold' : 'normal',
        fontStyle: fontStyle.includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal',
        textDecoration,
        textAlign: align as any,
        color: fill,
        lineHeight: 1.2,
      }}
      value={value}
      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          onCancel();
        }
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
          // Enter Р±РµР· Shift вЂ” РІСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РЅРѕРІСѓСЋ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ (РєР°Рє РІ draw.io)
          // Р”Р»СЏ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅРёСЏ вЂ” Shift+Enter
          return; // textarea СЃР°Рј РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚
        }
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.shiftKey) {
          e.preventDefault();
          onCommit();
        }
      }}
      onBlur={onCommit}
      autoFocus
      spellCheck={false}
    />
  );
};

import React, { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { RichTextEditor } from './RichTextEditor';
import { plainToHtml } from './lib/richText';

const RICH_MAX_W = 1200;
const RICH_MAX_H = 820;
const RICH_WIN_MARGIN = 40;

interface Props {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  value: string;
  html?: string;
  kind: 'text' | 'shape' | 'footer' | 'stamp' | 'table';
  richText?: boolean;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle?: string;
  textDecoration?: string;
  fill: string;
  align: string;
  verticalAlign?: 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
  padding?: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number };
  lineHeight?: number;
  clickX?: number;
  clickY?: number;
  onChange: (patch: { html?: string; text?: string; value?: string }) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const InlineTextEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({
  x, y, width, height, value, html, kind,
  richText,
  fontSize, fontFamily, fontStyle = 'normal', textDecoration = '',
  fill, align, verticalAlign = 'top',
  padding = { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 },
  lineHeight,
  clickX, clickY,
  onChange, onCommit, onCancel,
}) => {
  const isRich = (kind === 'text' || kind === 'footer') && richText !== false;

  if (isRich) {
    const winW = window.innerWidth;
    const winH = window.innerHeight;
    const w = Math.max(640, Math.min(RICH_MAX_W, winW - RICH_WIN_MARGIN * 2));
    const h = Math.max(500, Math.min(RICH_MAX_H, winH - RICH_WIN_MARGIN * 2));

    return (
      <>
        <div className="rich-modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} />
        <div className="rich-modal" style={{ width: w, height: h }} onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
          <RichTextEditor
            initialHtml={html && html.trim() ? html : plainToHtml(value || '')}
            fontSize={fontSize}
            fontFamily={fontFamily}
            fill={fill}
            align={align}
            lineHeight={lineHeight ?? 1.5}
            blockGap={8}
            width={w}
            minHeight={h}
            onChange={(h2, t) => onChange({ html: h2, text: t })}
            onCommit={onCommit}
            onCancel={onCancel}
          />
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  /* вњ… Inline вЂ” РґР»СЏ РїСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕРіРѕ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°, С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° РІ С„РёРіСѓСЂРµ Рё С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° РІ stamp.
     Padding СѓР¶Рµ СѓС‡С‚С‘РЅ РІ РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚Р°С…, РїРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РЅРµ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј. */
  const isStampLike = kind === 'stamp';

  return (
    <PlainOverlay
      x={x}
      y={y}
      width={width}
      height={height}
      value={value}
      fontSize={fontSize}
      fontFamily={fontFamily}
      fontStyle={fontStyle}
      textDecoration={textDecoration}
      fill={fill}
      align={isStampLike ? 'center' : align}
      verticalAlign={isStampLike ? 'middle' : verticalAlign}
      padding={{ top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }}
      lineHeight={lineHeight || 1.35}
      clickX={clickX}
      clickY={clickY}
      onChange={(v) => onChange({ value: v, text: v })}
      onCommit={onCommit}
      onCancel={onCancel}
    />
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   PlainOverlay вЂ” contenteditable РїРѕРІРµСЂС… СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р°
   ============================================================ */

interface PlainProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  value: string;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  fill: string;
  align: string;
  verticalAlign: 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
  padding: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number };
  lineHeight: number;
  clickX?: number;
  clickY?: number;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

const PlainOverlay: React.FC<PlainProps> = ({
  x, y, width, height, value,
  fontSize, fontFamily, fontStyle, textDecoration, fill,
  align, verticalAlign, padding, lineHeight,
  clickX, clickY,
  onChange, onCommit, onCancel,
}) => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    el.innerText = value;

    const sel = window.getSelection();

    /* РџС‹С‚Р°РµРјСЃСЏ РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РєР°СЂРµС‚РєСѓ РІ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ РєР»РёРєР° */
    let placed = false;
    if (clickX !== undefined && clickY !== undefined) {
      const doc = document as Document & {
        caretRangeFromPoint?: (x: number, y: number) => Range | null;
        caretPositionFromPoint?: (x: number, y: number) => { offsetNode: Node; offset: number } | null;
      };
      let range: Range | null = null;
      if (typeof doc.caretRangeFromPoint === 'function') {
        range = doc.caretRangeFromPoint(clickX, clickY);
      } else if (typeof doc.caretPositionFromPoint === 'function') {
        const pos = doc.caretPositionFromPoint(clickX, clickY);
        if (pos) {
          range = document.createRange();
          range.setStart(pos.offsetNode, pos.offset);
          range.collapse(true);
        }
      }
      if (range && el.contains(range.startContainer)) {
        sel?.removeAllRanges();
        sel?.addRange(range);
        placed = true;
      }
    }

    if (!placed) {
      const range = document.createRange();
      range.setStart(el, 0);
      range.collapse(true);
      sel?.removeAllRanges();
      sel?.addRange(range);
    }

    el.focus();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const handleInput = () => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    onChange(el.innerText);
  };

  const justify =
    verticalAlign === 'middle' ? 'center'
    : verticalAlign === 'bottom' ? 'flex-end'
    : 'flex-start';

  return (
    <div
      className="inline-editor-wrap plain-overlay"
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        left: x,
        top: y,
        width,
        height,
        zIndex: 1000,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        justifyContent: justify,
        boxSizing: 'border-box',
        padding: `${padding.top}px ${padding.right}px ${padding.bottom}px ${padding.left}px`,
        background: 'transparent',
        border: 'none',
        outline: '1.5px dashed #6366f1',
        outlineOffset: 1,
        boxShadow: 'none',
        overflow: 'visible',
      }}
      onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
    >
      <div
        ref={ref}
        className="plain-contenteditable"
        contentEditable
        suppressContentEditableWarning
        onInput={handleInput}
        onBlur={onCommit}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); onCancel(); }
          if (e.key === 'Enter' && (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey)) {
            e.preventDefault();
            onCommit();
          }
        }}
        style={{
          outline: 'none',
          minHeight: '1em',
          width: '100%',
          fontSize,
          fontFamily,
          fontStyle: fontStyle.includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal',
          fontWeight: fontStyle.includes('bold') ? 700 : 400,
          textDecoration,
          color: fill,
          lineHeight,
          textAlign: align as any,
          whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
          wordBreak: 'break-word',
          boxSizing: 'border-box',
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

export function download(content: string, name: string, mime: string): void {
  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mime });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export function makeArrayTable(
  x: number, y: number,
  payload: {
    path: string;
    arrayName: string;
    sampleCount?: number;
    arrayChildren: { name: string; type: string }[];
  },
): TableElement {
  const children = payload.arrayChildren || [];
  const count = Math.max(1, payload.sampleCount ?? 3);
  const header: string[] = ['РђС‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚'];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) header.push(`#${i + 1}`);
  const data: string[][] = [header];
  children.forEach((child) => {
    const row: string[] = [child.name];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) row.push(`{{${payload.path}[${i}].${child.name}}}`);
    data.push(row);
  });
  const rows = data.length;
  const cols = 1 + count;
  return {
    id: uid(), type: 'table',
    x: Math.max(0, x - 100), y: Math.max(0, y - 20),
    width: 40 + cols * 120, height: 20 + rows * 32, rotation: 0,
    rows, cols, data,
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1', headerBg: '#eef2ff', fontSize: 13,
  };
}

import type { CanvasElement, Slide } from '../types';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '../types';

export interface BBox { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; }

export function getBBox(el: CanvasElement): BBox {
  if ((el.type === 'line' || el.type === 'fill') && el.points && el.points.length >= 4) {
    const xs: number[] = [];
    const ys: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < el.points.length; i += 2) {
      xs.push(el.points[i]);
      ys.push(el.points[i + 1]);
    }
    const minX = Math.min(...xs);
    const minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
    const maxY = Math.max(...ys);
    return { x: el.x + minX, y: el.y + minY, w: maxX - minX, h: maxY - minY };
  }
  return { x: el.x, y: el.y, w: el.width, h: el.height };
}

export function hitShapeEl(el: CanvasElement, px: number, py: number): boolean {
  if (el.type === 'shape') {
    if (px < el.x || py < el.y || px > el.x + el.width || py > el.y + el.height) return false;
    if (el.shape === 'circle') {
      const cx = el.x + el.width / 2;
      const cy = el.y + el.height / 2;
      const r = Math.min(el.width, el.height) / 2;
      return Math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy) <= r;
    }
    return true;
  }
  if (el.type === 'fill' && el.points && el.points.length >= 6) {
    const pts = el.points;
    const n = pts.length / 2;
    let inside = false;
    for (let i = 0, j = n - 1; i < n; j = i++) {
      const xi = el.x + pts[i * 2];
      const yi = el.y + pts[i * 2 + 1];
      const xj = el.x + pts[j * 2];
      const yj = el.y + pts[j * 2 + 1];
      const intersect =
        yi > py !== yj > py && px < ((xj - xi) * (py - yi)) / (yj - yi + 0.000001) + xi;
      if (intersect) inside = !inside;
    }
    return inside;
  }
  return false;
}

export function findElementAt(slide: Slide, x: number, y: number): CanvasElement | null {
  for (let i = slide.elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = slide.elements[i];
    if (el.type === 'line') {
      const pts = el.points || [0, el.height / 2, el.width, el.height / 2];
      const xs: number[] = [];
      const ys: number[] = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < pts.length; j += 2) {
        xs.push(el.x + pts[j]);
        ys.push(el.y + pts[j + 1]);
      }
      const bx = Math.min(...xs);
      const by = Math.min(...ys);
      const bw = Math.max(...xs) - bx;
      const bh = Math.max(...ys) - by;
      if (x >= bx - 6 && x <= bx + bw + 6 && y >= by - 6 && y <= by + bh + 6) return el;
      continue;
    }
    if (x >= el.x && x <= el.x + el.width && y >= el.y && y <= el.y + el.height) return el;
  }
  return null;
}

export const gridRound = (v: number): number => Math.round(v / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;

import type { DataRoot } from '../types';

export function resolveSample(data: DataRoot[], path: string): string | null {
  if (!path) return null;
  const clean = path.replace(/\[\d+\]/g, '');
  const parts = clean.split('.');
  const rootName = parts.shift();
  if (!rootName) return null;
  const root = data.find((d) => d.name === rootName);
  if (!root) return null;

  let fields = root.fields;
  let current: any = null;
  for (const p of parts) {
    current = fields.find((f) => f.name === p);
    if (!current) return null;
    fields = current.children || [];
  }
  return current ? current.sample : null;
}

/* Р РµСЌРєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚ РґР»СЏ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕР№ СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё. Р’СЃС‘ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ РїРµСЂРµРЅРµСЃРµРЅРѕ

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: lib\richText\layout.ts
SIZE: 5212 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/* ============================================================
   Р Р°СЃРєР»Р°РґРєР° СЂР°РЅРѕРІ РїРѕ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР°Рј.
   РџСѓСЃС‚Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° РјРµР¶РґСѓ \n\n = РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї РјРµР¶РґСѓ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°РјРё.
   РўСЂРµР№Р»РёРЅРі \n\n РќР• РґР°С‘С‚ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї РІ РєРѕРЅС†Рµ РїРѕС‚РѕРєР°.
   ============================================================ */

import type { TextRun, PositionedRun, LayoutResult } from './types';
import { measureText } from './measure';

type Token = TextRun & { width: number; fsize: number; ffamily: string; style: string };

/** РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРї РјРµР¶РґСѓ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°РјРё (РјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЊ РѕС‚ lineHeight). */
const PARAGRAPH_GAP_COEF = 0.5;

export function layoutRuns(
  runs: TextRun[],
  maxWidth: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
): LayoutResult {
  if (!runs.length || maxWidth <= 0) {
    return { lines: [], totalHeight: 0, lineHeight: 0 };
  }

  let baseFontSize = 16;
  let baseFontFamily = 'Arial';
  for (const run of runs) {
    if (run.fontSize) baseFontSize = run.fontSize;
    if (run.fontFamily) baseFontFamily = run.fontFamily;
  }

  const tokens = tokenize(runs, baseFontSize, baseFontFamily);
  const lineHeight = Math.round(baseFontSize * lineHeightCoef);
  const paragraphGap = Math.round(lineHeight * PARAGRAPH_GAP_COEF);

  const lines: PositionedRun[][] = [];
  let currentLine: PositionedRun[] = [];
  let cursorX = 0;
  let cursorY = 0;
  let pendingNewlines = 0;

  const flushLine = () => {
    if (currentLine.length) {
      lines.push(currentLine);
      currentLine = [];
      cursorX = 0;
      cursorY += lineHeight;
    }
  };

  /**
   * РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј РЅР°РєРѕРїР»РµРЅРЅС‹Рµ \n.
   *  вЂў РѕРґРёРЅ \n вЂ” РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ РїРµСЂРµРЅРѕСЃ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё
   *  вЂў РґРІР° Рё Р±РѕР»РµРµ вЂ” РїРµСЂРµРЅРѕСЃ + paragraphGap (РЅРѕ РќР• РІ РєРѕРЅС†Рµ РїРѕС‚РѕРєР°)
   *  вЂў РІ СЃР°РјРѕРј РЅР°С‡Р°Р»Рµ вЂ” РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј
   */
  const applyPendingNewlines = (isEnd: boolean) => {
    if (pendingNewlines === 0) return;

    /* Р’ РЅР°С‡Р°Р»Рµ РїРѕС‚РѕРєР° вЂ” РІРѕРѕР±С‰Рµ РЅРµ РґР°С‘Рј РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї */
    if (lines.length === 0 && currentLine.length === 0) {
      pendingNewlines = 0;
      return;
    }

    flushLine();

    /* вњ… Р’ РєРѕРЅС†Рµ РїРѕС‚РѕРєР° paragraphGap РЅРµ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј */
    if (!isEnd && pendingNewlines >= 2) {
      cursorY += paragraphGap;
    }

    pendingNewlines = 0;
  };

  const pushToken = (tk: Token, text: string, width: number) => {
    currentLine.push({
      text,
      bold: tk.bold,
      italic: tk.italic,
      underline: tk.underline,
      strike: tk.strike,
      color: tk.color,
      bgColor: tk.bgColor,
      href: tk.href,
      fontSize: tk.fsize,
      fontFamily: tk.ffamily,
      x: cursorX,
      y: cursorY,
      width,
      actualFontSize: tk.fsize,
    });
    cursorX += width;
  };

  for (const tk of tokens) {
    if (tk.text === '\n') {
      pendingNewlines++;
      continue;
    }

    const isSpace = /^\s+$/.test(tk.text);
    if (isSpace && cursorX === 0 && pendingNewlines === 0) continue;

    applyPendingNewlines(false);

    if (cursorX + tk.width <= maxWidth) {
      pushToken(tk, tk.text, tk.width);
      continue;
    }

    if (cursorX > 0) flushLine();

    if (isSpace) continue;

    if (tk.width > maxWidth) {
      const chars = Array.from(tk.text);
      let chunk = '';
      let chunkWidth = 0;

      for (const ch of chars) {
        const cw = measureText(ch, tk.fsize, tk.ffamily, tk.style);
        if (chunkWidth + cw > maxWidth && chunk.length > 0) {
          pushToken(tk, chunk, chunkWidth);
          flushLine();
          chunk = ch;
          chunkWidth = cw;
        } else {
          chunk += ch;
          chunkWidth += cw;
        }
      }

      if (chunk.length) pushToken(tk, chunk, chunkWidth);
      continue;
    }

    pushToken(tk, tk.text, tk.width);
  }

  /* вњ… Р¤РёРЅР°Р» вЂ” РѕР±СЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµРј РєР°Рє isEnd (Р±РµР· paragraphGap) */
  applyPendingNewlines(true);
  flushLine();

  return { lines, totalHeight: cursorY, lineHeight };
}

/* ============================================================ */

function tokenize(
  runs: TextRun[],
  baseFontSize: number,
  baseFontFamily: string,
): Token[] {
  const tokens: Token[] = [];

  for (const run of runs) {
    const fs = run.fontSize ?? baseFontSize;
    const ff = run.fontFamily ?? baseFontFamily;
    const style =
      run.bold && run.italic ? 'italic bold'
      : run.bold ? 'bold'
      : run.italic ? 'italic'
      : 'normal';

    for (const part of run.text.split(/(\n)/)) {
      if (part === '') continue;
      if (part === '\n') {
        tokens.push({ ...run, text: '\n', width: 0, fsize: fs, ffamily: ff, style });
        continue;
      }
      for (const w of part.split(/(\s+)/)) {
        if (w === '') continue;
        tokens.push({
          ...run,
          text: w,
          width: measureText(w, fs, ff, style),
          fsize: fs,
          ffamily: ff,
          style,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  return tokens;
}

/* ============================================================
   РР·РјРµСЂРµРЅРёРµ С€РёСЂРёРЅС‹ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° С‡РµСЂРµР· offscreen canvas.
   ============================================================ */

let measureCtx: CanvasRenderingContext2D | null | undefined;

function getCtx(): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null {
  if (measureCtx === undefined) {
    measureCtx =
      typeof document !== 'undefined'
        ? document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d')
        : null;
  }
  return measureCtx;
}

export function measureText(
  text: string,
  fontSize: number,
  fontFamily: string,
  fontStyle: string,
): number {
  const ctx = getCtx();
  if (!ctx) return text.length * fontSize * 0.55;
  const style = fontStyle || 'normal';
  ctx.font = `${style} ${fontSize}px ${fontFamily}, Arial, sans-serif`;
  return ctx.measureText(text).width;
}

FILE: lib\richText\parser\blocks.ts
SIZE: 4145 bytes

import type { BaseStyle, RichBlock } from '../types';
import { parseHtmlToRuns } from './runs';
import { parseTableEl } from './table';

/* ============================================================
   HTML в†’ RichBlock[]  (С‚РµРєСЃС‚ + С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹ РІРµСЂС…РЅРµРіРѕ СѓСЂРѕРІРЅСЏ)
   ============================================================ */

export function parseHtmlToBlocks(html: string, base: BaseStyle): RichBlock[] {
  if (!html) return [];
  if (typeof DOMParser === 'undefined') {
    const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(html, base);
    return runs.length ? [{ kind: 'text', runs }] : [];
  }

  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
  return parseNodesToBlocks(doc.body.childNodes, base);
}

/** Р Р°Р·Р±РѕСЂ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРіРѕ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹. */
export function parseCellBlocks(cellEl: HTMLElement, base: BaseStyle): RichBlock[] {
  const clone = cellEl.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;
  clone.querySelectorAll('p').forEach((p) => {
    if (!p.textContent?.trim() && !p.querySelector('br, table')) p.remove();
  });
  while (clone.firstChild && (clone.firstChild as Element).nodeName === 'BR') {
    clone.removeChild(clone.firstChild);
  }
  while (clone.lastChild && (clone.lastChild as Element).nodeName === 'BR') {
    clone.removeChild(clone.lastChild);
  }
  return parseNodesToBlocks(clone.childNodes, base);
}

/* ============================================================
   Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРµРµ
   ============================================================ */

/** РўРµРіРё-РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєРё, РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹С… РјС‹ СЃРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРјСЃСЏ, РµСЃР»Рё С‚Р°Рј РµСЃС‚СЊ <table>. */
const CONTAINER_TAGS = new Set([
  'div', 'section', 'article', 'main', 'aside', 'figure',
  'blockquote', 'header', 'footer', 'nav', 'span',
]);

function parseNodesToBlocks(
  nodes: NodeListOf<ChildNode> | ChildNode[],
  base: BaseStyle,
): RichBlock[] {
  const blocks: RichBlock[] = [];
  let pendingHtml = '';

  const flushText = () => {
    if (!pendingHtml.trim()) { pendingHtml = ''; return; }
    const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(pendingHtml, base);
    if (runs.length) blocks.push({ kind: 'text', runs });
    pendingHtml = '';
  };

  const visit = (node: Node) => {
    if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
      pendingHtml += (node.textContent ?? '')
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return;
    }
    if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
      // РєРѕРјРјРµРЅС‚С‹, CDATA Рё С‚.Рї. вЂ” РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј
      return;
    }

    const el = node as HTMLElement;
    const tag = el.tagName.toLowerCase();

    /* ---------- РўР°Р±Р»РёС†Р° ---------- */
    if (tag === 'table') {
      flushText();
      try {
        const data = parseTableEl(el, base, parseCellBlocks);
        if (data && data.cells.length) {
          blocks.push({ kind: 'table', data });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        // Р•СЃР»Рё РїР°СЂСЃРµСЂ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹ СѓРїР°Р» вЂ” РЅРµ С‚РµСЂСЏРµРј С‚РµРєСЃС‚,
        // СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂРёРј СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ СЏС‡РµРµРє РєР°Рє РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚.
        console.error('[richText] parseTableEl failed:', err);
        const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(el.textContent ?? '', base);
        if (runs.length) blocks.push({ kind: 'text', runs });
      }
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- РћР±С‘СЂС‚РєР°, РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕР№ РµСЃС‚СЊ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р° ----------
       TipTap РѕР±РѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°РµС‚ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Сѓ РІ <div class="tableWrapper">.
       Р—Р°С…РѕРґРёРј РІРЅСѓС‚СЂСЊ Рё РѕР±СЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµРј childNodes РїРѕ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєСѓ. */
    if (CONTAINER_TAGS.has(tag) && el.querySelector('table')) {
      for (const child of Array.from(el.childNodes)) {
        visit(child);
      }
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- РћР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ СѓР·РµР» вЂ” РєР°Рє HTML РІ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Р№ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ ---------- */
    pendingHtml += el.outerHTML;
  };

  for (const node of Array.from(nodes)) {
    visit(node);
  }
  flushText();

  return blocks;
}

export { parseHtmlToBlocks, parseCellBlocks } from './blocks';
export { parseTableEl } from './table';

FILE: lib\richText\parser\runs.ts

import type { TextRun, BaseStyle } from '../types';

/* ============================================================
   HTML в†’ TextRun[]  (Р±РµР· С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†)
   ============================================================ */

export function parseHtmlToRuns(html: string, base: BaseStyle): TextRun[] {
  if (!html) return [];
  if (typeof DOMParser === 'undefined') {
    return [{ ...baseToRun(base), text: html.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '') }];
  }

  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
  const runs: TextRun[] = [];

  /** РћРґРёРЅРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№ РїРµСЂРµРЅРѕСЃ вЂ” РґР»СЏ <br> Рё РјРµР¶РґСѓ РїСѓРЅРєС‚Р°РјРё СЃРїРёСЃРєР°. */
  const pushLine = (style: Partial<TextRun>) => {
    if (runs.length && runs[runs.length - 1].text.endsWith('\n')) return;
    runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n' });
  };

  /**
   * РђР±Р·Р°С†РЅС‹Р№ РїРµСЂРµРЅРѕСЃ вЂ” РџРћРЎР›Р• РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ Р±Р»РѕС‡РЅРѕРіРѕ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р°.
   * Р”Р°С‘С‚ РґРІРѕР№РЅРѕР№ \n, С‡С‚Рѕ РІ layoutRuns = РїСѓСЃС‚Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° = РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї РјРµР¶РґСѓ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°РјРё.
   */
  const pushParagraphBreak = (style: Partial<TextRun>) => {
    const last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    if (last && last.text.endsWith('\n\n')) return;
    if (last && last.text.endsWith('\n')) {
      runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n' });
      return;
    }
    runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n\n' });
  };

  /** РњР°СЂРєРµСЂ СЃРїРёСЃРєР°. 6 РїСЂРѕР±РµР»РѕРІ РЅР° СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ РІР»РѕР¶РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё. */
  const makeListMarker = (depth: number, ordered: boolean, index: number): string => {
    const indent = '      '.repeat(Math.max(0, depth));
    const bullet = ordered ? `${index + 1}. ` : 'вЂў ';
    return indent + bullet;
  };

  function walk(node: Node, style: Partial<TextRun>, listDepth = 0) {
    if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
      const text = node.textContent || '';
      if (text) runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text });
      return;
    }
    if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) return;

    const el = node as HTMLElement;
    const tag = el.tagName.toLowerCase();

    if (tag === 'br') {
      pushLine(style);
      return;
    }
    if (tag === 'table') return;

    /* ---------- РЎРїРёСЃРєРё: ul / ol ---------- */
    if (tag === 'ul' || tag === 'ol') {
      const ordered = tag === 'ol';
      const liList = Array.from(el.children).filter(
        (c) => (c as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'li',
      );
      liList.forEach((li, idx) => {
        const marker = makeListMarker(listDepth, ordered, idx);
        runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: marker });

        li.childNodes.forEach((child) => {
          if (child.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
            const ct = (child as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase();
            if (ct === 'p') {
              child.childNodes.forEach((c) => walk(c, style, listDepth));
              return;
            }
            if (ct === 'ul' || ct === 'ol') {
              pushLine(style);
              walk(child, style, listDepth + 1);
              return;
            }
          }
          walk(child, style, listDepth);
        });

        pushLine(style);
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєРё ---------- */
    const next: Partial<TextRun> = { ...style };
    const baseFontSize = base.fontSize || 16;
    if (tag === 'h1') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.75); next.bold = true; }
    else if (tag === 'h2') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.4); next.bold = true; }
    else if (tag === 'h3') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.15); next.bold = true; }

    /* ---------- РРЅР»Р°Р№РЅ-СЃС‚РёР»Рё С‚РµРіР°РјРё ---------- */
    if (tag === 'b' || tag === 'strong') next.bold = true;
    if (tag === 'i' || tag === 'em') next.italic = true;
    if (tag === 'u') next.underline = true;
    if (tag === 's' || tag === 'strike' || tag === 'del') next.strike = true;

    if (tag === 'a') {
      const href = el.getAttribute('href') || '';
      if (href) next.href = href;
      next.underline = true;
      if (!next.color) next.color = '#2563eb';
    }
    if (tag === 'mark') {
      const bg = el.style.backgroundColor || el.getAttribute('data-color');
      if (bg) next.bgColor = bg;
    }

    /* ---------- РРЅР»Р°Р№РЅ-СЃС‚РёР»Рё С‡РµСЂРµР· style="вЂ¦" ---------- */
    const s = el.style;
    if (s) {
      if (s.color) next.color = s.color;
      if (s.backgroundColor) next.bgColor = s.backgroundColor;
      if (s.fontSize) {
        const n = parseFloat(s.fontSize);
        if (!Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0) next.fontSize = n;
      }
      if (s.fontFamily) {
        next.fontFamily = s.fontFamily.replace(/["']/g, '').split(',')[0].trim();
      }
      const fw = s.fontWeight;
      if (fw === 'bold' || (fw && parseInt(fw, 10) >= 600)) next.bold = true;
      if (s.fontStyle === 'italic') next.italic = true;
      const td = s.textDecoration || '';
      if (td.includes('underline')) next.underline = true;
      if (td.includes('line-through')) next.strike = true;
    }

    const isBlock = tag === 'p' || tag === 'div' || tag === 'h1' || tag === 'h2' || tag === 'h3';

    if (isBlock) {
      el.childNodes.forEach((child) => walk(child, next, listDepth));
      pushParagraphBreak(style);
      return;
    }

    el.childNodes.forEach((child) => walk(child, next, listDepth));
  }

  walk(doc.body, {});

  while (runs.length && runs[0].text.trim() === '') runs.shift();
  while (runs.length && runs[runs.length - 1].text.trim() === '') runs.pop();

  return runs;
}

/* ============================================================ */

export function baseToRun(base: BaseStyle): Omit<TextRun, 'text'> {
  return {
    bold: base.fontStyle.includes('bold'),
    italic: base.fontStyle.includes('italic'),
    underline: base.textDecoration.includes('underline'),
    strike: base.textDecoration.includes('line-through'),
    color: base.fill,
    fontSize: base.fontSize,
    fontFamily: base.fontFamily,
  };
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: lib\richText\parser\table.ts
SIZE: 11057 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import type {
  BaseStyle,
  CellAlign,
  CellVerticalAlign,
  RichTableCell,
  RichTableCellPadding,
  RichTableData,
  RichBlock,
} from '../types';

/** Callback РґР»СЏ СЂР°Р·Р±РѕСЂР° СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРіРѕ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё вЂ” РїРµСЂРµРґР°С‘С‚СЃСЏ СЃРЅР°СЂСѓР¶Рё,
 *  С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РёР·Р±РµР¶Р°С‚СЊ С†РёРєР»РёС‡РµСЃРєРѕР№ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё РјРµР¶РґСѓ blocks.ts Рё table.ts. */
export type ParseCellBlocksFn = (cellEl: HTMLElement, base: BaseStyle) => RichBlock[];

/* ============================================================
   РџР°СЂСЃРµСЂ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹: colspan/rowspan, С€РёСЂРёРЅС‹ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє, align.
   ============================================================ */

export function parseTableEl(
  table: HTMLElement,
  base: BaseStyle,
  parseCellBlocks: ParseCellBlocksFn,
): RichTableData | null {
  if (!table) return null;

  const trList = Array.from(
    table.querySelectorAll(
      ':scope > thead > tr, :scope > tbody > tr, :scope > tfoot > tr, :scope > tr',
    ),
  );

  const cells: RichTableCell[] = [];
  const grid: (RichTableCell | null)[][] = [];

  const ensureRow = (r: number) => { while (grid.length <= r) grid.push([]); };
  const isOccupied = (r: number, c: number): boolean => {
    const row = grid[r];
    if (!row) return false;
    return row[c] !== undefined && row[c] !== null;
  };

  trList.forEach((tr, r) => {
    ensureRow(r);
    let c = 0;
    while (isOccupied(r, c)) c++;

    const cellEls = Array.from(tr.childNodes).filter(
      (ch): ch is HTMLElement =>
        ch.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE &&
        ((ch as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'td' ||
          (ch as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'th'),
    );

    for (const cellEl of cellEls) {
      while (isOccupied(r, c)) c++;

      const colspan = Math.max(1, parseInt(cellEl.getAttribute('colspan') || '1', 10) || 1);
      const rowspan = Math.max(1, parseInt(cellEl.getAttribute('rowspan') || '1', 10) || 1);
      const isHeader = cellEl.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'th';

      const cell: RichTableCell = {
        blocks: parseCellBlocks(cellEl, base),
        isHeader,
        colspan,
        rowspan,
        rowIndex: r,
        colIndex: c,
        rawWidths: parseRawWidths(cellEl),
        bgColor: cellEl.style.backgroundColor || undefined,
        borderColor: cellEl.style.borderColor || undefined,
        borderWidth: parseFloatOrUndef(cellEl.style.borderWidth),
        borderStyle: cellEl.style.borderStyle || undefined,
        padding: parsePadding(cellEl),

        /* вњ… Р’С‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёРµ: СЃРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° СЃРјРѕС‚СЂРёРј РЅР° СЃР°РјРѕР№ СЏС‡РµР№РєРµ, РїРѕС‚РѕРј
           РЅР° РїРµСЂРІРѕРј Р±Р»РѕРєРµ-РїРѕС‚РѕРјРєРµ (TipTap РїРёС€РµС‚ text-align РІ <p>). */
        align: readCellAlign(cellEl, isHeader),
        verticalAlign: readCellVerticalAlign(cellEl),
      };
      cells.push(cell);

      for (let rr = 0; rr < rowspan; rr++) {
        ensureRow(r + rr);
        for (let cc = 0; cc < colspan; cc++) {
          grid[r + rr][c + cc] = cell;
        }
      }
      c += colspan;
    }
  });

  let colCount = 0;
  for (const row of grid) {
    if (!row) continue;
    for (let i = row.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (row[i] !== undefined && row[i] !== null) {
        if (i + 1 > colCount) colCount = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  if (colCount === 0) colCount = 1;

  const colWidths = resolveColWidths(table, cells, colCount);

  return { cells, rowCount: grid.length, colCount, colWidths };
}

/* ============================================================
   Р§С‚РµРЅРёРµ РІС‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёСЏ вЂ” СЃ fallback РЅР° РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ Р±Р»РѕРє РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё
   ============================================================ */

const ALIGN_VALUES: CellAlign[] = ['left', 'center', 'right', 'justify'];

function normalizeAlign(v: string | undefined): CellAlign | null {
  const s = (v || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (s === 'start') return 'left';
  if (s === 'end') return 'right';
  if (ALIGN_VALUES.includes(s as CellAlign)) return s as CellAlign;
  return null;
}

function readCellAlign(cellEl: HTMLElement, isHeader: boolean): CellAlign {
  // 1. РџСЂСЏРјРѕ РЅР° СЏС‡РµР№РєРµ
  const onCell = normalizeAlign(cellEl.style.textAlign);
  if (onCell) return onCell;

  // 2. РЈ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ Р±Р»РѕРєР°-РїРѕС‚РѕРјРєР° (РѕР±С‹С‡РЅРѕ <p>)
  //    TipTap TextAlign РїРёС€РµС‚ РЅР° РїР°СЂР°РіСЂР°С„ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё СЏС‡РµР№РєРё, Р° РЅРµ РЅР° СЃР°Рј <td>
  const firstBlock = cellEl.querySelector(
    ':scope > p, :scope > h1, :scope > h2, :scope > h3, :scope > div, :scope > blockquote, :scope > li',
  ) as HTMLElement | null;
  if (firstBlock) {
    const fromBlock = normalizeAlign(firstBlock.style.textAlign);
    if (fromBlock) return fromBlock;
  }

  // 3. РРЅРѕРіРґР° TipTap РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµС‚ align РєРѕ РІСЃРµРј <p> вЂ” РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРёРј РµС‰С‘ СЂР°Р·
  //    РЅР° СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№, РµСЃР»Рё РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ Р±Р»РѕРє вЂ” С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РІ span.
  const anyAligned = cellEl.querySelector('[style*="text-align"]') as HTMLElement | null;
  if (anyAligned) {
    const fromAny = normalizeAlign(anyAligned.style.textAlign);
    if (fromAny) return fromAny;
  }

  // 4. Р”РµС„РѕР»С‚ РїРѕ СЃРµРјР°РЅС‚РёРєРµ HTML
  return isHeader ? 'center' : 'left';
}

function readCellVerticalAlign(cellEl: HTMLElement): CellVerticalAlign {
  const onCell = (cellEl.style.verticalAlign || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (onCell === 'middle' || onCell === 'center') return 'middle';
  if (onCell === 'bottom') return 'bottom';
  if (onCell === 'top' || onCell === 'baseline') return 'top';

  // РРЅРѕРіРґР° РІРµСЂС‚РёРєР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ РІС‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёРµ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РЅР° РїРµСЂРІРѕРј Р±Р»РѕРєРµ вЂ”
  // РµСЃР»Рё С‚Р°Рє вЂ” РЅР°СЃР»РµРґСѓРµРј.
  const firstBlock = cellEl.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
  if (firstBlock) {
    const v = (firstBlock.style.verticalAlign || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (v === 'middle' || v === 'center') return 'middle';
    if (v === 'bottom') return 'bottom';
  }

  return 'top';
}

/* ============================================================
   РЁРёСЂРёРЅС‹ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє
   ============================================================ */

function resolveColWidths(
  table: HTMLElement,
  cells: RichTableCell[],
  colCount: number,
): number[] | null {
  const fromColgroup = readColgroup(table, colCount);
  if (fromColgroup && fromColgroup.hasRealWidth) {
    return fromColgroup.widths;
  }

  const fromCells = readCellWidths(cells, colCount);
  if (fromCells) return fromCells;

  if (fromColgroup) return fromColgroup.widths;

  return null;
}

function readColgroup(
  table: HTMLElement,
  colCount: number,
): { widths: number[]; hasRealWidth: boolean } | null {
  const colEls = table.querySelectorAll(':scope > colgroup > col');
  if (colEls.length !== colCount) return null;

  const widths: number[] = new Array(colCount).fill(NaN);
  let hasRealWidth = false;

  colEls.forEach((col, i) => {
    const colEl = col as HTMLElement;
    const w = parseFloat(colEl.style.width);
    const mw = parseFloat(colEl.style.minWidth);
    const aw = parseFloat(colEl.getAttribute('width') || '');

    if (!Number.isNaN(w) && w > 0) {
      widths[i] = w;
      hasRealWidth = true;
    } else if (!Number.isNaN(aw) && aw > 0) {
      widths[i] = aw;
      hasRealWidth = true;
    } else if (!Number.isNaN(mw) && mw > 0) {
      widths[i] = mw;
    }
  });

  fillMissingWithAvg(widths, colCount);
  return { widths, hasRealWidth };
}

function readCellWidths(cells: RichTableCell[], colCount: number): number[] | null {
  const widths: number[] = new Array(colCount).fill(NaN);

  for (const cell of cells) {
    if (!cell.rawWidths) continue;
    if (cell.rawWidths.length === cell.colspan) {
      for (let i = 0; i < cell.colspan; i++) {
        const col = cell.colIndex + i;
        if (col < colCount && Number.isNaN(widths[col])) {
          widths[col] = cell.rawWidths[i];
        }
      }
    } else if (cell.rawWidths.length === 1 && cell.colspan === 1) {
      if (Number.isNaN(widths[cell.colIndex])) {
        widths[cell.colIndex] = cell.rawWidths[0];
      }
    }
  }

  const known = widths.filter((w) => !Number.isNaN(w));
  if (!known.length) return null;

  const allSame = new Set(known).size === 1;
  const onlyOneKnown = known.length < colCount;
  if (allSame && onlyOneKnown) return null;

  fillMissingWithAvg(widths, colCount);
  return widths;
}

function fillMissingWithAvg(widths: number[], colCount: number): void {
  const known = widths.filter((w) => !Number.isNaN(w));
  const avg = known.length ? known.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / known.length : 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < colCount; i++) {
    if (Number.isNaN(widths[i])) widths[i] = avg;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   РҐРµР»РїРµСЂС‹
   ============================================================ */

function parseFloatOrUndef(v: string | null | undefined): number | undefined {
  if (!v) return undefined;
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return Number.isNaN(n) ? undefined : n;
}

export function parsePadding(el: HTMLElement): RichTableCellPadding | undefined {
  const s = el.style;
  if (!s) return undefined;

  const top = parseFloat(s.paddingTop);
  const right = parseFloat(s.paddingRight);
  const bottom = parseFloat(s.paddingBottom);
  const left = parseFloat(s.paddingLeft);
  const anySide = [top, right, bottom, left].some((n) => !Number.isNaN(n));

  let p: RichTableCellPadding | null = null;
  if (anySide) {
    p = {
      top: Number.isNaN(top) ? 0 : top,
      right: Number.isNaN(right) ? 0 : right,
      bottom: Number.isNaN(bottom) ? 0 : bottom,
      left: Number.isNaN(left) ? 0 : left,
    };
  }

  const shorthand = s.padding;
  if (shorthand) {
    const parts = shorthand.trim().split(/\s+/).map((x) => parseFloat(x) || 0);
    if (parts.length === 1) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[0], bottom: parts[0], left: parts[0] };
    else if (parts.length === 2) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[0], left: parts[1] };
    else if (parts.length === 3) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[2], left: parts[1] };
    else if (parts.length >= 4) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[2], left: parts[3] };
  }

  return p ?? undefined;
}

function parseRawWidths(cellEl: HTMLElement): number[] | null {
  const fromAttr = cellEl.getAttribute('colwidth');
  if (fromAttr) {
    const arr = fromAttr
      .split(',')
      .map((x) => parseFloat(x.trim()))
      .filter((n) => !Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0);
    if (arr.length) return arr;
  }
  const sw = cellEl.style?.width;
  if (sw) {
    const n = parseFloat(sw);
    if (!Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0) return [n];
  }
  return null;
}

/* ============================================================
   Fallback: plain text в†’ HTML РґР»СЏ РµРґРёРЅРѕРѕР±СЂР°Р·РЅРѕРіРѕ РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР°.
   ============================================================ */

export function plainToHtml(text: string): string {
  const esc = (text || '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  return `<p>${esc.replace(/\n/g, '<br>')}</p>`;
}

export function loadCustomStamps(): StampTemplate[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : [];
  } catch { return []; }
}

export function saveCustomStamps(stamps: StampTemplate[]): void {
  try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(stamps)); } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

export function getAllStamps(): StampTemplate[] {
  return [...BUILTIN_STAMPS, ...loadCustomStamps()];
}

export function addCustomStamp(t: StampTemplate): void {
  const list = loadCustomStamps();
  list.push({ ...t, custom: true });
  saveCustomStamps(list);
}

export function removeCustomStamp(id: string): void {
  const list = loadCustomStamps().filter((s) => s.id !== id);
  saveCustomStamps(list);
}

import type { DashType } from '../../types';

export const STAMP_DASH_MAP: Record<DashType, number[] | undefined> = {
  solid: undefined,
  dashed: [10, 6],
  dotted: [2, 5],
  dashdot: [10, 5, 2, 5],
  longdash: [20, 8],
};

export const konvaStyle = (s: string): string => {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (s.includes('bold')) parts.push('bold');
  if (s.includes('italic')) parts.push('italic');
  return parts.join(' ') || 'normal';
};

export const canvasFont = (family: string, style: string, size: number): string => {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (style.includes('italic')) parts.push('italic');
  if (style.includes('bold')) parts.push('bold');
  parts.push(`${size}px`);
  parts.push(family);
  return parts.join(' ');
};

export interface ArcTextParams {
  text: string;
  cx: number;
  cy: number;
  radius: number;
  startDeg: number;
  endDeg: number;
  flip: boolean;
  font: string;
  fill: string;
}

export function drawArcText(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: ArcTextParams): void {
  if (!p.text) return;
  const start = (p.startDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const end = (p.endDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const chars = p.text.split('');
  const step = chars.length > 1 ? (end - start) / (chars.length - 1) : 0;

  ctx.save();
  ctx.font = p.font;
  ctx.fillStyle = p.fill;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

  chars.forEach((ch, i) => {
    const a = start + i * step;
    const x = p.cx + Math.cos(a) * p.radius;
    const y = p.cy + Math.sin(a) * p.radius;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(x, y);
    ctx.rotate(a + (p.flip ? -Math.PI / 2 : Math.PI / 2));
    ctx.fillText(ch, 0, 0);
    ctx.restore();
  });

  ctx.restore();
}

import React, { useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { useEditor, EditorContent } from '@tiptap/react';
import { buildEditorExtensions } from './components/richText/extensions';
import { RichTextToolbar } from './components/richText/RichTextToolbar';

interface Props {
  initialHtml: string;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fill: string;
  align: string;
  /** вњ… РРЅС‚РµСЂР»РёРЅСЊСЏР¶ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р° (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, 1.2) вЂ” СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·РёСЂСѓРµРј СЃ РєР°РЅРІР°СЃРѕРј */
  lineHeight?: number;
  /** вњ… РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРї РјРµР¶РґСѓ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°РјРё (РІ px) вЂ” СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·РёСЂСѓРµРј СЃ РєР°РЅРІР°СЃРѕРј */
  blockGap?: number;
  width: number;
  minHeight: number;
  onChange: (html: string, text: string) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const RichTextEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({
  initialHtml,
  fontSize,
  fontFamily,
  fill,
  align,
  lineHeight = 1.2,
  blockGap = 8,
  width,
  minHeight,
  onChange,
  onCommit,
  onCancel,
}) => {
  const editor = useEditor({
    extensions: buildEditorExtensions(align),
    content: initialHtml || '<p></p>',
    autofocus: 'end',
    onUpdate: ({ editor }) => onChange(editor.getHTML(), editor.getText()),
    onBlur: ({ editor }) => {
      try { editor.view.dispatch(editor.state.tr); } catch { /* noop */ }
      onChange(editor.getHTML(), editor.getText());
    },
    editorProps: {
      attributes: {
        spellcheck: 'true',
        style: [
          `font-size:${fontSize}px`,
          `font-family:${fontFamily}, Arial, sans-serif`,
          `color:${fill}`,
          `line-height:${lineHeight}`,
          `--block-gap:${blockGap}px`,
        ].join(';') + ';',
      },
    },
  });

  const contentRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  /* РЈРЅРёС‡С‚РѕР¶Р°РµРј СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ РїСЂРё СЂР°Р·РјРѕРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё */
  useEffect(() => () => { editor?.destroy(); }, [editor]);

  /* Р¤РѕРєСѓСЃРёСЂСѓРµРј ProseMirror РїСЂРё РјРѕРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё вЂ” С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ Ctrl+A СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°Р» РІ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРµ */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editor) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      try { editor.commands.focus('end'); } catch { /* noop */ }
    }, 0);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [editor]);

  /* Р¤РѕСЂСЃРёСЂСѓРµРј РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ view-СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёСЏ Рє РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Сѓ РїРµСЂРµРґ С„РёРєСЃР°С†РёРµР№ html */
  const flushAndGetHtml = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editor) return { html: '', text: '' };
    try { editor.view.dispatch(editor.state.tr); } catch { /* noop */ }
    return { html: editor.getHTML(), text: editor.getText() };
  }, [editor]);

  const handleCommit = useCallback(() => {
    const { html, text } = flushAndGetHtml();
    onChange(html, text);
    setTimeout(onCommit, 0);
  }, [flushAndGetHtml, onChange, onCommit]);

  /* Р›СЋР±РѕР№ РєР»РёРє РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ РїРµСЂРµРІРѕРґРёС‚ С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ ProseMirror */
  const handleContentMouseDown = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
    const isInToolbar = target.closest('.rt-toolbar');
    if (isInToolbar) return;
    if (target.closest('.ProseMirror')) return;
    if (!editor) return;
    setTimeout(() => {
      try { editor.commands.focus(); } catch { /* noop */ }
    }, 0);
  }, [editor]);

  if (!editor) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="rt-editor"
      style={{ width, minHeight }}
      onMouseDown={handleContentMouseDown}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        /* Escape / Ctrl+Enter вЂ” РЅРµ РјРµС€Р°РµРј Ctrl+A Рё РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРјСѓ РЅР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕРјСѓ РїРѕРІРµРґРµРЅРёСЋ */
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          onCancel();
          return;
        }
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleCommit();
          return;
        }
        /* Ctrl+A РЅРµ РїРµСЂРµС…РІР°С‚С‹РІР°РµРј вЂ” ProseMirror СЃР°Рј РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ */
      }}
    >
      <RichTextToolbar
        editor={editor}
        fallbackFontFamily={fontFamily}
        fallbackFontSize={fontSize}
        fallbackColor={fill}
      />
      <div className="rt-content" ref={contentRef}>
        <EditorContent editor={editor} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: services\snap.ts

import type { CanvasElement, Slide } from '../types';
import { SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '../types';
import { gridRound } from '../lib/geometry';

export interface SnapResult {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  v: number[];
  h: number[];
}

export function computeSnap(
  el: CanvasElement,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide,
  showGrid: boolean,
): SnapResult {
  const v: number[] = [];
  const h: number[] = [];
  const bw = el.width;
  const bh = el.height;
  const xs: { v: number; d: number; guide: number }[] = [];
  const ys: { v: number; d: number; guide: number }[] = [];

  if (showGrid) {
    const gx = gridRound(x);
    const gy = gridRound(y);
    xs.push({ v: gx, d: Math.abs(x - gx), guide: gx });
    ys.push({ v: gy, d: Math.abs(y - gy), guide: gy });
  }

  const cx = x + bw / 2;
  const cy = y + bh / 2;
  const stageW = slide.width;
  const stageH = slide.height;

  xs.push({ v: stageW / 2 - bw / 2, d: Math.abs(cx - stageW / 2), guide: stageW / 2 });
  xs.push({ v: 0, d: Math.abs(x), guide: 0 });
  xs.push({ v: stageW - bw, d: Math.abs(x + bw - stageW), guide: stageW });

  const pad = slide.padding;
  xs.push({ v: pad.left, d: Math.abs(x - pad.left), guide: pad.left });
  xs.push({ v: stageW - pad.right - bw, d: Math.abs(x + bw - (stageW - pad.right)), guide: stageW - pad.right });

  ys.push({ v: pad.top, d: Math.abs(y - pad.top), guide: pad.top });
  ys.push({ v: stageH - pad.bottom - bh, d: Math.abs(y + bh - (stageH - pad.bottom)), guide: stageH - pad.bottom });
  ys.push({ v: stageH / 2 - bh / 2, d: Math.abs(cy - stageH / 2), guide: stageH / 2 });
  ys.push({ v: 0, d: Math.abs(y), guide: 0 });
  ys.push({ v: stageH - bh, d: Math.abs(y + bh - stageH), guide: stageH });

  for (const other of slide.elements) {
    if (other.id === el.id) continue;
    const oL = other.x;
    const oR = other.x + other.width;
    const oC = other.x + other.width / 2;
    const oT = other.y;
    const oB = other.y + other.height;
    const oM = other.y + other.height / 2;
    const l = x;
    const r = x + bw;
    const c = x + bw / 2;
    const t = y;
    const b = y + bh;
    const m = y + bh / 2;

    xs.push({ v: oL, d: Math.abs(l - oL), guide: oL });
    xs.push({ v: oR - bw, d: Math.abs(r - oR), guide: oR });
    xs.push({ v: oC - bw / 2, d: Math.abs(c - oC), guide: oC });
    ys.push({ v: oT, d: Math.abs(t - oT), guide: oT });
    ys.push({ v: oB - bh, d: Math.abs(b - oB), guide: oB });
    ys.push({ v: oM - bh / 2, d: Math.abs(m - oM), guide: oM });
  }

  const bestX = xs.reduce((a, b) => (b.d < a.d ? b : a), xs[0]);
  const bestY = ys.reduce((a, b) => (b.d < a.d ? b : a), ys[0]);
  if (bestX && bestX.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD) { x = bestX.v; v.push(bestX.guide); }
  if (bestY && bestY.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD) { y = bestY.v; h.push(bestY.guide); }

  return { x, y, v, h };
}
