import type {
  Slide, StampElement, TextElement, PlaceholderElement, LineElement,
  FillElement, DataFieldType, DashType,
} from '../types';

const escape = (s: string): string =>
  s.replace(/[<>&'"]/g, (c) => ({ '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '&': '&amp;', "'": '&apos;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]!));

const dashSvg = (d: DashType): string =>
  d === 'solid' ? '' : ` stroke-dasharray="${
    d === 'dashed' ? '10,6' : d === 'dotted' ? '2,5' : d === 'dashdot' ? '10,5,2,5' : '20,8'}"`;

const fontFamilyAttr = (f?: string): string => {
  const name = f || 'Arial';
  return name.includes(' ') ? `'${name}'` : name;
};

/* ============================================================
   РџРµСЂРµРЅРѕСЃ СЃС‚СЂРѕРє вЂ” РєР°Рє Konva.Text
   ============================================================ */

function wrapLines(
  text: string,
  maxWidth: number,
  fontSize: number,
  fontFamily: string,
  fontStyle: string,
): string[] {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return text.split('\n');

  const ctx = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) return text.split('\n');

  const weight = fontStyle.includes('bold') ? 'bold' : 'normal';
  const italic = fontStyle.includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal';
  ctx.font = `${italic} ${weight} ${fontSize}px ${fontFamily}, Arial, sans-serif`;

  const measure = (s: string) => ctx.measureText(s).width;

  const result: string[] = [];

  for (const paragraph of text.split('\n')) {
    if (paragraph === '') { result.push(''); continue; }

    const parts = paragraph.split(/(\s+)/);
    let line = '';

    for (const part of parts) {
      const candidate = line + part;
      if (measure(candidate) <= maxWidth) {
        line = candidate;
        continue;
      }

      if (line.length) {
        result.push(line.replace(/\s+$/, ''));
        line = '';
      }

      if (/^\s+$/.test(part)) continue;

      if (measure(part) <= maxWidth) {
        line = part;
        continue;
      }

      let chunk = '';
      for (const ch of part) {
        if (measure(chunk + ch) > maxWidth && chunk.length > 0) {
          result.push(chunk);
          chunk = ch;
        } else {
          chunk += ch;
        }
      }
      line = chunk;
    }

    if (line.length) result.push(line.replace(/\s+$/, ''));
    else if (parts.length === 0) result.push('');
  }

  return result.length ? result : [''];
}

/* ============================================================
   РЈС‡С‘С‚ verticalAlign
   ============================================================ */

interface VAlignInput {
  y: number;
  height: number;
  verticalAlign?: 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
  padding?: { top: number; bottom: number };
}

function calcStartY(
  el: VAlignInput,
  linesCount: number,
  lineHeight: number,
): number {
  const pad = el.padding || { top: 0, bottom: 0 };
  const totalH = linesCount * lineHeight;
  const va = el.verticalAlign || 'top';

  if (va === 'middle') {
    return el.y + (el.height - totalH) / 2;
  }
  if (va === 'bottom') {
    return el.y + el.height - pad.bottom - totalH;
  }
  return el.y + pad.top;
}

/* ============================================================
   Stamp вЂ” РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј РѕСЃСЊ РІСЂР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ РІ С†РµРЅС‚СЂРµ (СЃРѕРІРїР°РґР°РµС‚ СЃ Konva-РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚РѕРј Stamp)
   ============================================================ */

function stampToSvg(el: StampElement): string {
  const sw = el.strokeWidth, isw = el.innerStrokeWidth, c = el.stampColor;
  const cx = el.width / 2, cy = el.height / 2;
  const t = `transform="translate(${el.x},${el.y}) rotate(${el.rotation},${cx},${cy})"`;
  let body = '';
  if (el.shape === 'circle') {
    const r = Math.min(el.width, el.height) / 2;
    body = `<circle cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" r="${r - 4}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${sw}"${dashSvg(el.strokeDash)}/>`;
    if (el.showInner) body += `<circle cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" r="${r - 20}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${isw}"${dashSvg(el.innerStrokeDash)}/>`;
  } else if (el.shape === 'oval') {
    body = `<ellipse cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" rx="${el.width / 2 - 4}" ry="${el.height / 2 - 4}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${sw}"${dashSvg(el.strokeDash)}/>`;
    if (el.showInner) body += `<ellipse cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" rx="${el.width / 2 - 20}" ry="${el.height / 2 - 20}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${isw}"${dashSvg(el.innerStrokeDash)}/>`;
  } else if (el.shape === 'rect') {
    body = `<rect x="4" y="4" width="${el.width - 8}" height="${el.height - 8}" rx="6" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${sw}"${dashSvg(el.strokeDash)}/>`;
    if (el.showInner) body += `<rect x="16" y="16" width="${el.width - 32}" height="${el.height - 32}" rx="4" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${isw}"${dashSvg(el.innerStrokeDash)}/>`;
  } else {
    body = `<polygon points="${cx},4 ${el.width - 4},${el.height - 4} 4,${el.height - 4}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${sw}" stroke-linejoin="round"${dashSvg(el.strokeDash)}/>`;
    if (el.showInner) body += `<polygon points="${cx},20 ${el.width - 20},${el.height - 20} 20,${el.height - 20}" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${isw}" stroke-linejoin="round"${dashSvg(el.innerStrokeDash)}/>`;
  }

  const topSize = el.topFontSize || 14;
  const centerSize = el.centerFontSize || 20;
  const bottomSize = el.bottomFontSize || 12;
  const topBold = (el.topFontStyle || '').includes('bold') ? 'bold' : 'normal';
  const topItalic = (el.topFontStyle || '').includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal';
  const centerBold = (el.centerFontStyle || '').includes('bold') ? 'bold' : 'normal';
  const centerItalic = (el.centerFontStyle || '').includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal';
  const botBold = (el.bottomFontStyle || '').includes('bold') ? 'bold' : 'normal';
  const botItalic = (el.bottomFontStyle || '').includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal';

  const idSafe = `s${el.id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '')}`;
  let topText = '';
  let botText = '';
  let defs = '';

  if (el.shape === 'circle' || el.shape === 'oval') {
    const r = Math.min(el.width, el.height) / 2 - 32;
    const aT1 = 1.15 * Math.PI, aT2 = 1.85 * Math.PI;
    const t1x = cx + r * Math.cos(aT1), t1y = cy + r * Math.sin(aT1);
    const t2x = cx + r * Math.cos(aT2), t2y = cy + r * Math.sin(aT2);
    const topPath = `M ${t1x} ${t1y} A ${r} ${r} 0 0 1 ${t2x} ${t2y}`;
    const aB1 = 0.85 * Math.PI, aB2 = 0.15 * Math.PI;
    const b1x = cx + r * Math.cos(aB1), b1y = cy + r * Math.sin(aB1);
    const b2x = cx + r * Math.cos(aB2), b2y = cy + r * Math.sin(aB2);
    const botPath = `M ${b1x} ${b1y} A ${r} ${r} 0 0 0 ${b2x} ${b2y}`;
    const topId = `${idSafe}-top`;
    const botId = `${idSafe}-bot`;
    defs = `<defs><path id="${topId}" d="${topPath}" fill="none"/><path id="${botId}" d="${botPath}" fill="none"/></defs>`;
    topText = `<text font-size="${topSize}" fill="${el.topColor || c}" font-family="${fontFamilyAttr(el.topFontFamily)}" font-weight="${topBold}" font-style="${topItalic}"><textPath href="#${topId}" xlink:href="#${topId}" startOffset="50%" text-anchor="middle">${escape(el.topText)}</textPath></text>`;
    botText = `<text font-size="${bottomSize}" fill="${el.bottomColor || c}" font-family="${fontFamilyAttr(el.bottomFontFamily)}" font-weight="${botBold}" font-style="${botItalic}"><textPath href="#${botId}" xlink:href="#${botId}" startOffset="50%" text-anchor="middle">${escape(el.bottomText)}</textPath></text>`;
  } else {
    const topY = el.shape === 'rect' ? 32 : 42;
    const botY = el.shape === 'rect' ? el.height - 20 : el.height - 24;
    topText = `<text x="${cx}" y="${topY}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="${topSize}" fill="${el.topColor || c}" font-family="${fontFamilyAttr(el.topFontFamily)}" font-weight="${topBold}" font-style="${topItalic}">${escape(el.topText)}</text>`;
    botText = `<text x="${cx}" y="${botY}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="${bottomSize}" fill="${el.bottomColor || c}" font-family="${fontFamilyAttr(el.bottomFontFamily)}" font-weight="${botBold}" font-style="${botItalic}">${escape(el.bottomText)}</text>`;
  }

  const centerText = `<text x="${cx}" y="${cy}" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="central" font-size="${centerSize}" fill="${el.centerColor || c}" font-family="${fontFamilyAttr(el.centerFontFamily)}" font-weight="${centerBold}" font-style="${centerItalic}">${escape(el.centerText)}</text>`;

  return `<g ${t}>${defs}${body}${topText}${centerText}${botText}</g>`;
}

/* ============================================================
   Text вЂ” вњ… РѕСЃСЊ РІСЂР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ = РІРµСЂС…РЅРёР№ Р»РµРІС‹Р№ СѓРіРѕР» (el.x, el.y)
   ============================================================ */

function textToSvg(el: TextElement): string {
  const pad = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerW = Math.max(10, el.width - pad.left - pad.right);

  const fs = el.fontStyle.includes('bold') ? 'bold' : 'normal';
  const fst = el.fontStyle.includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal';
  const anchor = el.align === 'center' ? 'middle' : el.align === 'right' ? 'end' : 'start';
  const tx = el.x + pad.left + (el.align === 'center' ? innerW / 2 : el.align === 'right' ? innerW : 0);

  const bg = el.background && el.background !== 'transparent'
    ? `<rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" fill="${el.background}"/>`
    : '';

  /* вњ… Konva.Group x,y,rotation в†’ РІСЂР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі (x, y) */
  const tr = `transform="rotate(${el.rotation},${el.x},${el.y})"`;

  const lines = wrapLines(el.text, innerW, el.fontSize, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle);
  const lineHeight = el.fontSize * (el.lineHeight || 1.2);
  const startY = calcStartY(el, lines.length, lineHeight);

  const textSvg = lines.map((ln, i) =>
    `<text x="${tx}" y="${startY + i * lineHeight}" ` +
    `dominant-baseline="text-before-edge" ` +
    `font-size="${el.fontSize}" font-family="${fontFamilyAttr(el.fontFamily)}" ` +
    `font-weight="${fs}" font-style="${fst}" text-decoration="${el.textDecoration}" ` +
    `fill="${el.fill}" text-anchor="${anchor}">${escape(ln)}</text>`
  ).join('');

  return `<g ${tr}>${bg}${textSvg}</g>`;
}

/* ============================================================
   Placeholder вЂ” вњ… С‚Рѕ Р¶Рµ
   ============================================================ */

function placeholderToSvg(el: PlaceholderElement): string {
  const pad = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerW = Math.max(10, el.width - pad.left - pad.right);
  const fs = el.fontStyle.includes('bold') ? 'bold' : 'normal';
  const fst = el.fontStyle.includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal';
  const anchor = el.align === 'center' ? 'middle' : el.align === 'right' ? 'end' : 'start';
  const tx = el.x + pad.left + (el.align === 'center' ? innerW / 2 : el.align === 'right' ? innerW : 0);

  const tr = `transform="rotate(${el.rotation},${el.x},${el.y})"`;

  const displayText = el.sample || `{{${el.fieldPath}}}`;
  const bg = el.background && el.background !== 'transparent'
    ? `<rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" fill="${el.background}"/>`
    : '';

  if (el.fieldType === 'image') {
    const src = el.sample || `{{${el.fieldPath}}}`;
    return `<g ${tr}>${bg}<image href="${escape(src)}" x="${el.x + pad.left}" y="${el.y + pad.top}" width="${innerW}" height="${Math.max(10, el.height - pad.top - pad.bottom)}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"/></g>`;
  }

  const lines = wrapLines(displayText, innerW, el.fontSize, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle);
  const lineHeight = el.fontSize * (el.lineHeight || 1.2);
  const startY = calcStartY(el, lines.length, lineHeight);

  const linesSvg = lines.map((ln, i) =>
    `<text x="${tx}" y="${startY + i * lineHeight}" ` +
    `dominant-baseline="text-before-edge" ` +
    `font-size="${el.fontSize}" font-family="${fontFamilyAttr(el.fontFamily)}" ` +
    `font-weight="${fs}" font-style="${fst}" ` +
    `fill="${el.fill}" text-anchor="${anchor}">${escape(ln)}</text>`
  ).join('');

  return `<g ${tr}>${bg}${linesSvg}</g>`;
}

/* ============================================================
   Line вЂ” вњ… РІСЂР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі (el.x, el.y)
   ============================================================ */

function lineToSvg(el: LineElement): string {
  const points = el.points && el.points.length >= 4 ? el.points : [0, el.height / 2, el.width, el.height / 2];
  const tr = `transform="translate(${el.x},${el.y}) rotate(${el.rotation})"`;
  const pts = points.join(',');
  let arrow = '';
  if (el.isArrow && points.length >= 4) {
    const n = points.length;
    const x1 = points[n - 4], y1 = points[n - 3], x2 = points[n - 2], y2 = points[n - 1];
    const angle = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
    const len = 14;
    const a1 = angle + Math.PI - 0.35;
    const a2 = angle + Math.PI + 0.35;
    arrow = `<polyline points="${x2 + len * Math.cos(a1)},${y2 + len * Math.sin(a1)} ${x2},${y2} ${x2 + len * Math.cos(a2)},${y2 + len * Math.sin(a2)}" fill="none" stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`;
  }
  return `<g ${tr}><polyline points="${pts}" fill="none" stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}"${dashSvg(el.dash)} stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>${arrow}</g>`;
}

function fillToSvg(el: FillElement): string {
  if (!el.points || el.points.length < 6) return '';
  const tr = `transform="translate(${el.x},${el.y}) rotate(${el.rotation})"`;
  return `<polygon ${tr} points="${el.points.join(',')}" fill="${el.fill}"${el.stroke ? ` stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}"` : ''}/>`;
}

/* ============================================================
   Root builder
   ============================================================ */

export function buildSvg(slide: Slide): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  parts.push(
    `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" width="${slide.width}" height="${slide.height}" viewBox="0 0 ${slide.width} ${slide.height}">`
  );

  if (slide.backgroundImage) {
    parts.push(
      `<image href="${slide.backgroundImage}" x="0" y="0" width="${slide.width}" height="${slide.height}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice"/>`
    );
  } else {
    parts.push(`<rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="${slide.background}"/>`);
  }

  const fills = slide.elements.filter((el) => el.type === 'fill');
  if (fills.length) {
    parts.push('<g opacity="0.6">');
    for (const el of fills) parts.push(fillToSvg(el as FillElement));
    parts.push('</g>');
  }

  for (const el of slide.elements) {
    if (el.type === 'fill') continue;
    if (el.type === 'text') parts.push(textToSvg(el));
    else if (el.type === 'placeholder') parts.push(placeholderToSvg(el));
    else if (el.type === 'stamp') parts.push(stampToSvg(el));
    else if (el.type === 'line') parts.push(lineToSvg(el));
    else if (el.type === 'image') {
      /* вњ… РІСЂР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі РІРµСЂС…РЅРµРіРѕ Р»РµРІРѕРіРѕ СѓРіР»Р° */
      parts.push(
        `<image href="${el.src}" x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" transform="rotate(${el.rotation},${el.x},${el.y})"/>`
      );
    } else if (el.type === 'shape') {
      const fillAttr = el.fill === 'transparent' ? 'none' : el.fill;
      /* вњ… РІСЂР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі РІРµСЂС…РЅРµРіРѕ Р»РµРІРѕРіРѕ СѓРіР»Р° */
      const tr = `transform="rotate(${el.rotation},${el.x},${el.y})"`;
      const r = el.radius || 0;
      if (el.shape === 'rect' || el.shape === 'roundRect') {
        parts.push(
          `<rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" rx="${r}" ry="${r}" fill="${fillAttr}" stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}" ${tr}/>`
        );
      } else if (el.shape === 'circle') {
        parts.push(
          `<ellipse cx="${el.x + el.width / 2}" cy="${el.y + el.height / 2}" rx="${el.width / 2}" ry="${el.height / 2}" fill="${fillAttr}" stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}" ${tr}/>`
        );
      } else if (el.shape === 'triangle') {
        parts.push(
          `<polygon points="${el.x + el.width / 2},${el.y} ${el.x + el.width},${el.y + el.height} ${el.x},${el.y + el.height}" fill="${fillAttr}" stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}" ${tr}/>`
        );
      }
      if (el.text) {
        parts.push(
          `<text x="${el.x + el.width / 2}" y="${el.y + el.height / 2}" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="central" font-size="${el.fontSize}" fill="${el.textFill}" ${tr}>${escape(el.text)}</text>`
        );
      }
    } else if (el.type === 'table') {
      const cw = el.width / el.cols;
      const ch = el.height / el.rows;
      /* вњ… РІСЂР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі РІРµСЂС…РЅРµРіРѕ Р»РµРІРѕРіРѕ СѓРіР»Р° */
      const tr = `transform="rotate(${el.rotation},${el.x},${el.y})"`;
      parts.push(`<g ${tr}>`);
      for (let r = 0; r < el.rows; r++) {
        for (let c = 0; c < el.cols; c++) {
          const bg = r === 0 ? el.headerBg : '#fff';
          parts.push(
            `<rect x="${el.x + c * cw}" y="${el.y + r * ch}" width="${cw}" height="${ch}" fill="${bg}" stroke="${el.borderColor}" stroke-width="1"/>`
          );
          const txt = el.data[r]?.[c] ?? '';
          if (txt) {
            parts.push(
              `<text x="${el.x + c * cw + 6}" y="${el.y + r * ch + el.fontSize}" font-size="${el.fontSize}" fill="#111">${escape(txt)}</text>`
            );
          }
        }
      }
      parts.push('</g>');
    } else if (el.type === 'footer') {
      /* вњ… РІСЂР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі РІРµСЂС…РЅРµРіРѕ Р»РµРІРѕРіРѕ СѓРіР»Р° */
      const tr = `transform="rotate(${el.rotation},${el.x},${el.y})"`;
      parts.push(
        `<g ${tr}><rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" fill="${el.bgColor}"/><text x="${el.x + el.width / 2}" y="${el.y + el.height / 2}" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="central" font-size="${el.fontSize}" fill="${el.fill}">${escape(el.text)}</text></g>`
      );
    }
  }
  parts.push('</svg>');
  return parts.join('\n');
}

export type { DataFieldType };

export const SlideThumb: React.FC<Props> = ({ slide, index, active, onClick, onDelete }) => (

    <div className="num">{index + 1}</div>
    <div className="name">{slide.name}</div>
    <div className="count">{slide.elements.length} СЌР».</div>
    <button className="del" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onDelete(); }}>Г—</button>
  </div>

import React from 'react';
import { Group } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampElement } from './types';
import { StampRenderer } from './components/stamps/StampRenderer';
import { StampLegacy } from './components/stamps/StampLegacy';

type Zone = 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom';

interface Props {
  el: StampElement;
  onSelect: (e?: any) => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<StampElement>) => void;
  onDblClick?: (zone: Zone, e?: any) => void;
  onDragStart?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragMove?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragEnd?: (e: any) => void;
  onContextMenu?: (e: any) => void;
  isEditingZone?: Zone | null;
}

export const Stamp: React.FC<Props> = (props) => {
  const { el } = props;

  /* РќРѕРІР°СЏ РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ */
  if (el.shapes && el.texts) {
    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id}
        name="element"
        x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
        draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; props.onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; props.onSelect(e); }}
        onDragStart={props.onDragStart}
        onDragMove={props.onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { props.onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); props.onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={props.onContextMenu}
        onTransformEnd={(e) => {
          const n = e.target;
          const sx = n.scaleX(), sy = n.scaleY();
          n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
          props.onUpdate({
            x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
            width: Math.max(40, el.width * sx),
            height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
          });
        }}
      >
        <StampRenderer
          width={el.width}
          height={el.height}
          shapes={el.shapes}
          texts={el.texts}
        />
      </Group>
    );
  }

  /* Legacy */
  return <StampLegacy {...props} />;
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: TableCellInput.tsx
SIZE: 694 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

interface Props {
  value: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  onCommit: (v: string) => void;
}

export const TableCellInput: React.FC<Props> = ({ value, placeholder, onCommit }) => {
  const [v, setV] = useState(value);

  useEffect(() => { setV(value); }, [value]);

  return (
    <input
      value={v}
      placeholder={placeholder}
      style={{ marginBottom: 4 }}
      onChange={(e) => setV(e.target.value)}
      onBlur={() => onCommit(v)}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
          onCommit(v);
          (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur();
        }
      }}
    />
  );
};

/* =========================================================
   6. РљРђРўРђР›РћР“ РўРћР’РђР РћР’ (РњР°РіРЅРёС‚)

function cardElements(x: number, y: number, idx: number): CanvasElement[] {
  const CARD_W = 238;
  const CARD_H = 290;
  const RED = '#E30611';
  const DARK = '#111111';
  const MUTED = '#777777';
  const GRAY_BG = '#F5F5F5';
  const p = `catalog.items[${idx}]`;

  return [
    makeShape({ x, y, width: CARD_W, height: CARD_H, fill: GRAY_BG, stroke: '#E5E5E5', strokeWidth: 1, radius: 10 }),
    makeShape({ x: x + 10, y: y + 10, width: CARD_W - 20, height: 130, fill: '#FFFFFF', stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0, radius: 6 }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 20, y: y + 20, width: CARD_W - 40, height: 110,
      fieldPath: `${p}.image`, fieldType: 'image',
      sample: `https://picsum.photos/seed/magnit${idx}/200/120`,
    }),

    makeShape({ x: x + 10, y: y + 10, width: 56, height: 26, fill: RED, stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0, radius: 6 }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 10, y: y + 15, width: 56, height: 20,
      fieldPath: `${p}.discount`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '-25%',
      fontSize: 13, fontStyle: 'bold', align: 'center', fill: '#FFFFFF',
    }),

    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 152, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 40,
      fieldPath: `${p}.name`, fieldType: 'string',
      sample: 'РњРѕР»РѕРєРѕ РїР°СЃС‚РµСЂРёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕРµ 3,2%',
      fontSize: 13, fontStyle: 'bold', lineHeight: 1.25, fill: DARK,
    }),

    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 200, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 32,
      fieldPath: `${p}.price`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '99 в‚Ѕ',
      fontSize: 24, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: RED,
    }),

    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 240, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 20,
      fieldPath: `${p}.oldPrice`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '129 в‚Ѕ',
      fontSize: 14, fill: MUTED, textDecoration: 'line-through',
    }),

    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 262, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 18,
      fieldPath: `${p}.unit`, fieldType: 'string', sample: 'Р·Р° 1 Р»',
      fontSize: 10, fill: MUTED,
    }),
  ];
}

import type { Slide, DataRoot, CanvasElement } from '../../types';

import type { TemplateBundle } from './types';

/* ---------- РћРґРЅР° РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєР° С‚РѕРІР°СЂР° (СЃ РїР»РµР№СЃС…РѕР»РґРµСЂР°РјРё) ---------- */
function card(x: number, y: number, idx: number): CanvasElement[] {
  const CARD_W = 238;
  const CARD_H = 290;
  const RED = '#E30611';
  const DARK = '#111111';
  const MUTED = '#777777';
  const GRAY_BG = '#F5F5F5';
  const p = `items[${idx}]`;

  return [
    /* Р¤РѕРЅ РєР°СЂС‚РѕС‡РєРё */
    makeShape({
      x, y, width: CARD_W, height: CARD_H,
      fill: GRAY_BG, stroke: '#E5E5E5', strokeWidth: 1, radius: 10,
    }),

    /* РћР±Р»Р°СЃС‚СЊ РєР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєРё */
    makeShape({
      x: x + 10, y: y + 10, width: CARD_W - 20, height: 130,
      fill: '#FFFFFF', stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0, radius: 6,
    }),
    /* РљР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєР° С‚РѕРІР°СЂР° вЂ” РїР»РµР№СЃС…РѕР»РґРµСЂ image */
    makePh({
      x: x + 20, y: y + 20, width: CARD_W - 40, height: 110,
      fieldPath: `${p}.image`, fieldType: 'image',
      sample: 'https://picsum.photos/seed/p' + idx + '/200/120',
    }),

    /* Р‘РµР№РґР¶ СЃРєРёРґРєРё */
    makeShape({
      x: x + 10, y: y + 10, width: 56, height: 26,
      fill: RED, stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0, radius: 6,
    }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 10, y: y + 15, width: 56, height: 20,
      fieldPath: `${p}.discount`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '-25%',
      fontSize: 13, fontStyle: 'bold', align: 'center', fill: '#FFFFFF',
    }),

    /* РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ */
    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 152, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 40,
      fieldPath: `${p}.name`, fieldType: 'string',
      sample: 'РњРѕР»РѕРєРѕ РїР°СЃС‚РµСЂРёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕРµ 3,2%',
      fontSize: 13, fontStyle: 'bold', lineHeight: 1.25, fill: DARK,
    }),

    /* Р¦РµРЅР° */
    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 200, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 32,
      fieldPath: `${p}.price`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '99 в‚Ѕ',
      fontSize: 24, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: RED,
    }),

    /* РЎС‚Р°СЂР°СЏ С†РµРЅР° */
    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 240, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 20,
      fieldPath: `${p}.oldPrice`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '129 в‚Ѕ',
      fontSize: 14, fill: MUTED, textDecoration: 'line-through',
    }),

    /* Р•РґРёРЅРёС†Р° РёР·РјРµСЂРµРЅРёСЏ */
    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 262, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 18,
      fieldPath: `${p}.unit`, fieldType: 'string', sample: 'Р·Р° 1 С€С‚.',
      fontSize: 10, fill: MUTED,
    }),
  ];
}

import type { Slide, DataRoot } from '../../types';

export interface TemplateBundle {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  icon: string;

  build: () => { slide: Slide; data: DataRoot[] };

function yellowStripe(x: number, y: number, w: number, rotation: number) {
  return makeShape({
    x, y, width: w, height: 46,
    fill: '#FFDD00', stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0,
    rotation, radius: 0,
  });
}

import type {
  TextElement, ShapeElement, PlaceholderElement,
  TableElement, LineElement, FooterElement,
} from '../types';

export const uid = () => Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);

export function makeText(o: Partial<TextElement> = {}): TextElement {
  const base: TextElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'text', x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, height: 30, rotation: 0,
    text: '', fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fill: '#111',
    fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
    align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
    padding: { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
    background: 'transparent',
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeShape(o: Partial<ShapeElement> = {}): ShapeElement {
  const base: ShapeElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'shape', shape: 'rect',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100, rotation: 0,
    fill: 'transparent', stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0,
    text: '', fontSize: 16, textFill: '#111', radius: 0,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makePh(o: Partial<PlaceholderElement> = {}): PlaceholderElement {
  const base: PlaceholderElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'placeholder',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, height: 30, rotation: 0,
    fieldPath: '', fieldType: 'string', sample: '', label: '',
    inline: true,
    fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fill: '#111',
    fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
    align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
    padding: { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
    background: 'transparent',
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeTable(o: Partial<TableElement> = {}): TableElement {
  const base: TableElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'table',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 400, height: 120, rotation: 0,
    rows: 3, cols: 3,
    data: [['A', 'B', 'C'], ['', '', ''], ['', '', '']],
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1', headerBg: '#e2e8f0', fontSize: 13,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeLine(o: Partial<LineElement> = {}): LineElement {
  const base: LineElement = {

    points: [0, 10, 300, 10],

  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeFooter(o: Partial<FooterElement> = {}): FooterElement {
  const base: FooterElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'footer',
    x: 0, y: 1000, width: 794, height: 40, rotation: 0,
    text: 'РћРћРћ В«РљРѕРјРїР°РЅРёСЏВ» В· +7 (000) 000-00-00',

/**
 * Р¤СѓС‚РµСЂ, РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅРЅС‹Р№ Рє РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё РїРµС‡Р°С‚Рё СЃР»Р°Р№РґР°.
 *   x      = padding.left
 *   width  = slideW - padding.left - padding.right
 *   y      = slideH - padding.bottom - height
 *
 * РџРѕР»СЏ x/y/width РІ `o` РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ вЂ” РѕРЅРё РІС‹С‡РёСЃР»СЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ.
 */
export function makeFooterInPrintArea(
  slideW: number,
  slideH: number,
  padding: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number },
  o: Partial<FooterElement> = {},
): FooterElement {
  const height = o.height ?? 40;
  const { x: _x, y: _y, width: _w, ...rest } = o;
  return {
    id: uid(), type: 'footer',
    x: padding.left,
    y: slideH - padding.bottom - height,
    width: Math.max(0, slideW - padding.left - padding.right),
    height,
    rotation: 0,
    text: 'РћРћРћ В«РљРѕРјРїР°РЅРёСЏВ» В· www.example.com',
    fill: '#ffffff', bgColor: '#1e293b', fontSize: 12,
    ...rest,
  };
}

FILE: TextToolbar.tsx

import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  fill: string;
  align: string;
  onChange: (patch: any) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
}

const FONTS = ['Arial', 'Times New Roman', 'Courier New', 'Georgia', 'Verdana', 'Comic Sans MS'];
const SIZES = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 24, 28, 32, 36, 48, 64, 72];

export const TextToolbar: React.FC<Props> = ({
  x, y, fontSize, fontFamily, fontStyle, textDecoration, fill, align,
  onChange, onCommit,
}) => {
  const isBold = fontStyle.includes('bold');
  const isItalic = fontStyle.includes('italic');
  const isUnderline = textDecoration === 'underline';

  const toggleBold = () => {
    const parts = fontStyle.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
    const idx = parts.indexOf('bold');
    if (idx >= 0) parts.splice(idx, 1); else parts.push('bold');
    onChange({ fontStyle: parts.join(' ') || 'normal' });
  };
  const toggleItalic = () => {
    const parts = fontStyle.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
    const idx = parts.indexOf('italic');
    if (idx >= 0) parts.splice(idx, 1); else parts.push('italic');
    onChange({ fontStyle: parts.join(' ') || 'normal' });
  };
  const toggleUnderline = () => {
    onChange({ textDecoration: isUnderline ? '' : 'underline' });
  };

  // РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ РєРЅРѕРїРѕРє: СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ С„РѕРєСѓСЃ textarea.
  // РќР° select Рё color РќР• РІРµС€Р°РµРј, РёРЅР°С‡Рµ Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ РЅРµ РѕС‚РєСЂРѕРµС‚ РёС….
  const keepFocus = (e: React.MouseEvent) => e.preventDefault();

  // РћР±С‰РёР№ РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂ Р»РѕРІРёС‚ mousedown, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РєР»РёРє РїРѕ С‚СѓР»Р±Р°СЂСѓ РЅРµ РєРѕРјРјРёС‚РёР» СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ.
  const stopBubble = (e: React.MouseEvent) => e.stopPropagation();

  return (
    <div
      className="text-toolbar"
      style={{ left: x, top: y }}
      onMouseDown={stopBubble}
    >
      <select
        value={fontFamily}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontFamily: e.target.value })}
        className="tt-select"
        title="РЁСЂРёС„С‚"
      >
        {FONTS.map((f) => <option key={f} value={f}>{f}</option>)}
      </select>

      <select
        value={Math.round(fontSize)}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontSize: +e.target.value })}
        className="tt-select tt-size"
        title="Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ"
      >
        {SIZES.map((s) => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
      </select>

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button type="button" onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isBold ? 'active' : ''} onClick={toggleBold} title="Р–РёСЂРЅС‹Р№">
          <b>B</b>
        </button>
        <button type="button" onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isItalic ? 'active' : ''} onClick={toggleItalic} title="РљСѓСЂСЃРёРІ">
          <i>I</i>
        </button>
        <button type="button" onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isUnderline ? 'active' : ''} onClick={toggleUnderline} title="РџРѕРґС‡С‘СЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№">
          <u>U</u>
        </button>
      </div>

      <input
        type="color"
        value={fill}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })}
        className="tt-color"
        title="Р¦РІРµС‚"
      />

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button type="button" onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'left' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'left' })} title="Р’Р»РµРІРѕ">в¬…</button>
        <button type="button" onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'center' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'center' })} title="РџРѕ С†РµРЅС‚СЂСѓ">в†”</button>
        <button type="button" onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'right' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'right' })} title="Р’РїСЂР°РІРѕ">вћЎ</button>
      </div>

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          onClick={onCommit}
          title="Р“РѕС‚РѕРІРѕ (Shift+Enter)"
          className="tt-done"
        >вњ“</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export type StampShape = 'circle' | 'oval' | 'rect' | 'triangle';
export type DashType = 'solid' | 'dashed' | 'dotted' | 'dashdot' | 'longdash';
export type BasicShape = 'rect' | 'roundRect' | 'circle' | 'triangle' | 'star' | 'arrow';
export type TextAlign = 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
export type TextVerticalAlign = 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
export type Orientation = 'portrait' | 'landscape';
export type DocFormat = 'a4' | 'a5' | 'a6' | 'letter' | 'ticket' | 'card' | 'custom';

export interface Padding { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number; }

export type DataFieldType = 'string' | 'number' | 'date' | 'boolean' | 'image' | 'object' | 'array';

export interface DataField {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: DataFieldType;
  sample: string;
  children?: DataField[];
  sampleCount?: number;
}

export interface DataRoot {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  fields: DataField[];
}

export interface BaseElement {
  id: string;
  x: number; y: number;
  width: number; height: number;
  rotation: number;
  locked?: boolean;
}

   РќРѕРІР°СЏ РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ С€С‚Р°РјРїР° вЂ” РІРµРєС‚РѕСЂРЅС‹Р№ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ

export type StampShapeKind = 'circle' | 'ellipse' | 'rect' | 'triangle' | 'line';

export interface StampShapeItem {
  id: string;
  kind: StampShapeKind;

  /** Bounding box РІ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРµ РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚ С€С‚Р°РјРїР°. */
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;

  /** РЎРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ СѓРіР»РѕРІ вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ rect. */
  cornerRadius: number;

  /** Р”Р»СЏ line вЂ” РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ С‚РѕС‡РµРє [x1,y1, x2,y2, ...] РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ (x, y). */
  points?: number[];

  /* РЎС‚РёР»СЊ */
  stroke: string;
  strokeWidth: number;
  dash: DashType;
  fill?: string;

  /* РњРµС‚Р°РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃР»РѕСЏ */
  name?: string;
  locked?: boolean;
  hidden?: boolean;
}

export interface StampTextItem {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  mode: 'straight' | 'arc';

  /* Р”Р»СЏ straight вЂ” bbox РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ С€С‚Р°РјРїР° */
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  align: TextAlign;

  /* Р”Р»СЏ arc */
  arcCx: number;
  arcCy: number;
  arcRadius: number;
  arcStartDeg: number;
  arcEndDeg: number;
  arcFlip: boolean;

  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  fill: string;

  name?: string;
  locked?: boolean;
  hidden?: boolean;
}

  /* Legacy-РїРѕР»СЏ (РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹С… С€С‚Р°РјРїРѕРІ) */

  stampColor?: string;
  strokeWidth?: number;
  strokeDash?: DashType;
  innerStrokeWidth?: number;
  innerStrokeDash?: DashType;

  topFontStyle?: string; topTextDecoration?: string; topColor?: string;
  centerText?: string; centerFontSize?: number; centerFontFamily?: string;
  centerFontStyle?: string; centerTextDecoration?: string; centerColor?: string;
  bottomText?: string; bottomFontSize?: number; bottomFontFamily?: string;
  bottomFontStyle?: string; bottomTextDecoration?: string; bottomColor?: string;
  centerImage?: string;
}

/* ============================================================
   РћСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚С‹ (Р±РµР· РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№)

  | StampElement | TextElement | ImageElement

export const FORMATS: Record<DocFormat, { label: string; portrait: { w: number; h: number } }> = {
  a4:     { label: 'A4 (210Г—297 РјРј)',    portrait: { w: 794,  h: 1123 } },
  a5:     { label: 'A5 (148Г—210 РјРј)',    portrait: { w: 559,  h: 794  } },
  a6:     { label: 'A6 (105Г—148 РјРј)',    portrait: { w: 397,  h: 559  } },
  letter: { label: 'Letter (216Г—279 РјРј)',portrait: { w: 816,  h: 1056 } },
  ticket: { label: 'Р‘РёР»РµС‚ (200Г—80 РјРј)',  portrait: { w: 302,  h: 756  } },
  card:   { label: 'Р’РёР·РёС‚РєР° (90Г—50 РјРј)', portrait: { w: 189,  h: 340  } },
  custom: { label: 'РЎРІРѕР№ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ',        portrait: { w: 794,  h: 1123 } },
};

export const getFormatSize = (
  format: DocFormat,
  orientation: Orientation,
  custom?: { w: number; h: number },
): { w: number; h: number } => {
  let base = FORMATS[format].portrait;
  if (format === 'custom' && custom) base = custom;
  return orientation === 'portrait'
    ? { w: base.w, h: base.h }
    : { w: base.h, h: base.w };
};
